  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 4. Purchase Order Read by ID: GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id', () => {
    it('returns 200 with PurchaseOrder schema including lines and calculated totals', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/00000000-0000-0000-0001-000000000001',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      const parsed = PurchaseOrderSchema.safeParse(json);
      expect(parsed.success).toBe(true);

      expect(json.po_number).toBe('PO-2026-0001');
      expect(json.total).toBe('150.00');
      expect(Array.isArray(json.lines)).toBe(true);
      expect(json.lines.length).toBe(1);
      expect(json.lines[0].quantity).toBe('5.00');
      expect(json.lines[0].price).toBe('30.00');
      expect(json.lines[0].line_total).toBe('150.00');
    });

    it('returns 404 for nonexistent purchase order ID', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    });

    it('returns 400 for malformed purchase order UUID', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/invalid-uuid',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('INVALID_UUID');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 5. Domain Invariants & Mutations
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders (Domain Invariants)', () => {
    it('[AC-6] [DOMAIN INVARIANT] verifies product references via ERP-1 contract; rejects unknown product', async () => {
      const unknownProductId = '99999999-8888-7777-6666-555555555555';
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload: {
          vendor_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
          order_date: '2026-03-15',
          currency: 'USD',
          lines: [
            {
              product_id: unknownProductId,
              quantity: '2.00',
              price: '50.00',
            },
          ],
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('UNKNOWN_PRODUCT');
      expect(json.message).toContain('references unknown product ID');
    });

    it('[AC-9] creates purchase order when products are valid; recomputes total server-side', async () => {
      const validProductId = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload: {
          vendor_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
          order_date: '2026-03-16',
          currency: 'USD',
          lines: [
            {
              product_id: validProductId,
              quantity: '3.00',
              price: '45.50',
            },
          ],
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.status).toBe('Draft');
      // 3.00 * 45.50 = 136.50
      expect(json.total).toBe('136.50');
      expect(json.lines[0].line_total).toBe('136.50');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 6. Purchase Order Status Transitions
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id (Status State Machine)', () => {
    it('[AC-8] allows valid transition Draft -> Sent', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/00000000-0000-0000-0001-000000000001',
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.status).toBe('Sent');
    });

    it('[AC-8] rejects invalid/backward transition (Sent -> Draft)', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/00000000-0000-0000-0001-000000000002', // Currently 'Sent'
        payload: { status: 'Draft' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });

    it('[AC-8] rejects skipping transitions (Draft -> Received directly)', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/00000000-0000-0000-0001-000000000001', // Currently 'Draft'
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 7. Authentication & Authorization (AC-5)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('Authentication & Authorization (AC-5)', () => {
    it('rejects request with 401 when authRequired is true and Authorization header is missing', async () => {
      const res = await authApp.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(401);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.error).toBe('Unauthorized');
      expect(json.message).toContain('Missing Authorization header');
    });

    it('rejects request with 401 when bearer token is invalid', async () => {
      const res = await authApp.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        headers: {
          authorization: 'Bearer invalid-token',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(401);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.error).toBe('Unauthorized');
    });

    it('accepts request with 200 when valid bearer token is provided', async () => {
      const res = await authApp.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        headers: {
          authorization: 'Bearer role:purchasing_admin-valid-token-12345',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(Array.isArray(json.items)).toBe(true);
    });
  });
});
=== apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/api/mutations.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing — Mutation API and Domain Validation Test Suite
 *
 * Exhaustively validates the write surface for Issue #18 (ERP2-03):
 *   - AC-1: Route-boundary Zod validation & authorization (400 / 401)
 *   - AC-2: Domain invariant enforcement at service & API level (422 / 409 / 404)
 *   - AC-3: Invalid business values return 422; duplicate tax numbers / key conflicts return 409
 *   - AC-4: Repeated import request with same idempotency key returns original receipt (200)
 *   - AC-5: Unexpected failures return correlation ID and do not leak SQL, secrets or stack traces
 *   - AC-6: Product reference validation via ERP-1 contract (BR-ERP2-01)
 *   - AC-7: Order total server-side recomputation from lines via Decimal arithmetic (FR-ERP2-05)
 *   - AC-8: Strict unidirectional PO status transition Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed (FR-ERP2-04)
 *   - AC-9: Server-side bank detail masking XXXX-XXXX-7890 (FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)
 *   - AC-10: Protected/system-maintained fields are never accepted from browser
 *
 * @module erp2/tests/api/mutations.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ReferenceResolver } from '@cs/kernel';
import { buildApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { InMemoryErp2Repository } from '../../src/services/repository.js';
import { Erp2Service } from '../../src/services/erp2.service.js';

class MockErp1Resolver implements ReferenceResolver {
  public validProducts = new Set<string>([
    '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
    '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
    '33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333',
  ]);

  async exists(entityType: string, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (entityType === 'product') {
      return this.validProducts.has(id);
    }
    return false;
  }

  async get<T = unknown>(_entityType: string, _id: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return null;
  }
}

describe('ERP-2 Mutation API & Domain Validation (Issue #18 / ERP2-03)', () => {
  let app: FastifyInstance;
  let authApp: FastifyInstance;
  let repo: InMemoryErp2Repository;
  let resolver: MockErp1Resolver;
  let service: Erp2Service;

  const VENDOR_ID = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001';
  const VALID_PRODUCT_1 = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';
  const VALID_PRODUCT_2 = '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222';
  const UNKNOWN_PRODUCT = '99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999';

  const AUTH_HEADER = { authorization: 'Bearer role:editor' };

  beforeEach(() => {
    repo = new InMemoryErp2Repository(true); // seeds default Acme vendor
    resolver = new MockErp1Resolver();
    service = new Erp2Service({ repository: repo, referenceResolver: resolver });

    app = buildApp({
      repository: repo,
      erp2Service: service,
      referenceResolver: resolver,
      authRequired: false,
    });

    authApp = buildApp({
      repository: repo,
      erp2Service: service,
      referenceResolver: resolver,
      authRequired: true,
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 1. POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors (Vendor Creation & Masking)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors', () => {
    it('creates a vendor and masks bank details server-side (FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        name: 'Apex Industrial Parts',
        tax_number: 'TX-APEX-001',
        email: 'orders@apex-parts.com',
        phone: '+1-555-0144',
        address_line: '420 Manufacturing Blvd',
        city: 'Detroit',
        country: 'USA',
        bank_details: '1234567890123456', // Raw sensitive bank account
        currency: 'USD',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(body.name).toBe('Apex Industrial Parts');
      expect(body.tax_number).toBe('TX-APEX-001');
      expect(body.email).toBe('orders@apex-parts.com');
      // Bank details MUST be masked, raw account never returned
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-3456');
      expect(body).not.toHaveProperty('bank_details');
    });

    it('rejects invalid request body with 400 and field details', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload: {
          name: '', // Empty name
          tax_number: 'TX-1',
          email: 'not-an-email', // Invalid email
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
      expect(body.details).toEqual(
        expect.arrayContaining([
          expect.objectContaining({ field: 'name' }),
          expect.objectContaining({ field: 'email' }),
        ])
      );
    });

    it('rejects duplicate tax number with 409 Conflict', async () => {
      const payload = {
        name: 'Duplicate Vendor Co',
        tax_number: 'TX-998877', // Same tax number as seed Acme vendor
        email: 'dup@example.com',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('DUPLICATE_TAX_NUMBER');
      expect(body.message).toContain('TX-998877');
    });

    it('enforces authentication when authRequired is active (AC-1)', async () => {
      const res = await authApp.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload: {
          name: 'Secure Vendor',
          tax_number: 'TX-SEC-01',
          email: 'sec@example.com',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(401);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('UNAUTHORIZED');
    });

    it('allows authenticated requests with valid Bearer token', async () => {
      const res = await authApp.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        headers: AUTH_HEADER,
        payload: {
          name: 'Authenticated Vendor',
          tax_number: 'TX-AUTH-01',
          email: 'auth@example.com',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.name).toBe('Authenticated Vendor');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 2. PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id (Vendor Updates & Field Protection)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id', () => {
    it('updates allowed editable fields and masks updated bank details', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/${VENDOR_ID}`,
        payload: {
          name: 'Acme Industrial Supplies International',
          phone: '+1-555-9999',
          bank_details: '9876543210987654', // Update sensitive bank details
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.id).toBe(VENDOR_ID);
      expect(body.name).toBe('Acme Industrial Supplies International');
      expect(body.phone).toBe('+1-555-9999');
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7654');
    });

    it('returns 404 for nonexistent vendor ID', async () => {
      const nonExistent = 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff';
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/${nonExistent}`,
        payload: { name: 'New Name' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    });

    it('returns 400 for malformed UUID in route parameter', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/invalid-uuid-123',
        payload: { name: 'New Name' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_UUID');
    });

    it('rejects duplicate tax number update with 409 Conflict', async () => {
      // Create second vendor
      const v2 = await repo.createVendor(
        {
          name: 'Second Vendor',
          tax_number: 'TX-V2-UNIQUE',
          email: 'v2@example.com',
        },
        null
      );

      // Attempt to update Acme's tax number to Second Vendor's tax number
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/${VENDOR_ID}`,
        payload: { tax_number: 'TX-V2-UNIQUE' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('DUPLICATE_TAX_NUMBER');
    });

    it('ignores protected/system fields submitted by client (AC-10)', async () => {
      const original = await repo.getVendorById(VENDOR_ID);

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/${VENDOR_ID}`,
        payload: {
          name: 'Acme Protected Test',
          // Client attempts to overwrite protected fields:
          id: '99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999',
          created_at: '2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
          bank_masked: 'XXXX-XXXX-0000',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      // ID and bank_masked remain protected from direct overwrite
      expect(body.id).toBe(VENDOR_ID);
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe(original?.bank_masked);
      expect(body.name).toBe('Acme Protected Test');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 3. POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders (PO Creation, Totals, ERP-1)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders', () => {
    it('creates a PO with lines, default Draft status, and server-recomputed totals (FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-05)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: VENDOR_ID,
        order_date: '2026-10-25',
        currency: 'USD',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: VALID_PRODUCT_1,
            quantity: '10.00',
            price: '15.50', // 155.00
          },
          {
            product_id: VALID_PRODUCT_2,
            quantity: '3.00',
            price: '20.25', // 60.75
          },
        ],
        // Client attempts to provide incorrect total:
        total: '10.00',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(body.vendor_id).toBe(VENDOR_ID);
      expect(body.status).toBe('Draft');
      // Server-side recomputed: 155.00 + 60.75 = 215.75 (client total ignored)
      expect(body.total).toBe('215.75');
      expect(body.lines).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(body.lines[0].line_total).toBe('155.00');
      expect(body.lines[1].line_total).toBe('60.75');
    });

    it('rejects purchase order if product does not exist in ERP-1 (BR-ERP2-01, AC-6)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: VENDOR_ID,
        order_date: '2026-10-25',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: UNKNOWN_PRODUCT, // Nonexistent product ID
            quantity: '5.00',
            price: '10.00',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('UNKNOWN_PRODUCT');
      expect(body.message).toContain(UNKNOWN_PRODUCT);
    });

    it('rejects purchase order if vendor does not exist (AC-3)', async () => {
      const nonExistentVendor = 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff';
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: nonExistentVendor,
        order_date: '2026-10-25',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: VALID_PRODUCT_1,
            quantity: '2.00',
            price: '50.00',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('VENDOR_NOT_FOUND');
    });

    it('rejects empty lines array with 400 Bad Request', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: VENDOR_ID,
        order_date: '2026-10-25',
        lines: [],
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 4. PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id (Status FSM Invariants)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id (Status Transitions)', () => {
    let poId: string;

    beforeEach(async () => {
      const po = await service.createPurchaseOrder({
        vendor_id: VENDOR_ID,
        order_date: '2026-10-20',
        currency: 'USD',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: VALID_PRODUCT_1,
            quantity: '1.00',
            price: '100.00',
          },
        ],
      });
      poId = po.id;
    });

    it('allows sequential forward progression Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed (FR-ERP2-04)', async () => {
      // 1. Draft -> Sent
      const r1 = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });
      expect(r1.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(r1.payload).status).toBe('Sent');

      // 2. Sent -> Received
      const r2 = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });
      expect(r2.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(r2.payload).status).toBe('Received');

      // 3. Received -> Closed
      const r3 = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Closed' },
      });
      expect(r3.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(r3.payload).status).toBe('Closed');
    });

    it('rejects skipping states (Draft -> Received) with 422 Unprocessable Entity', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
      expect(body.message).toContain("Allowed forward flow is Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed");
    });

    it('rejects skipping states (Draft -> Closed) with 422', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Closed' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });

    it('rejects backward transitions (Sent -> Draft) with 422', async () => {
      // Move to Sent first
      await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });

      // Attempt backward to Draft
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Draft' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });

    it('rejects transitions from Closed terminal state with 422', async () => {
      // Move Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed
      await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });
      await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });
      await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Closed' },
      });

      // Attempt transition from Closed to any state
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });

    it('returns 404 for nonexistent purchase order', async () => {
      const nonExistent = 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff';
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${nonExistent}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 5. Idempotent Import Mutations & Dual-Write Receipts (AC-4, KIT-08)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('Migration Import & Idempotency Dual-Write (AC-4, KIT-08)', () => {
    it('creates vendor with durable receipt and returns original on replay without re-mutating', async () => {
      const batchId = randomUUID();
      const idempotencyKey = `batch-${batchId}-row-1`;

      const payload = {
        name: 'Imported Vendor LLC',
        tax_number: 'TX-IMPORT-001',
        email: 'imported@llc.com',
        batch_id: batchId,
        source_row: 1,
        idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
      };

      // 1. Initial request: 201 Created
      const res1 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res1.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const vendor1 = JSON.parse(res1.payload);
      expect(vendor1.name).toBe('Imported Vendor LLC');

      // Verify receipt stored
      const receipt = await repo.getImportReceiptByKey(idempotencyKey);
      expect(receipt).not.toBeNull();
      expect(receipt?.target_id).toBe(vendor1.id);
      expect(receipt?.outcome).toBe('loaded');

      // 2. Replay with exact same idempotency key and payload: 200 OK (prior result)
      const res2 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res2.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const vendor2 = JSON.parse(res2.payload);
      expect(vendor2.id).toBe(vendor1.id);

      // Verify no duplicate vendor was created in repository
      const allVendors = await repo.listVendors({ page: 1, pageSize: 100 });
      const matches = allVendors.vendors.filter((v) => v.tax_number === 'TX-IMPORT-001');
      expect(matches).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('rejects idempotency key reuse with mismatched payload with 409 Conflict', async () => {
      const batchId = randomUUID();
      const idempotencyKey = `batch-${batchId}-row-2`;

      // 1. First request
      await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload: {
          name: 'First Payload Corp',
          tax_number: 'TX-PAYLOAD-1',
          email: 'first@corp.com',
          batch_id: batchId,
          source_row: 2,
          idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
        },
      });

      // 2. Second request reusing key with altered payload
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload: {
          name: 'Completely Different Company',
          tax_number: 'TX-PAYLOAD-DIFFERENT',
          email: 'diff@corp.com',
          batch_id: batchId,
          source_row: 2,
          idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH');
    });

    it('accepts idempotency key provided via Idempotency-Key HTTP header', async () => {
      const batchId = randomUUID();
      const keyHeader = `hdr-key-${randomUUID()}`;

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        headers: {
          'idempotency-key': keyHeader,
        },
        payload: {
          name: 'Header Idempotency Inc',
          tax_number: 'TX-HDR-KEY-1',
          email: 'hdr@example.com',
          batch_id: batchId,
          source_row: 5,
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const receipt = await repo.getImportReceiptByKey(keyHeader);
      expect(receipt).not.toBeNull();
      expect(receipt?.idempotency_key).toBe(keyHeader);
    });
  });

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 6. Security, Correlation IDs & Unexpected Failures (AC-5)
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  describe('Error Envelope & Security Protections (AC-5)', () => {
    it('propagates x-correlation-id in header and payload on all errors', async () => {
      const testCorrelationId = 'test-corr-id-12345';
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/invalid-uuid-syntax',
        headers: {
          'x-correlation-id': testCorrelationId,
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      expect(res.headers['x-correlation-id']).toBe(testCorrelationId);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.correlation_id).toBe(testCorrelationId);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_UUID');
    });

    it('masks internal server errors to 500 without leaking SQL or stack traces', async () => {
      // Force repository failure by throwing unexpected Error
      repo.getVendorById = async () => {
        throw new Error('SELECT * FROM secret_erp_table WHERE password_hash = "secret"');
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/${VENDOR_ID}`,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(500);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR');
      // Must not leak SQL or secret text
      expect(body.message).not.toContain('secret_erp_table');
      expect(body.message).not.toContain('password_hash');
      expect(body).not.toHaveProperty('stack');
      expect(body.correlation_id).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});
=== apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/api/purchase-orders.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-2 Purchase Orders API Test Suite
 *
 * Verifies FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-04, FR-ERP2-05, BR-ERP2-01:
 *   - Purchase order creation with lines
 *   - Server-side calculated order totals (ignoring client total)
 *   - Cross-module product reference verification via ERP-1 ReferenceResolver
 *   - Strict unidirectional status state machine (Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed)
 *   - No inventory side effects on status Received in V1
 *
 * @module erp2/tests/api/purchase-orders.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type { ReferenceResolver } from '@cs/kernel';
import { buildApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { InMemoryErp2Repository } from '../../src/services/repository.js';
import { Erp2Service } from '../../src/services/erp2.service.js';

class MockErp1Resolver implements ReferenceResolver {
  public validProducts = new Set<string>([
    'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa',
    'bbbbbbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb',
  ]);

  async exists(entityType: string, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (entityType === 'product') {
      return this.validProducts.has(id);
    }
    return false;
  }

  async get<T = unknown>(_entityType: string, _id: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return null;
  }
}

describe('ERP-2 Purchase Orders REST API (FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-04, FR-ERP2-05, BR-ERP2-01)', () => {
  let app: FastifyInstance;
  let repo: InMemoryErp2Repository;
  let resolver: MockErp1Resolver;
  let service: Erp2Service;

  const vendorId = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001';
  const prodA = 'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa';
  const prodB = 'bbbbbbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb';

  beforeEach(() => {
    repo = new InMemoryErp2Repository(true); // seeds default Acme vendor
    resolver = new MockErp1Resolver();
    service = new Erp2Service({ repository: repo, referenceResolver: resolver });
    app = buildApp({ repository: repo, erp2Service: service, referenceResolver: resolver });
  });

  describe('POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders', () => {
    it('creates purchase order and recomputes total server-side (FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-05)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: vendorId,
        order_date: '2026-10-20',
        currency: 'USD',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: prodA,
            quantity: '10.00',
            price: '15.50', // 155.00
          },
          {
            product_id: prodB,
            quantity: '3.00',
            price: '20.25', // 60.75
          },
        ],
        // Client attempting to provide custom/incorrect total:
        total: '9999.99',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(body.vendor_id).toBe(vendorId);
      expect(body.status).toBe('Draft'); // Default status
      // Expected: 155.00 + 60.75 = 215.75 (client-provided total ignored)
      expect(body.total).toBe('215.75');
      expect(body.lines).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(body.lines[0].line_total).toBe('155.00');
      expect(body.lines[1].line_total).toBe('60.75');
    });

    it('rejects purchase order if product does not exist in ERP-1 (BR-ERP2-01)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: vendorId,
        order_date: '2026-10-20',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: '99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999', // Unknown product
            quantity: '1.00',
            price: '50.00',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('UNKNOWN_PRODUCT');
    });

    it('rejects purchase order with non-existent vendor (HTTP 422)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        vendor_id: '99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999',
        order_date: '2026-10-20',
        lines: [
          {
            product_id: prodA,
            quantity: '1.00',
            price: '50.00',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('VENDOR_NOT_FOUND');
    });
  });

  describe('PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id (Status FSM)', () => {
    it('executes valid forward transition flow: Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed (FR-ERP2-04)', async () => {
      // 1. Create draft PO
      const createRes = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload: {
          vendor_id: vendorId,
          order_date: '2026-10-20',
          lines: [{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '1.00', price: '10.00' }],
        },
      });
      const poId = JSON.parse(createRes.payload).id;

      // 2. Draft -> Sent
      const resSent = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });
      expect(resSent.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(resSent.payload).status).toBe('Sent');

      // 3. Sent -> Received (does not mutate inventory in V1)
      const resReceived = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });
      expect(resReceived.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(resReceived.payload).status).toBe('Received');

      // 4. Received -> Closed
      const resClosed = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Closed' },
      });
      expect(resClosed.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(JSON.parse(resClosed.payload).status).toBe('Closed');
    });

    it('rejects backwards transition (e.g. Sent -> Draft) with HTTP 422', async () => {
      // Create PO and move to Sent
      const createRes = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload: {
          vendor_id: vendorId,
          order_date: '2026-10-20',
          lines: [{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '1.00', price: '10.00' }],
        },
      });
      const poId = JSON.parse(createRes.payload).id;

      await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Sent' },
      });

      // Try backwards transition: Sent -> Draft
      const resBackwards = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Draft' },
      });

      expect(resBackwards.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(resBackwards.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });

    it('rejects skipped transition (e.g. Draft -> Received) with HTTP 422', async () => {
      const createRes = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders',
        payload: {
          vendor_id: vendorId,
          order_date: '2026-10-20',
          lines: [{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '1.00', price: '10.00' }],
        },
      });
      const poId = JSON.parse(createRes.payload).id;

      // Try skipping: Draft -> Received
      const resSkip = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/${poId}`,
        payload: { status: 'Received' },
      });

      expect(resSkip.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(resSkip.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION');
    });
  });

  describe('GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders', () => {
    it('returns paged list with status and vendor filters', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders?vendor_id=${vendorId}`,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.items).toBeDefined();
      expect(body.total).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});

/**
 * ERP-2 Vendors API Test Suite
 *
 * Verifies FR-ERP2-01, FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06:
 *   - Vendor listing with bounded pagination & search by name
 *   - Vendor CRUD operations
 *   - Server-side bank detail masking (XXXX-XXXX-7890) and negative leakage tests
 *   - Duplicate tax number constraint
 *
 * @module erp2/tests/api/vendors.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { buildApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { InMemoryErp2Repository } from '../../src/services/repository.js';
import { Erp2Service } from '../../src/services/erp2.service.js';

describe('ERP-2 Vendors REST API (FR-ERP2-01, FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)', () => {
  let app: FastifyInstance;
  let repo: InMemoryErp2Repository;
  let service: Erp2Service;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repo = new InMemoryErp2Repository(true); // seeds default Acme vendor
    service = new Erp2Service({ repository: repo });
    app = buildApp({ repository: repo, erp2Service: service });
  });

  describe('GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors', () => {
    it('returns paginated vendor list with default pagination', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.items).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(body.total).toBe(1);
      expect(body.page).toBe(1);
      expect(body.pageSize).toBe(20);
      expect(body.items[0].name).toBe('Acme Industrial Supplies');
      expect(body.items[0].bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7890');
    });

    it('filters vendors by name search', async () => {
      // Seed another vendor
      await repo.createVendor(
        {
          name: 'Beta Electronics',
          tax_number: 'TX-BETA-01',
          email: 'contact@beta.com',
        },
        'XXXX-XXXX-1122'
      );

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors?search=Beta',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.items).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(body.items[0].name).toBe('Beta Electronics');
    });

    it('rejects invalid page or pageSize > 100', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors?pageSize=200',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
    });
  });

  describe('POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors', () => {
    it('creates a new vendor and masks bank details server-side (FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        name: 'Delta Logistics',
        tax_number: 'TX-DELTA-99',
        email: 'ops@deltalogistics.com',
        phone: '+1-555-8888',
        address_line: '500 Freight Way',
        city: 'Chicago',
        country: 'USA',
        bank_details: '98765432101234', // Raw sensitive bank account number
        currency: 'USD',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(body.name).toBe('Delta Logistics');
      expect(body.tax_number).toBe('TX-DELTA-99');
      // Verify mask format XXXX-XXXX-1234 (last 4 digits of 98765432101234 are 1234)
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-1234');
      // Assert raw bank details never leak
      expect(JSON.stringify(body)).not.toContain('98765432101234');
    });

    it('rejects creating vendor with duplicate tax number (HTTP 409)', async () => {
      const payload = {
        name: 'Another Acme Clone',
        tax_number: 'TX-998877', // Same as seeded Acme
        email: 'clone@acme.com',
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('DUPLICATE_TAX_NUMBER');
    });
  });

  describe('GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id', () => {
    it('retrieves single vendor by UUID', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.name).toBe('Acme Industrial Supplies');
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7890');
    });

    it('returns 404 when vendor does not exist', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    });
  });

  describe('PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id', () => {
    it('updates vendor and masks new bank details', async () => {
      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
        payload: {
          phone: '+1-555-9999',
          bank_details: '5555666677778888',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.phone).toBe('+1-555-9999');
      expect(body.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-8888');
      expect(JSON.stringify(body)).not.toContain('5555666677778888');
    });
  });
});
=== apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/domain/rules.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing — Domain Rules Unit Tests
 *
 * Exhaustively tests pure domain invariants without database or network I/O:
 *   - FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06: Bank details masking and format assertion
 *   - FR-ERP2-04: Strict unidirectional status machine (Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed)
 *   - FR-ERP2-05: Server-side calculated order totals and decimal rounding
 *   - BR-ERP2-01: Line quantity and price constraints
 *
 * @module erp2/tests/domain/rules.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  Erp2DomainError,
  maskBankDetails,
  assertValidBankDetails,
  assertValidPoStatusTransition,
  calculatePoLinesAndTotal,
  ALLOWED_PO_STATUS_TRANSITIONS,
} from '../../src/domain/rules.js';

describe('ERP-2 Domain Rules — Pure Invariants', () => {
  describe('Bank Details Masking (FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)', () => {
    it('masks standard 12 to 16 digit account numbers with XXXX-XXXX-7890 format', () => {
      expect(maskBankDetails('1234567890123456')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-3456');
      expect(maskBankDetails('98765432101234')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-1234');
      expect(maskBankDetails('40001234567890')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7890');
    });

    it('ignores non-digit characters and correctly masks last 4 digits', () => {
      expect(maskBankDetails('1234-5678-9012-7890')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7890');
      expect(maskBankDetails('GB29 XBAR 1234 5678 9999')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-9999');
    });

    it('pads short account strings with leading zeros', () => {
      expect(maskBankDetails('99')).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-0099');
    });

    it('returns null for empty, null or undefined input', () => {
      expect(maskBankDetails(null)).toBeNull();
      expect(maskBankDetails(undefined)).toBeNull();
      expect(maskBankDetails('')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('assertValidBankDetails accepts valid strings and nullish values', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidBankDetails(null)).not.toThrow();
      expect(() => assertValidBankDetails(undefined)).not.toThrow();
      expect(() => assertValidBankDetails('123456789')).not.toThrow();
    });

    it('assertValidBankDetails throws for strings shorter than 4 characters', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidBankDetails('123')).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
      expect(() => assertValidBankDetails('   ')).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
    });
  });

  describe('Purchase Order Status Transitions (FR-ERP2-04)', () => {
    it('allows valid forward sequential transitions', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Draft', 'Sent')).not.toThrow();
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Sent', 'Received')).not.toThrow();
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Received', 'Closed')).not.toThrow();
    });

    it('rejects skipping states in forward flow', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Draft', 'Received')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Draft', 'Closed')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Sent', 'Closed')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
    });

    it('rejects backward transitions', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Sent', 'Draft')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Received', 'Sent')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Received', 'Draft')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Closed', 'Received')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Closed', 'Draft')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
    });

    it('rejects self-transitions', () => {
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Draft', 'Draft')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Sent', 'Sent')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Received', 'Received')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
      expect(() => assertValidPoStatusTransition('Closed', 'Closed')).toThrowError(
        Erp2DomainError
      );
    });

    it('has empty allowed transitions for Closed terminal state', () => {
      expect(ALLOWED_PO_STATUS_TRANSITIONS['Closed']).toEqual([]);
    });
  });

  describe('Order Total Recomputation & Decimal Arithmetic (FR-ERP2-05, BR-ERP2-01)', () => {
    const prodA = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000aa';
    const prodB = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000bb';

    it('accurately computes line totals and overall total with 2 decimal places', () => {
      const { lines, total } = calculatePoLinesAndTotal([
        { product_id: prodA, quantity: '10.00', price: '15.50' }, // 155.00
        { product_id: prodB, quantity: '3.00', price: '20.25' },  // 60.75
      ]);

      expect(total).toBe('215.75');
      expect(lines).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(lines[0].line_total).toBe('155.00');
      expect(lines[1].line_total).toBe('60.75');
    });

    it('applies standard half-up currency rounding for fractional cent values', () => {
      const { lines, total } = calculatePoLinesAndTotal([
        { product_id: prodA, quantity: '3', price: '1.333' }, // 3 * 1.333 = 3.999 -> 4.00
      ]);

      expect(lines[0].line_total).toBe('4.00');
      expect(total).toBe('4.00');
    });

    it('rejects empty order lines', () => {
      expect(() => calculatePoLinesAndTotal([])).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
    });

    it('rejects zero or negative quantities', () => {
      expect(() =>
        calculatePoLinesAndTotal([{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '0.00', price: '10.00' }])
      ).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);

      expect(() =>
        calculatePoLinesAndTotal([{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '-5.00', price: '10.00' }])
      ).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
    });

    it('rejects negative prices', () => {
      expect(() =>
        calculatePoLinesAndTotal([{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '1.00', price: '-1.00' }])
      ).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
    });

    it('allows zero price (e.g. promotional or warranty items)', () => {
      const { lines, total } = calculatePoLinesAndTotal([
        { product_id: prodA, quantity: '1.00', price: '0.00' },
      ]);
      expect(lines[0].line_total).toBe('0.00');
      expect(total).toBe('0.00');
    });

    it('rejects non-numeric quantity or price strings', () => {
      expect(() =>
        calculatePoLinesAndTotal([{ product_id: prodA, quantity: 'invalid', price: '10.00' }])
      ).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);

      expect(() =>
        calculatePoLinesAndTotal([{ product_id: prodA, quantity: '10.00', price: 'invalid' }])
      ).toThrowError(Erp2DomainError);
    });
  });
});
=== apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/migration/idempotency.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-2 Idempotency & Migration Target Adapter Test Suite
 *
 * Verifies Acceptance Criteria for Issue #22 (KIT-08 / Decision C4):
 *   1. Fixture row loaded via the same public API used by the Kit; Kit has no direct target-table write privilege.
 *   2. Retrying identical row payload/key 3 times produces exactly 1 business record and 1 durable receipt.
 *   3. Reusing a key with a different payload returns HTTP 409 and does not mutate data.
 *   4. Business-rule rejection returns a reason code and safe message; simulated 503 is retryable and not recorded as rejected.
 *   5. Receipt and target mutation commit or roll back together atomically.
 *   6. Every purchase-order line references a valid existing product through the owning ERP-1 contract.
 *
 * @module erp2/tests/migration/idempotency.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type { ReferenceResolver } from '@cs/kernel';
import { buildApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { InMemoryErp2Repository } from '../../src/services/repository.js';
import { Erp2Service } from '../../src/services/erp2.service.js';
import { Erp2TargetAdapter } from '../../src/migration/target-adapter.js';

class MockErp1Resolver implements ReferenceResolver {
  public validProducts = new Set<string>([
    '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
    '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
  ]);

  async exists(entityType: string, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (entityType === 'product') {
      return this.validProducts.has(id);
    }
    return false;
  }

  async get<T = unknown>(_entityType: string, _id: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return null;
  }
}

describe('ERP-2 Migration Kit Adapter & Durable Import Receipts (Issue #22)', () => {
  let app: FastifyInstance;
  let repo: InMemoryErp2Repository;
  let resolver: MockErp1Resolver;
  let service: Erp2Service;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repo = new InMemoryErp2Repository(true); // seeds default Acme vendor
    resolver = new MockErp1Resolver();
    service = new Erp2Service({ repository: repo, referenceResolver: resolver });
    app = buildApp({ repository: repo, erp2Service: service, referenceResolver: resolver });
  });

  describe('1. In-Process TargetAdapter (KIT-08 Contract)', () => {
    it('executes preflight dry-run validation without mutating the database', async () => {
      const adapter = new Erp2TargetAdapter(service, 'vendor');

      const rows = [
        { name: 'Vendor 1', tax_number: 'TAX-1', email: 'v1@test.com' },
        { name: 'Invalid Vendor', tax_number: '', email: 'no-email' },
      ];

      const res = await adapter.preflight('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000010', rows);
      expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
      if (res.ok) {
        expect(res.value.validCount).toBe(1);
        expect(res.value.rejectCount).toBe(1);
      }

      // Assert no database mutation occurred
      const { total } = await repo.listVendors({ page: 1, pageSize: 20 });
      expect(total).toBe(1); // Only seeded Acme vendor
    });

    it('commits target business record and durable receipt atomically', async () => {
      const adapter = new Erp2TargetAdapter(service, 'vendor');
      const batchId = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000010';
      const key = 'idem-vendor-001';

      const res = await adapter.importRow(batchId, 0, key, {
        name: 'Steel Works Ltd',
        tax_number: 'TX-STEEL-01',
        email: 'info@steelworks.com',
        bank_details: '1234567890',
      });

      expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
      if (res.ok) {
        expect(res.value.targetId).toBeDefined();
        expect(res.value.receipt.idempotencyKey).toBe(key);
        expect(res.value.receipt.entity).toBe('vendor');
        expect(res.value.receipt.batchId).toBe(batchId);
      }

      // Assert business row exists and has masked bank details
      const created = await repo.getVendorByTaxNumber('TX-STEEL-01');
      expect(created).not.toBeNull();
      expect(created?.bank_masked).toBe('XXXX-XXXX-7890');

      // Assert receipt row exists
      const receipt = await repo.getImportReceiptByKey(key);
      expect(receipt).not.toBeNull();
      expect(receipt?.target_id).toBe(created?.id);
    });
  });

  describe('2. Identical Retries (3x)', () => {
    it('retrying identical row payload and key 3 times produces exactly 1 business record and 1 durable receipt', async () => {
      const payload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000020',
        source_row: 1,
        source_system: 'legacy_erp',
        source_id: 'LEG-991',
        idempotency_key: 'key-vendor-retry-test',
        entity: 'vendor',
        record: {
          name: 'Global Fasteners Inc',
          tax_number: 'TAX-GLOBAL-991',
          email: 'orders@globalfasteners.com',
          bank_details: '9876543210',
        },
      };

      // Call 1 (Fresh)
      const res1 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        headers: { 'idempotency-key': payload.idempotency_key },
        payload,
      });
      expect(res1.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const data1 = JSON.parse(res1.payload);
      expect(data1.prior_result).toBe(false);
      const targetId = data1.target_id;

      // Call 2 (Replay 1)
      const res2 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        headers: { 'idempotency-key': payload.idempotency_key },
        payload,
      });
      expect(res2.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const data2 = JSON.parse(res2.payload);
      expect(data2.prior_result).toBe(true);
      expect(data2.target_id).toBe(targetId);

      // Call 3 (Replay 2)
      const res3 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        headers: { 'idempotency-key': payload.idempotency_key },
        payload,
      });
      expect(res3.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const data3 = JSON.parse(res3.payload);
      expect(data3.prior_result).toBe(true);
      expect(data3.target_id).toBe(targetId);

      // Verify exactly 1 business record with this tax number exists
      const vendor = await repo.getVendorByTaxNumber('TAX-GLOBAL-991');
      expect(vendor).not.toBeNull();
      expect(vendor?.id).toBe(targetId);

      // Verify exactly 1 durable receipt exists
      const receipt = await repo.getImportReceiptByKey(payload.idempotency_key);
      expect(receipt).not.toBeNull();
      expect(receipt?.target_id).toBe(targetId);
    });
  });

  describe('3. Reused Key with Different Payload (HTTP 409 Conflict)', () => {
    it('reusing an idempotency key with a different payload returns 409 and does not mutate data', async () => {
      const idempotencyKey = 'key-vendor-conflict-test';

      const initialPayload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000030',
        source_row: 1,
        idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
        entity: 'vendor',
        record: {
          name: 'First Vendor Name',
          tax_number: 'TAX-CONFLICT-1',
          email: 'first@test.com',
        },
      };

      const res1 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        payload: initialPayload,
      });
      expect(res1.statusCode).toBe(201);

      // Attempt to reuse same key with different payload
      const conflictingPayload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000030',
        source_row: 2,
        idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
        entity: 'vendor',
        record: {
          name: 'Second Conflicting Vendor',
          tax_number: 'TAX-CONFLICT-2',
          email: 'second@test.com',
        },
      };

      const res2 = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        payload: conflictingPayload,
      });

      expect(res2.statusCode).toBe(409);
      const body2 = JSON.parse(res2.payload);
      expect(body2.code).toBe('IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH');

      // Verify second vendor was NOT mutated or inserted
      const secondVendor = await repo.getVendorByTaxNumber('TAX-CONFLICT-2');
      expect(secondVendor).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('4. Business Validation & Transient Infrastructure Failures', () => {
    it('rejects purchase order with unknown product with stable reason code and creates no receipt', async () => {
      const unknownProductId = '99999999-9999-9999-9999-999999999999';
      const key = 'key-unknown-prod-test';

      const payload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000040',
        source_row: 5,
        idempotency_key: key,
        entity: 'purchase_order',
        record: {
          vendor_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
          order_date: '2026-10-20',
          lines: [
            {
              product_id: unknownProductId,
              quantity: '5.00',
              price: '100.00',
            },
          ],
        },
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('UNKNOWN_PRODUCT');

      // Verify no receipt was saved
      const receipt = await repo.getImportReceiptByKey(key);
      expect(receipt).toBeNull();
    });

    it('simulated 503 infrastructure failure rolls back transaction and is not recorded as rejected', async () => {
      // Mock executeTransactional to simulate a database crash / network drop
      const failingRepo = new InMemoryErp2Repository(true);
      failingRepo.executeTransactional = async () => {
        const error = new Error('Database connection timeout');
        (error as any).statusCode = 503;
        throw error;
      };

      const failingService = new Erp2Service({ repository: failingRepo, referenceResolver: resolver });
      const failingApp = buildApp({ repository: failingRepo, erp2Service: failingService });

      const key = 'key-infra-failure-test';
      const payload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000050',
        source_row: 1,
        idempotency_key: key,
        entity: 'vendor',
        record: {
          name: 'Crash Vendor',
          tax_number: 'TX-CRASH-01',
          email: 'crash@test.com',
        },
      };

      const res = await failingApp.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(503);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.code).toBe('SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE');

      // Assert no vendor was committed
      const vendor = await failingRepo.getVendorByTaxNumber('TX-CRASH-01');
      expect(vendor).toBeNull();

      // Assert no receipt was saved
      const receipt = await failingRepo.getImportReceiptByKey(key);
      expect(receipt).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('5. Domain Invariant: ERP-1 Product Verification & Calculated Totals', () => {
    it('successfully creates purchase order with valid ERP-1 products and calculated total', async () => {
      const validProductId = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';
      const key = 'key-po-success-test';

      const payload = {
        batch_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000060',
        source_row: 1,
        idempotency_key: key,
        entity: 'purchase_order',
        record: {
          vendor_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
          order_date: '2026-10-20',
          lines: [
            {
              product_id: validProductId,
              quantity: '3.00',
              price: '45.50',
            },
            {
              product_id: validProductId,
              quantity: '2.00',
              price: '10.25',
            },
          ],
        },
      };

      const res = await app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-vendors/import',
        payload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const body = JSON.parse(res.payload);
      expect(body.outcome).toBe('loaded');

      const po = await repo.getPurchaseOrderById(body.target_id);
      expect(po).not.toBeNull();
      // Total calculation: 3 * 45.50 = 136.50; 2 * 10.25 = 20.50; Total = 157.00
      expect(po?.total).toBe('157.00');
      expect(po?.status).toBe('Draft');
      expect(po?.lines).toHaveLength(2);
    });
  });
});

=== docs/data-dictionaries/erp-2.md ===
# ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing — Data Dictionary

This document is the global reference copy of the data dictionary for ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing.

---

## Module Summary

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Module** | ERP-2 Vendors and Purchasing |
| **Issues** | #17: ERP2-02 (List/Read API with Bounded Paging & OpenAPI), #18: ERP2-03 (Mutation API and Domain Validation), #22: ERP2-07 (Migration Kit Adapter) |
| **PostgreSQL Schema** | `erp2` |
| **API Base** | `/api/v1/erp-vendors` |
| **OpenAPI Contract** | `packages/contracts/openapi/erp-2.yaml` |
| **TypeScript Contracts** | `packages/contracts/src/erp-2.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/import-target.ts` |
| **Tables** | `erp2.vendors`, `erp2.purchase_orders`, `erp2.po_lines`, `erp2.import_receipts` |
| **Version** | V1.0 |
| **SDS Reference** | ClearShift ERP v2.0 §4.3, §4.7, §4.8, §7.7, FR-ERP2-01..06, BR-ERP2-01 |

---

## Table Index

| Table | Purpose | Key Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| `erp2.vendors` | Supplier / vendor master catalog with masked bank details | UNIQUE(`tax_number`), INDEX(`name`) |
| `erp2.purchase_orders` | Purchase order headers with status lifecycle & total | UNIQUE(`po_number`), FK(`vendor_id` $\rightarrow$ `vendors.id`) |
| `erp2.po_lines` | Line items linked to purchase order and ERP-1 product | FK(`po_id` $\rightarrow$ `purchase_orders.id`), INDEX(`product_id`) |
| `erp2.import_receipts` | Durable idempotency receipts for migration & integration | UNIQUE(`idempotency_key`), INDEX(`batch_id`, `source_row`) |

---

## 1. `erp2.vendors` — Vendor Master Catalog

**Purpose**: Stores supplier and vendor company profiles.  
**Functional Requirements**: FR-ERP2-01, FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06.

| Column | PostgreSQL Type | Nullable | Default | Meaning | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | Primary key | PK |
| `name` | `VARCHAR(255)` | NOT NULL | — | Legal business name of vendor | INDEX(`name`) |
| `tax_number` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NOT NULL | — | Unique taxpayer identification number | UNIQUE |
| `email` | `VARCHAR(255)` | NOT NULL | — | Primary procurement contact email | — |
| `phone` | `VARCHAR(32)` | NULL | — | Vendor contact phone number | — |
| `address_line` | `VARCHAR(255)` | NULL | — | Street address | — |
| `city` | `VARCHAR(100)` | NULL | — | City | — |
| `country` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NULL | — | Country of incorporation / residence | — |
| `bank_masked` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NULL | — | Masked banking details (`XXXX-XXXX-7890`) | Serialized masked only |
| `currency` | `VARCHAR(3)` | NOT NULL | `'USD'` | ISO 4217 currency code | 3 uppercase letters |
| `source_system` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NULL | — | Migration Kit provenance: source system | — |
| `source_id` | `VARCHAR(128)` | NULL | — | Migration Kit provenance: source entity ID | — |
| `created_at` | `TIMESTAMPTZ` | NOT NULL | `now()` | Row creation timestamp | — |
| `updated_at` | `TIMESTAMPTZ` | NOT NULL | `now()` | Last modification timestamp | — |

---

## 2. `erp2.purchase_orders` — Purchase Order Headers

**Purpose**: Stores purchasing headers, status progression, and computed order total.  
**Functional Requirements**: FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-04, FR-ERP2-05.

| Column | PostgreSQL Type | Nullable | Default | Meaning | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | Primary key | PK |
| `po_number` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NOT NULL | — | Canonical human-readable PO number | UNIQUE |
| `vendor_id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | — | Foreign key to `erp2.vendors.id` | FK $\rightarrow$ `vendors(id)` |
| `order_date` | `DATE` | NOT NULL | — | Transaction date of order | — |
| `status` | `erp2.po_status` | NOT NULL | `'Draft'` | Status: `Draft`, `Sent`, `Received`, `Closed` | Strict unidirectional FSM |
| `currency` | `VARCHAR(3)` | NOT NULL | `'USD'` | ISO 4217 currency code | — |
| `total` | `NUMERIC(18,2)` | NOT NULL | `0.00` | Server-recomputed order total | Non-negative decimal |
| `created_at` | `TIMESTAMPTZ` | NOT NULL | `now()` | Row creation timestamp | — |
| `updated_at` | `TIMESTAMPTZ` | NOT NULL | `now()` | Last modification timestamp | — |

---

## 3. `erp2.po_lines` — Purchase Order Lines

**Purpose**: Line items referencing catalog products from ERP-1.  
**Functional Requirements**: FR-ERP2-03, FR-ERP2-05, BR-ERP2-01.

| Column | PostgreSQL Type | Nullable | Default | Meaning | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | Primary key | PK |
| `po_id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | — | Owning purchase order | FK $\rightarrow$ `purchase_orders(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE |
| `product_id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | — | Product identifier owned by ERP-1 | Logical ref; verified via `ReferenceResolver` |
| `quantity` | `NUMERIC(12,2)` | NOT NULL | — | Purchased quantity | $> 0` |
| `price` | `NUMERIC(18,2)` | NOT NULL | — | Unit purchase price | $\ge 0` |
| `line_total` | `NUMERIC(18,2)` | NOT NULL | — | Server computed: `round(qty * price, 2)` | Decimal half-up rounding |

---

## 4. `erp2.import_receipts` — Durable Import Receipts

**Purpose**: Tracks mutation provenance, idempotency keys, and canonical payload hashes for KIT-08 / Decision C4.  
**Functional Requirements**: KIT-08, Decision C4.

| Column | PostgreSQL Type | Nullable | Default | Meaning | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | Primary key | PK |
| `batch_id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | — | Migration batch identifier | — |
| `source_row` | `INTEGER` | NOT NULL | — | 0-indexed row position in migration batch | — |
| `source_system`| `VARCHAR(64)` | NULL | — | Source system name (e.g. legacy ERP) | — |
| `source_id` | `VARCHAR(128)` | NULL | — | Primary key in source system | — |
| `entity` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NOT NULL | — | Imported entity type (`vendor`, `purchase_order`) | — |
| `schema_version`| `INTEGER` | NOT NULL | `1` | Migration schema version | — |
| `idempotency_key`| `VARCHAR(128)` | NOT NULL | — | Stable idempotency key from caller | UNIQUE |
| `request_hash` | `VARCHAR(64)` | NOT NULL | — | SHA-256 hash of canonicalized JSON request | Mismatch detection (HTTP 409) |
| `target_id` | `UUID` | NOT NULL | — | Created target record primary key | — |
| `kind` | `VARCHAR(16)` | NOT NULL | `'created'` | Operation kind: `created`, `linked` | — |
| `outcome` | `VARCHAR(16)` | NOT NULL | `'loaded'` | Outcome: `loaded`, `rejected`, `merged`, `skipped` | — |
| `response_payload`| `TEXT` | NULL | — | Serialized target response payload | — |
| `created_at` | `TIMESTAMPTZ` | NOT NULL | `now()` | Timestamp receipt was committed | — |

---

## 5. API Endpoints

| Method | Endpoint | Description | Constraints / Behaviors |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors` | List vendors | Paged (`page`, `pageSize <= 100`), search by name, returns `bank_masked` |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors` | Create vendor | Validates tax number uniqueness, masks bank details, optional idempotency key |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id` | Get vendor by UUID | Returns single vendor with masked bank details |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/:id` | Update vendor | Updates mutable fields; bank details masked server-side |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders` | List purchase orders | Bounded pagination, filter by `vendor_id` and `status` |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id` | Get purchase order | Returns purchase order with line items |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders` | Create purchase order | Validates vendor and products via ERP-1, computes order total server-side |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/:id` | Update PO status | Allowed forward transitions only: `Draft` $\rightarrow$ `Sent` $\rightarrow$ `Received` $\rightarrow$ `Closed` |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/erp-vendors/import` | Import row | KIT-08 adapter endpoint; atomic dual-write of target entity + receipt |

---

## 6. Core Domain Invariants & Business Rules

1. **Bank Details Privacy (FR-ERP2-06)**: Raw bank account numbers are never exposed in any list or detail query response, error message, or log. Serialized representation strictly follows the mask format `XXXX-XXXX-7890`.
2. **Server-Side Calculated Total (FR-ERP2-05)**: Line total is calculated as `round(quantity * price, 2)` using decimal half-up rounding. The order total is calculated as the sum of line totals. Any client-submitted total is ignored and overwritten.
3. **Cross-Module Referential Integrity (BR-ERP2-01 / Decision C7)**: Each purchase order line product reference is verified via the `ReferenceResolver` port provided by the kernel. If a product does not exist in ERP-1, the request is rejected with `UNKNOWN_PRODUCT` (HTTP 422).
4. **Strict State Machine (FR-ERP2-04)**: Status transitions must follow `Draft` $\rightarrow$ `Sent` $\rightarrow$ `Received` $\rightarrow$ `Closed`. Backwards transitions or cancellations are rejected with `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION` (HTTP 422). Marking status as `Received` does not alter inventory in V1.
5. **Idempotency & Atomic Dual-Write (KIT-08 / Decision C4)**:
   - Target row mutation and receipt insertion execute inside a single local PostgreSQL transaction.
   - Identical 3x replays produce 1 business row and 1 receipt (HTTP 200/201).
   - Key reuse with mismatched payload returns HTTP 409 Conflict with zero database mutation.
   - Unexpected infrastructure failure (HTTP 503) rolls back the transaction and is not recorded as a rejected business record.

---

## 7. Synthetic Demo & Runbook

### 7.1 Local Verification & Test Execution

Run the complete ERP-2 API and domain test suite:

```bash
# Run unit & API test suite for list/read and domain invariants
npx vitest run apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/api/list.test.ts

# Run mutation and domain rules test suites
npx vitest run apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/api/mutations.test.ts apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/domain/rules.test.ts

# Run complete ERP-2 test suite (vendors, purchase-orders, idempotency)
npx vitest run apps/erp/modules/erp-2/tests/
```

### 7.2 Synthetic Endpoint Demo Flow

#### 1. List Vendors with Bounded Paging & Search
```bash
# Bounded pagination (page 1, pageSize 20) with search by legal business name
curl -X GET "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors?page=1&pageSize=20&search=Acme" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin"
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 200 with `items: [...]`, `total: 1`, `page: 1`, `pageSize: 20`, `totalPages: 1`, with `bank_masked: "XXXX-XXXX-7890"`.

#### 2. Read Single Vendor by ID
```bash
curl -X GET "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin"
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 200 with full vendor profile, bank details masked to `XXXX-XXXX-7890`.

#### 3. Create a Vendor with Server-Side Bank Masking
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin" \
  -d '{
    "name": "Apex Industrial Supplies",
    "tax_number": "TX-APEX-001",
    "email": "procurement@apex-supplies.com",
    "phone": "+1-555-0188",
    "address_line": "500 Logistics Parkway",
    "city": "Chicago",
    "country": "USA",
    "bank_details": "98765432101234",
    "currency": "USD"
  }'
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 201 Created with `bank_masked: "XXXX-XXXX-1234"`.

#### 4. Create Purchase Order with ERP-1 Product Verification & Server-Side Totals
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin" \
  -d '{
    "vendor_id": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
    "order_date": "2026-10-25",
    "currency": "USD",
    "lines": [
      {
        "product_id": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
        "quantity": "10.00",
        "price": "15.50"
      },
      {
        "product_id": "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
        "quantity": "3.00",
        "price": "20.25"
      }
    ],
    "total": "9999.99"
  }'
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 201 Created (Note: client-submitted `total: 9999.99` is ignored; server recalculates `155.00 + 60.75 = 215.75`).

#### 5. Filter Purchase Orders by Status
```bash
curl -X GET "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders?status=Draft&page=1&pageSize=10" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin"
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 200 with list of Draft POs.

#### 6. Transition Purchase Order Status
```bash
curl -X PATCH "http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/00000000-0000-0000-0001-000000000001" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer role:purchasing_admin" \
  -d '{ "status": "Sent" }'
```
*Expected Output*: HTTP 200 with `status: "Sent"`. Attempting to transition back to `Draft` returns HTTP 422 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION`.

info:
  title: ClearShift Shared API Contract & Error Envelope
  version: 1.0.0
  description: Canonical OpenAPI components for shared errors, bounded pagination, event envelopes, and health checks (PLT-04).

paths:
  /health:
    get:
      summary: Health check endpoint
      description: Returns service status, module identity, and dependency readiness.
      operationId: getCommonHealth
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Service is healthy
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/HealthResponse'
        '500':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/InternalServerError'

components:
  schemas:
    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      required:
        - message
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
          description: Specific payload field path causing the error
          example: pageSize
        message:
          type: string
          description: Human-readable error detail
          example: pageSize cannot exceed 100
        code:
          type: string
          description: Machine-readable detail code
          example: VALUE_OUT_OF_RANGE

    ErrorEnvelope:
      type: object
      required:
        - code
        - message
      properties:
        code:
          type: string
          description: Stable machine-readable error code
          example: BAD_REQUEST
        message:
          type: string
          description: Safe human-readable summary of the failure
          example: Invalid query parameter values
        details:
          description: Structured field-level validation errors or context dictionary
          oneOf:
            - type: array
              items:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'
            - type: object
              additionalProperties: true
        correlation_id:
          type: string
          description: Unique request/event correlation identifier
          example: 123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000

    BasePaginationQuery:

          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          default: 1
          description: 1-based page number
