          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 100
          default: 20
          description: Number of items per page (strictly capped at 100)

    CursorPaginationQuery:
      type: object
      properties:
        cursor:
          type: string
          description: Opaque cursor for stream or log continuation
        limit:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 100
          default: 50
          description: Number of items per page (strictly capped at 100)
        direction:
          type: string
          enum: [forward, backward]
          default: forward

    PaginatedResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - items
        - page
        - pageSize
        - total
      properties:
        items:
          type: array
          items: {}
        page:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        pageSize:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 100

          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        totalPages:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0

          type: string

            required:
              - status
            properties:
              status:
                type: string
                enum: [ok, degraded, down]
              latencyMs:
                type: number
              message:
                type: string

      type: object

        - event_id
        - event_type
        - schema_version
        - occurred_at
        - correlation_id
        - producer
        - entity_type
        - entity_id

          example: a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d
        event_type:
          type: string
          description: Domain event name
          example: erp.journal_entry.posted
        schema_version:
          type: string
          example: 1.0.0
        occurred_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
          example: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z'
        correlation_id:
          type: string
          example: req-cor-12345
        producer:
          type: string
          example: erp-4
        entity_type:
          type: string
          example: journal_entry
        entity_id:
          type: string
          example: 00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001
        payload:
          type: object
          description: Event body
        metadata:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: true

  responses:
    BadRequest:
      description: Invalid request payload, malformed query, or schema violation (400)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope'
          example:
            code: BAD_REQUEST
            message: Validation failed
            details:
              - field: pageSize
                message: pageSize cannot exceed 100

    Unauthorized:
      description: Authentication required or JWT token missing/invalid/expired (401)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:

          example:

            message: Missing or invalid authentication token

    Forbidden:
      description: Caller lacks required RBAC role or capability (403)

          example:

            message: Insufficient permissions for requested resource

    NotFound:
      description: Referenced domain resource does not exist (404)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope'
          example:
            code: NOT_FOUND
            message: Resource not found

    Conflict:
      description: Deterministic state conflict, duplicate natural key, or version mismatch (409)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope'
          example:
            code: CONFLICT
            message: Exact natural key collision detected

    PayloadTooLarge:
      description: Payload or uploaded file exceeds size limit (413)

          example:
            code: PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE
            message: Uploaded file exceeds 50 MB limit

    UnprocessableEntity:
      description: Domain business invariant violation (422)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope'
          example:
            code: UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY
            message: Domain invariant violation

    TooManyRequests:
      description: Rate limit exceeded (429)
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:

            message: Rate limit exceeded. Try again later.

        application/json:

            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope'
          example:

            message: An unexpected server error occurred

          type: string
          pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$'

        sourceId: { type: string, nullable: true }
        createdAt: { type: string, format: date-time }
        updatedAt: { type: string, format: date-time }

    CreateProductInput:
      type: object

        sku: { type: string, minLength: 1 }

        price: { oneOf: [{ type: string, pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$' }, { type: number, minimum: 0 }] }
        currency: { $ref: '#/components/schemas/Currency' }
        taxRate: { oneOf: [{ type: string, pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$' }, { type: number, minimum: 0 }], default: "0.00" }
        sourceSystem: { type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 64 }

=== packages/contracts/openapi/erp-2.yaml ===
openapi: "3.1.0"

info:
  title: ClearShift ERP — Vendors & Purchasing API
  description: |
    **ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing module** — public REST contract.

    Base path: `/api/v1/erp-vendors`

    Implements:
    - Vendor list with search and bounded pagination (FR-ERP2-01)
    - Create and edit vendor with server-side masked bank details (FR-ERP2-02, FR-ERP2-06)
    - Purchase orders with line items (FR-ERP2-03)
    - Unidirectional purchase order status lifecycle: Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed (FR-ERP2-04)
    - Server-side calculated order totals using decimal arithmetic (FR-ERP2-05)
    - Product line referential integrity via ERP-1 contract (BR-ERP2-01)
    - In-process target adapter and durable import receipts (KIT-08 / Decision C4)

    Amounts are string-encoded decimals (2 decimal places) with ISO currency codes.
    Bank details are masked server-side (`XXXX-XXXX-7890`) and never exposed unmasked.
  version: "1.0.0"
  contact:
    name: ClearShift Engineering — ERP-2 Team
  license:
    name: Private

servers:
  - url: http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-vendors
    description: Local development server
  - url: https://api.clearshift.internal/api/v1/erp-vendors
    description: Staging / production

tags:
  - name: Vendors
    description: Vendor catalog management and search
  - name: Purchase Orders
    description: Purchase order creation, calculations, and status transitions
  - name: Migration Import
    description: Migration Kit target adapter and durable import receipts (KIT-08)

paths:
  /vendors:
    get:
      operationId: listVendors

          schema:

          $ref: "#/components/responses/BadRequest"

openapi: "3.1.0"

info:
  title: ClearShift ERP — Finance & Accounting API
  description: |
    **ERP-4 Finance & Accounting module** — public REST contract.

    Base path: `/api/v1/erp-finance`

    Implements:
    - Chart of Accounts (FR-ERP4-01)
    - Journal Entries with double-entry validation (FR-ERP4-02, BR-ERP4-01)
    - Trial Balance report (FR-ERP4-03)
    - Opening Balances (FR-ERP4-04)
    - Accounting Period management (BR-ERP4-02)

    All amounts are returned as **string-encoded decimals** (numeric strings)
    to avoid IEEE-754 floating-point rounding. Clients must use a
    fixed-precision decimal library for arithmetic.

    All timestamps are ISO 8601 with timezone (UTC).
  version: "1.0.0"
  contact:
    name: ClearShift Engineering — ERP Team
  license:
    name: Private

servers:
  - url: http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-finance
    description: Local development server
  - url: https://api.clearshift.internal/api/v1/erp-finance
    description: Staging / production

tags:
  - name: Accounts
    description: Chart of Accounts management
  - name: Journal Entries
    description: Double-entry journal creation, posting, and reversal
  - name: Trial Balance
    description: Period-scoped trial balance report
  - name: Opening Balances
    description: One-time opening balance loads

security:
  - BearerAuth: []

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Paths
# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
paths:

  # ── Chart of Accounts ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /accounts:
    get:
      operationId: listAccounts
      tags: [Accounts]
      summary: List all accounts in the Chart of Accounts
      description: |
        Returns all accounts. Supports filtering by type and active status.
        Ordered by `code` ascending.
      parameters:
        - name: page
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            default: 1
          description: Page number (1-indexed)
        - name: pageSize
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            maximum: 100
            default: 20
          description: Page size (deterministic paging, hard cap of 100)
        - name: search
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            maxLength: 100
          description: Server-side case-insensitive search across code and name
        - name: type
          in: query
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/AccountType"
          description: Filter by account classification
        - name: is_active
          in: query
          schema:
            type: boolean
          description: Filter by active status
        - name: currency_code
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 3
            maxLength: 3
            pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
          description: Filter by ISO 4217 currency code
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Successful response
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/AccountListResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    post:
      operationId: createAccount
      tags: [Accounts]
      summary: Create a new account (seed/admin/editor only)
      description: |
        Adds a new leaf or header account to the Chart of Accounts.
        Requires `finance:accounts:write` permission.
        Account `code` must be globally unique within the module.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateAccountRequest"
            examples:
              assetAccount:
                summary: Create a bank asset account
                value:
                  code: "1010-BANK"
                  name: "Main Current Account"
                  type: "asset"
                  currency_code: "INR"
                  is_posting_account: true
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Account created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Account"
        "200":
          description: Existing account returned (idempotent duplicate)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Account"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "409":
          description: Account code already exists or idempotency key conflict
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "422":
          description: Unprocessable entity (currency mismatch or parent error)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:accounts:write]

  /accounts/{id}:
    get:
      operationId: getAccount
      tags: [Accounts]
      summary: Get an account by ID
      description: Returns a single account in the Chart of Accounts by UUID.
      parameters:
        - name: id
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Unique account identifier
      responses:

          description: Successful response
          content:

              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Account"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":

          description: Account not found
          content:

                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    patch:
      operationId: updateAccount
      tags: [Accounts]
      summary: Update mutable account fields (admin/editor only)
      description: |
        Updates mutable fields on an account: name, description, is_active,
        is_posting_account, parent_id. Protected system fields (id, code,
        currency_code, created_at, updated_at) are immutable and cannot be changed.
      parameters:
        - name: id
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Unique account identifier
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdateAccountRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Account updated successfully
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Account"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          description: Account not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "422":
          description: Unprocessable entity (cyclic hierarchy or parent account error)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:accounts:write]

  # ── Journal Entries ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /journal-entries:
    get:
      operationId: listJournalEntries
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: List journal entries
      description: |
        Returns a paginated list of journal entries.
        Optionally filter by period (YYYY-MM) and/or status.
      parameters:
        - name: page
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            default: 1
          description: Page number (1-indexed)
        - name: pageSize
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            maximum: 100
            default: 20
          description: Page size (deterministic paging, hard cap of 100)
        - name: limit
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            maximum: 100
          description: Optional alias for pageSize (capped at 100)
        - name: search
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            maxLength: 100
          description: Server-side case-insensitive search across entry_number and description
        - name: period
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            pattern: "^\\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"
            example: "2026-04"
          description: Fiscal period label YYYY-MM
        - name: period_id
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Filter by accounting period UUID
        - name: status
          in: query
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntryStatus"
          description: Filter by journal entry status
        - name: from_date
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            format: date
          description: Filter entries on or after posting date (inclusive)
        - name: to_date
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            format: date
          description: Filter entries on or before posting date (inclusive)
        - name: currency_code
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 3
            maxLength: 3
            pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
          description: Filter by ISO 4217 currency code
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Paginated journal entries
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntryListResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    post:
      operationId: createJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Create a new journal entry (draft)
      description: |
        Creates a journal entry in **draft** status.

        Domain invariants enforced at API layer:
        - `SUM(debit_amount) == SUM(credit_amount)` at 4 decimal places
        - Each line must carry exactly one of debit > 0 OR credit > 0
        - At least 2 lines required

        Draft entries can be edited; only `POST /journal-entries/{id}/post`
        transitions them to `posted`.

        If `idempotency_key` is supplied and a matching entry already exists,
        the existing entry is returned (HTTP 200) without creating a duplicate.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateJournalEntryRequest"
            examples:
              simpleEntry:
                summary: Debit Expense, Credit Bank
                value:
                  period_id: "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890"
                  posting_date: "2026-04-15"
                  description: "Office supplies purchase"
                  currency_code: "INR"
                  lines:
                    - account_id: "1111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111"
                      debit_amount: "5000.0000"
                      credit_amount: "0.0000"
                      line_narration: "Stationery expense"
                    - account_id: "2222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222"
                      debit_amount: "0.0000"
                      credit_amount: "5000.0000"
                      line_narration: "Bank payment"
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Journal entry created as draft
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "200":
          description: Existing entry returned (idempotent duplicate)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "422":
          description: |
            Unprocessable — period is locked/soft-closed, or entry is unbalanced
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  /journal-entries/{id}:
    get:
      operationId: getJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Get a single journal entry with its lines
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      responses:

          description: Journal entry with lines

          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    patch:
      operationId: updateJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Update a draft journal entry
      description: |
        Updates draft journal entry header or lines.
        Only entries in `draft` status may be updated; posted entries are immutable (returns 422).
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdateJournalEntryRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Journal entry updated successfully
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "422":
          description: Entry is immutable (posted/reversed/voided) or lines unbalanced
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  /journal-entries/{id}/post:
    post:
      operationId: postJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Post a draft journal entry (makes it immutable)
      description: |
        Validates and posts a draft entry:
        1. Validates that the accounting period is `open` (or `soft_closed` for admins)
        2. Re-validates that debits == credits at 4dp (guard against concurrent edits)
        3. Transitions `status` → `posted`, sets `posted_at` and `posted_by_user_id`
        4. Posted entries are **immutable**; corrections require a reversal entry.

        Requires `finance:journal:post` permission.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      requestBody:
        required: false
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                notes:
                  type: string
                  maxLength: 500
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Entry posted successfully
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          description: Entry is already posted or reversed
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "422":
          description: Period is locked, or entry is unbalanced
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:journal:post]

  /journal-entries/{id}/reverse:
    post:
      operationId: reverseJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Reverse a posted journal entry
      description: |
        Creates a compensating reversal entry (debit↔credit swapped) and
        links the original entry's `reversed_by_entry_id` to the new entry.

        The original entry transitions to `status = 'reversed'`.
        Requires `finance:journal:post` permission.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      requestBody:
        required: false
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/ReverseJournalEntryRequest"
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Reversal entry created; original entry marked reversed
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          description: Entry is not in posted status and cannot be reversed
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "422":
          description: Entry is not reversible or target period locked
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:journal:post]

  /journal-entries/{id}/void:
    post:
      operationId: voidJournalEntry
      tags: [Journal Entries]
      summary: Void a draft journal entry
      description: |
        Voids an unposted draft journal entry.
        Only entries in `draft` status may be voided; posted entries cannot be voided and must be reversed.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Journal entry voided successfully
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/JournalEntry"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "422":
          description: Entry is immutable (cannot void posted entry)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  # ── Trial Balance ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /trial-balance:
    get:
      operationId: getTrialBalance
      tags: [Trial Balance]
      summary: Generate the trial balance for a period
      description: |
        Returns debit and credit totals grouped by account and currency
        for all **posted** journal entries within the given fiscal period.

        Opening balances are included in `opening_balance` and `closing_balance`
        columns.

        **Note**: Unlike currencies are never summed together.
        The `totals` object reports totals per currency; `is_balanced` is
        `true` only when every currency group balances independently.

        FR-ERP4-03
      parameters:
        - name: period
          in: query
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            pattern: "^\\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"
            example: "2026-04"
          description: Fiscal period in YYYY-MM format
        - name: currency_code
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 3
            maxLength: 3
            pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
          description: "Optional: filter results to a single currency"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Trial balance for the given period
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/TrialBalanceResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "404":
          description: No accounting period found for the given label
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  # ── Opening Balances ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /opening-balances:
    post:
      operationId: createOpeningBalances
      tags: [Opening Balances]
      summary: Load opening balances (one-time per account/currency)
      description: |
        Bulk-loads opening balances for the chart of accounts.

        Each `(account_id, currency_code)` pair is unique — attempting to load
        a balance for a pair that already has one returns a `409` or a skipped
        entry in the response, depending on server configuration.

        Requires `finance:opening-balances:write` permission.
        FR-ERP4-04
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateOpeningBalancesRequest"
            examples:
              bulkLoad:
                summary: Load opening balances for 3 accounts
                value:
                  balances:
                    - account_id: "aaaa-bbbb-cccc-dddd-eeeeeeeeeeee"
                      currency_code: "INR"
                      balance_amount: "150000.0000"
                      as_of_date: "2026-04-01"
                    - account_id: "ffff-gggg-hhhh-iiii-jjjjjjjjjjjj"
                      currency_code: "INR"
                      balance_amount: "-25000.0000"
                      as_of_date: "2026-04-01"
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Opening balances created (partial success reported in `skipped`)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateOpeningBalancesResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "409":
          description: Idempotency conflict
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:opening-balances:write]

    get:
      operationId: listOpeningBalances
      tags: [Opening Balances]
      summary: List opening balances
      description: Returns a paginated list of opening balances with bounded paging (hard cap of 100).
      parameters:
        - name: page
          in: query

        - name: pageSize
          in: query
          schema:
            type: integer
            minimum: 1
            maximum: 100
            default: 20
          description: Page size (deterministic paging, hard cap of 100)
        - name: account_id
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Filter by account UUID
        - name: currency_code
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 3
            maxLength: 3
            pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
          description: Filter by ISO 4217 currency code
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Paginated opening balances
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/OpeningBalanceListResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    patch:
      operationId: updatePeriod
      tags: [Accounting Periods]
      summary: Update accounting period lifecycle status
      description: |
        Updates the lifecycle status of an accounting period.
        Enforces FSM transitions:
        - `open` -> `soft_closed` (finance_admin or admin role)
        - `open` -> `locked` (admin role)
        - `soft_closed` -> `open` (admin role)
        - `soft_closed` -> `locked` (admin role)
        - `locked` -> any (FORBIDDEN, terminal status)
      parameters:
        - name: id
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Unique period identifier
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdatePeriodRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Period successfully updated
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/AccountingPeriod"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          description: Accounting period not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "422":
          description: Illegal period state transition
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: [finance:periods:write]

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Components
# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
components:

  securitySchemes:
    BearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT
      description: |
        Asymmetric EdDSA (Ed25519) JSON Web Token issued by ClearShift Hub (`clearshift-hub`).
        Audience: `clearshift`.
        Verifies active user state, expiration (`exp`), and RBAC roles (`viewer`, `editor`, `migration_runner`, `analyst`, `admin`).

  parameters:
    ResourceId:
      name: id
      in: path
      required: true
      schema:
        type: string
        format: uuid
      description: UUID of the resource
    IdempotencyKey:
      name: Idempotency-Key
      in: header
      required: false
      schema:
        type: string
        maxLength: 255
      description: Optional client-assigned idempotency key for safe retries

  responses:
    Unauthorized:
      description: Authentication required
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    Forbidden:
      description: Insufficient permissions
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    NotFound:
      description: Resource not found
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    ValidationError:
      description: Request validation failed
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    InternalError:
      description: Internal server error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"

  schemas:

    AccountType:
      type: string
      enum: [asset, liability, income, expense]
      description: Account classification per standard chart-of-accounts taxonomy

    JournalEntryStatus:
      type: string
      enum: [draft, posted, reversed, voided]

    PeriodStatus:
      type: string
      enum: [open, soft_closed, locked]

    Account:
      type: object
      required:
        - id
        - code
        - name
        - type
        - currency_code
        - is_active
        - is_posting_account
        - created_at
        - updated_at
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        code:
          type: string
          maxLength: 32
          example: "1010-BANK"
        name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 255
          example: "Main Current Account"
        type:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/AccountType"
        currency_code:
          type: string
          minLength: 3
          maxLength: 3
          example: "INR"
        parent_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
          nullable: true
        description:
          type: string
          nullable: true
        is_active:
          type: boolean
        is_posting_account:
          type: boolean
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    CreateAccountRequest:
      type: object
      required: [code, name, type]
      properties:
        code:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 32
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 255
        type:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/AccountType"
        currency_code:
          type: string
          minLength: 3
          maxLength: 3
          default: "INR"
        parent_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
          nullable: true
        description:
          type: string
          maxLength: 2000
          nullable: true
        is_posting_account:
          type: boolean
          default: true
        idempotency_key:
          type: string
          maxLength: 255
        batch_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        source_row:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0

    UpdateAccountRequest:
      type: object
      properties:
        name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 255
        description:
          type: string
          maxLength: 1000
          nullable: true
        is_active:
          type: boolean
        is_posting_account:
          type: boolean
        parent_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
          nullable: true

    AccountListResponse:
      type: object
      required: [data, total, page, pageSize, totalPages]
      properties:
        data:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/Account"
        total:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        page:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        pageSize:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 100
        totalPages:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0

    JournalLine:
      type: object
      required:
        - id
        - journal_entry_id
        - account_id
        - debit_amount
        - credit_amount
        - created_at
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        journal_entry_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        account_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        debit_amount:
          type: string
          description: Non-negative decimal string (4dp)
          example: "5000.0000"
        credit_amount:
          type: string
          description: Non-negative decimal string (4dp)
          example: "0.0000"
        line_narration:
          type: string
          maxLength: 255
          nullable: true
        department_ref:
          type: string
          maxLength: 64
          nullable: true
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    JournalEntry:
      type: object

          type: string

              type: string

              type: string
            difference:
              type: string

=== packages/contracts/openapi/erp-5.yaml ===
openapi: "3.1.0"

info:
  title: ClearShift ERP — HR & Employees API
  description: |
    **ERP-5 HR & Employees module** — public REST contract.

    Base path: `/api/v1/erp-hr`

    Implements:
    - Employee list, search and create/edit (FR-ERP5-01, FR-ERP5-02)
    - Departments with optional parent hierarchy (FR-ERP5-03)
    - Leave records with date range, type and status (FR-ERP5-04)
    - Read-only Hub Identity user projection (FR-ERP5-05, Master Architecture Spec C3)
    - Department/month payroll aggregates (FR-ERP5-06)

    Domain rules enforced by both the API and the database:
    - `BR-ERP5-01` — join date can never be before the birth date.
    - `BR-ERP5-02` — salary is never returned in full to viewers and never logged.

    Monetary values are returned as **string-encoded decimals** to avoid
    IEEE-754 floating-point rounding. All timestamps are ISO 8601 with timezone.
  version: "1.0.0"
  contact:
    name: ClearShift Engineering — ERP Team
  license:
    name: Private

servers:
  - url: http://localhost:3000/api/v1/erp-hr
    description: Local development server
  - url: https://api.clearshift.internal/api/v1/erp-hr
    description: Staging / production

tags:
  - name: Employees
    description: Employee master records
  - name: Departments
    description: Department hierarchy
  - name: Leave
    description: Leave records
  - name: Identity
    description: Read-only Hub Identity projection
  - name: Payroll
    description: Department/month payroll aggregates

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Paths
# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
paths:

  # ── Employees ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /employees:
    get:
      operationId: listEmployees
      tags: [Employees]
      summary: List employees
      description: |
        Returns a bounded page of employees. Results are ordered by the
        requested sort field with a deterministic `id` tie-breaker.
        Salary is never returned in full — the `EmployeeView` projection
        exposes `salary_display` only (BR-ERP5-02).
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/Page"
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/PageSize"
        - name: search
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            maxLength: 120
          description: Server-side case-insensitive search across first name, last name and employee number
        - name: department_id
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: Restrict the result set to one department
        - name: sort
          in: query
          schema:
            type: string
            enum: [name, employee_no, join_date]
            default: name
          description: Allow-listed sort field
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Successful response
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EmployeeListResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    post:
      operationId: createEmployee
      tags: [Employees]
      summary: Create an employee
      description: |
        Creates an employee. `employee_no` must be unique across the module
        and `join_date` must not precede `birth_date` (BR-ERP5-01).

        Requires the shared `module.write` capability — **Editor** and
        **Admin** (the role set of the central matrix's `hr.mutate` policy,
        PLT-06). Viewer, Analyst and Migration Runner receive `403`.

        The response carries the **masked** `EmployeeView` projection — the raw
        `salary` column never leaves the module (BR-ERP5-02, DR-5).

        Import/load write: supply `batch_id`, `source_row` and an
        `Idempotency-Key` together. The three fields travel together because
        an idempotency key without a batch and a source row cannot produce a
        durable receipt. The receipt is written in the same transaction as the
        employee row, and `source_system` / `source_id` may then be set by the
        Migration Kit. A repeated request with the same idempotency key
        returns the original receipt and the original row (HTTP 200) without
        performing a second mutation.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateEmployeeRequest"
            examples:
              employee:
                summary: Create an employee
                value:
                  employee_no: "EMP-0001"
                  first_name: "Asha"
                  last_name: "Iyer"
                  birth_date: "1990-04-12"
                  join_date: "2024-01-08"
                  department_id: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111"
                  job_title: "Software Engineer"
                  salary: "125000.0000"
                  currency_code: "INR"
              employeeImport:
                summary: Import an employee (Migration Kit)
                value:
                  employee_no: "EMP-0001"
                  first_name: "Asha"
                  last_name: "Iyer"
                  birth_date: "1990-04-12"
                  join_date: "2024-01-08"
                  department_id: "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111"
                  job_title: "Software Engineer"
                  salary: "125000.0000"
                  source_system: "legacy_hr_csv"
                  source_id: "row-0001"
                  batch_id: "44444444-4444-4444-4444-444444444444"
                  source_row: 1
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Employee created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EmployeeMutationResponse"
        "200":
          description: |
            Idempotent replay — the original employee and the original import
            receipt are returned and no second mutation was performed.
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EmployeeMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Conflict"
        "422":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  /employees/{id}:
    get:
      operationId: getEmployee
      tags: [Employees]
      summary: Read an employee
      description: |
        Returns one employee as the **masked** projection — the raw `salary`
        column never leaves the module (BR-ERP5-02).

        Requires the shared `module.read` capability.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Successful response
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EmployeeView"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    patch:
      operationId: updateEmployee
      tags: [Employees]
      summary: Edit an employee
      description: |
        Updates the allow-listed mutable fields of an employee. The
        `join_date`/`birth_date` pair is re-validated against the stored
        values (BR-ERP5-01). `employee_no` remains unique across the module.

        `id`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `currency_code`, `source_system` and
        `source_id` are system-maintained and are rejected with
        `400 HR_PROTECTED_FIELD` — a browser may never set them.

        Requires the shared `module.write` capability — **Editor** and
        **Admin** (PLT-06 `hr.mutate`).
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdateEmployeeRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Employee updated
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EmployeeMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Conflict"
        "422":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdateDepartmentRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Department updated
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/DepartmentMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"

          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Conflict"

          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"

          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:

    post:
      operationId: createLeave
      tags: [Leave]
      summary: Create a leave record
      description: |
        Creates a leave record. `date_to` must not precede `date_from` and the
        referenced employee must exist.

        Requires the shared `module.write` capability — **Editor** and
        **Admin** (PLT-06 `hr.mutate`).

        V1 records the leave status only; the request → approve/reject
        workflow (FR-ERP5-10) is a V2 capability.

        Import/load write: supply `batch_id`, `source_row` and an
        `Idempotency-Key` together. The receipt is written in the same
        transaction as the leave row.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreateLeaveRequest"
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Leave record created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/LeaveMutationResponse"
        "200":
          description: |
            Idempotent replay — the original leave record and the original
            import receipt are returned and no second mutation was performed.
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/LeaveMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Conflict"
        "422":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  /leave/{id}:
    get:
      operationId: getLeave
      tags: [Leave]
      summary: Read a leave record
      description: |
        Returns one leave record. Requires the shared `module.read` capability.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Successful response
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Leave"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

    patch:
      operationId: updateLeave
      tags: [Leave]
      summary: Edit a leave record and apply the V1 state transition
      description: |
        Updates a leave record.

        **State transition.** `status` follows the V1 leave state matrix:
        `pending → approved | rejected` is the only transition, and `approved`
        and `rejected` are terminal — a decision is not reversed and a leave
        is never un-decided. Setting the same status again is a no-op. A
        forbidden transition is answered with
        `422 HR_INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITION` and the allowed set in the
        message. The full request → approve/reject workflow with a log
        (FR-ERP5-10) is a V2 capability and is not exposed here.

        **Immutable subject.** The employee the leave belongs to is never
        re-parented — a leave is a fact about one employee.

        `date_from`, `date_to` and `type` stay editable and are re-validated
        (date range and enumeration).

        Requires the shared `module.write` capability — **Editor** and
        **Admin** (PLT-06 `hr.mutate`).
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/ResourceId"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/UpdateLeaveRequest"
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Leave record updated
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/LeaveMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "422":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  # ── Identity (read-only projection) ───────────────────────────────────────

        does not store credentials and does not grant application roles; the
        projection exposes identity attributes only — no password, hash, token

  # ── Payroll totals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /payroll-totals:

      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/Page"

        - BearerAuth: []

    post:
      operationId: createPayrollTotal
      tags: [Payroll]
      summary: Load a department/month payroll aggregate
      description: |
        Loads one department/month aggregate. One aggregate exists per
        department and month; a second load for the same pair is rejected with
        409. The payload carries no employee-level data — an employee field is
        rejected with `400 HR_PROTECTED_FIELD` (FR-ERP5-06).

        Authorization follows the central matrix of PLT-06
        (`hr.payroll_bulk`): the **Migration Runner** and **Admin** may load
        an aggregate; Viewer, Editor and Analyst receive `403`.

        Import/load write: supply `batch_id`, `source_row` and an
        `Idempotency-Key` together. The receipt is written in the same
        transaction as the aggregate row.
      parameters:
        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey"
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: "#/components/schemas/CreatePayrollTotalRequest"
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Aggregate created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/PayrollTotalMutationResponse"
        "200":
          description: |
            Idempotent replay — the original aggregate and the original import
            receipt are returned and no second mutation was performed.
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/PayrollTotalMutationResponse"
        "400":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "401":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Unauthorized"
        "403":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Forbidden"
        "404":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"
        "409":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/Conflict"
        "422":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/ValidationError"
        "500":
          $ref: "#/components/responses/InternalError"
      security:
        - BearerAuth: []

  /payroll-totals/{id}:
    get:

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Components
# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
components:

  securitySchemes:
    BearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT

  parameters:
    ResourceId:
      name: id
      in: path
      required: true
      schema:
        type: string
        format: uuid
      description: UUID of the resource
    Page:
      name: page
      in: query
      schema:
        type: integer
        minimum: 1
        default: 1
      description: Page number (1-indexed)
    PageSize:
      name: pageSize
      in: query
      schema:
        type: integer
        minimum: 1
        maximum: 100
        default: 25
      description: Page size (deterministic paging, hard cap of 100)
    IdempotencyKey:
      name: Idempotency-Key
      in: header
      required: false
      schema:
        type: string
        maxLength: 128
      description: |
        Stable idempotency key for a safe retry — `batch id + source row +
        target entity/schema version`. It must be supplied together with
        `batch_id` and `source_row` in the request body: an idempotency key
        without a batch and a source row cannot produce a durable import
        receipt and is therefore rejected with
        `400 HR_IMPORT_PROVENANCE_REQUIRED`.

        The key may also be sent as the `idempotency_key` body field; the
        header and the body field must be identical.

  responses:
    Unauthorized:
      description: Authentication required
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    Forbidden:
      description: Insufficient permissions
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    NotFound:
      description: Resource not found
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    ValidationError:
      description: Request failed validation
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    Conflict:
      description: Unique or idempotency conflict
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"
    InternalError:
      description: Unexpected server error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: "#/components/schemas/ApiError"

  schemas:

    ApiError:
      type: object
      description: |
        Standard error envelope. It mirrors the shared platform envelope
        merged with PLT-04 (`packages/contracts/openapi/common.yaml` →
        `#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope`, Zod contract
        `@clearshift/contracts` → `ErrorEnvelopeSchema`) — it is not redefined
        per module.

        Status mapping:

        | Status | Meaning | Example `code` |
        |---|---|---|
        | 400 | malformed request | `VALIDATION_ERROR`, `HR_PROTECTED_FIELD` |
        | 401 | unauthenticated | `UNAUTHORIZED` |
        | 403 | forbidden | `FORBIDDEN` |
        | 404 | missing record | `NOT_FOUND`, `HR_DEPARTMENT_REQUIRED`, `HR_EMPLOYEE_REQUIRED` |
        | 409 | duplicate / idempotency conflict | `HR_EMPLOYEE_NO_DUPLICATE`, `HR_DEPARTMENT_CODE_DUPLICATE`, `HR_PAYROLL_TOTAL_DUPLICATE`, `HR_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` |
        | 422 | invalid business value | `HR_JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE`, `HR_LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID`, `HR_SALARY_NON_NEGATIVE`, `HR_DEPARTMENT_SELF_PARENT`, `HR_DEPARTMENT_CYCLE` |
        | 500 | unexpected failure | `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR` |

        The envelope never carries a SQL statement, a stack trace, a secret or
        a raw row. An unexpected failure returns only a correlation id.
      required: [code, message]
      properties:
        code:
          type: string
          description: Stable machine-readable error code
          examples: ["HR_JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE"]
        message:
          type: string
          description: Human-readable summary
        details:
          type: array
          default: []
          items:
            type: object
            properties:
              field:
                type: string
              message:
                type: string
              code:
                type: string
        correlation_id:
          type: string
          description: Correlation id for log tracing

    ImportReceipt:
      type: object
      description: |
        Durable import receipt (`erp5.import_receipts`). It is written in the
        same PostgreSQL transaction as the target mutation, so a receipt can
        never exist without its row and a row can never exist without its
        receipt.
      required:
        [id, batch_id, source_row, entity, schema_version, idempotency_key,
         target_id, kind, created_at]
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        batch_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        source_row:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        entity:
          type: string
          maxLength: 64
          description: Target entity of the load
          examples: ["employee"]
        schema_version:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          default: 1
        idempotency_key:
          type: string
          maxLength: 128
        target_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        kind:
          type: string
          enum: [created, linked]
          default: created
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    ListMeta:
      type: object
      required: [page, pageSize, total, totalPages]
      properties:
        page:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
        pageSize:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 100
        total:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0
        totalPages:
          type: integer
          minimum: 0

    LeaveType:
      type: string
      enum: [sick, holiday, other]

    LeaveStatus:
      type: string
      enum: [pending, approved, rejected]

    Department:
      type: object
      required: [id, name, code, parent_id, created_at, updated_at]
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 255
        code:
          type: string
          maxLength: 32
          description: Unique department code
        parent_id:
          type: [string, "null"]
          format: uuid
          description: Optional parent department
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    CreateDepartmentRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required: [name, code]
      properties:
        name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 255
        code:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 32
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        parent_id:
          type: [string, "null"]
          format: uuid
        batch_id:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/BatchId"
        source_row:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/SourceRow"
        idempotency_key:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/IdempotencyKeyValue"

    UpdateDepartmentRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      minProperties: 1
      properties:
        name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 255
        code:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 32
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        parent_id:
          type: [string, "null"]
          format: uuid

    BatchId:
      type: string
      format: uuid
      description: Migration batch identifier — stored on the import receipt.

    SourceRow:
      type: integer
      minimum: 0
      maximum: 1000000
      description: Source row number inside the migration batch.

    IdempotencyKeyValue:
      type: string
      minLength: 1
      maxLength: 128
      description: |
        Stable idempotency key. It must travel together with `batch_id` and
        `source_row`; a partial triple is rejected with
        `400 HR_IMPORT_PROVENANCE_REQUIRED`.

    Employee:
      type: object
      description: |
        Privileged internal representation of an employee.

        **Not returned by any V1 endpoint.** Every employee response uses the
        masked `EmployeeView` projection, because BR-ERP5-02 forbids showing
        the full salary to a viewer and V1 defines no role that may see it
        (DR-5). The schema is kept so the internal shape stays documented.
      required:
        [id, employee_no, first_name, last_name, birth_date, join_date,
         department_id, job_title, salary, currency_code, source_system,
         source_id, created_at, updated_at]
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        employee_no:
          type: string
          maxLength: 32
          description: Unique declared employee identifier
        first_name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        last_name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        birth_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        join_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        department_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        job_title:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        salary:
          type: string
          pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$'
          description: String-encoded decimal. Masked before serialization for viewers.
        currency_code:
          type: string
          pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
        source_system:
          type: [string, "null"]
          maxLength: 64
        source_id:
          type: [string, "null"]
          maxLength: 128
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    EmployeeView:
      type: object
      description: |
        Employee projection for viewers — the raw `salary` column is absent and
        replaced by a masked display value (BR-ERP5-02).
      required:
        [id, employee_no, first_name, last_name, birth_date, join_date,
         department_id, job_title, salary_display, currency_code,
         created_at, updated_at]
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        employee_no:
          type: string
          maxLength: 32
        first_name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        last_name:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        birth_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        join_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        department_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        job_title:
          type: string
          maxLength: 120
        salary_display:
          type: string
          description: Masked salary — "XX,XXX" or "salary hidden"
          examples: ["XX,XXX"]
        currency_code:
          type: string
          pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    CreateEmployeeRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      required:
        [employee_no, first_name, last_name, birth_date, join_date,
         department_id, job_title, salary]
      properties:
        employee_no:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 32
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        first_name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        last_name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        birth_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        join_date:
          type: string
          format: date
          description: Must not precede birth_date (BR-ERP5-01)
        department_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        job_title:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        salary:
          type: string
          pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$'
        currency_code:
          type: string
          pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$"
          description: |
            ISO 4217 currency of the salary. Optional on create (defaults to
            `INR`) and immutable afterwards — a patch that carries
            `currency_code` is rejected with `400 HR_PROTECTED_FIELD`.
        source_system:
          type: string
          maxLength: 64
          description: |
            System-maintained. Only an import write (a request carrying
            `batch_id`, `source_row` and an idempotency key) may set it; a
            browser write is rejected with `400 HR_PROTECTED_FIELD`.
        source_id:
          type: string
          maxLength: 128
          description: |
            System-maintained. Only an import write (a request carrying
            `batch_id`, `source_row` and an idempotency key) may set it; a
            browser write is rejected with `400 HR_PROTECTED_FIELD`.
        batch_id:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/BatchId"
        source_row:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/SourceRow"
        idempotency_key:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/IdempotencyKeyValue"

    UpdateEmployeeRequest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: false
      minProperties: 1
      properties:
        employee_no:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 32
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        first_name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        last_name:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        birth_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        join_date:
          type: string
          format: date
        department_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        job_title:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
          maxLength: 120
        salary:
          type: string
          pattern: '^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$'

    Leave:
      type: object
      required: [id, employee_id, date_from, date_to, type, status, created_at, updated_at]
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        employee_id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        date_from:
          type: string
          format: date
        date_to:
          type: string
          format: date
          description: Must not precede date_from
        type:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/LeaveType"
        status:
          $ref: "#/components/schemas/LeaveStatus"
        created_at:
