import { z } from 'zod';

/**
 * Standard HTTP Status Codes mandated by ClearShift Architecture (PLT-04)
 */
export const HttpStatusCodes = {
  BAD_REQUEST: 400,
  UNAUTHORIZED: 401,
  FORBIDDEN: 403,
  NOT_FOUND: 404,
  CONFLICT: 409,
  PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE: 413,
  UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY: 422,
  TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: 429,
  INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR: 500,
} as const;

export type HttpStatusCode = (typeof HttpStatusCodes)[keyof typeof HttpStatusCodes];

/**
 * Standard Machine-Readable Error Codes
 */
export const StandardErrorCodes = {
  BAD_REQUEST: 'BAD_REQUEST',
  VALIDATION_ERROR: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
  UNAUTHORIZED: 'UNAUTHORIZED',
  FORBIDDEN: 'FORBIDDEN',
  NOT_FOUND: 'NOT_FOUND',
  CONFLICT: 'CONFLICT',
  PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE: 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',
  UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY: 'UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY',
  BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION: 'BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION',
  TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: 'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS',
  RATE_LIMITED: 'RATE_LIMITED',
  INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR: 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR',
  UNEXPECTED_ERROR: 'UNEXPECTED_ERROR',
} as const;

export const StandardErrorCodeSchema = z.enum([
  'BAD_REQUEST',
  'VALIDATION_ERROR',
  'UNAUTHORIZED',
  'FORBIDDEN',
  'NOT_FOUND',
  'CONFLICT',
  'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',
  'UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY',
  'BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION',
  'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS',
  'RATE_LIMITED',
  'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR',
  'UNEXPECTED_ERROR',
]);

export type StandardErrorCode = z.infer<typeof StandardErrorCodeSchema>;

/**
 * Standard Field-Level Error Detail Schema
 */
export const ErrorDetailSchema = z.object({
  field: z.string().optional(),
  message: z.string().min(1, 'Error detail message is required'),
  code: z.string().optional(),
});

export type ErrorDetail = z.infer<typeof ErrorDetailSchema>;

/**
 * Standard Flat Error Envelope Schema (PLT-04 / @cs/kernel)
 * Canonical format: { code, message, details, correlation_id }
 */
export const FlatErrorEnvelopeSchema = z.object({
  code: z.string().min(1, 'Error code is required'),
  message: z.string().min(1, 'Error message is required'),
  details: z
    .union([z.array(ErrorDetailSchema), z.record(z.unknown()), z.unknown()])
    .optional()
    .default([]),
  correlation_id: z.string().optional(),
});

/**
 * Nested Error Envelope Schema for module-level compatibility ({ error: { code, message, details } })
 */
export const NestedErrorEnvelopeSchema = z.object({
  error: z.object({
    code: z.string().min(1, 'Error code is required'),
    message: z.string().min(1, 'Error message is required'),
    details: z.unknown().optional(),
  }),
  correlation_id: z.string().optional(),
});

/**
 * Unified Error Envelope Schema: accepts canonical flat envelope or nested module format
 */
export const ErrorEnvelopeSchema = z.union([
  FlatErrorEnvelopeSchema,
  NestedErrorEnvelopeSchema,
]);

export type ErrorEnvelope = z.infer<typeof FlatErrorEnvelopeSchema>;

/**
 * Helper to build a canonical FlatErrorEnvelope response
 */
export function createErrorEnvelope(
  code: string,
  message: string,
  details: unknown = [],
  correlationId?: string
): ErrorEnvelope {
  return {
    code,
    message,
    details,
    ...(correlationId ? { correlation_id: correlationId } : {}),
  };
}

/**
 * Bounded Pagination Query Schema (Page/PageSize/Limit)
 * Non-negotiable constraint: pageSize and limit must be integers capped at 100
 */
export const BasePaginationQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'page must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'page must be >= 1' })
    .default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'pageSize must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'pageSize must be >= 1' })
    .max(100, { message: 'pageSize cannot exceed 100' })
    .optional(),
  limit: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'limit must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'limit must be >= 1' })
    .max(100, { message: 'limit cannot exceed 100' })
    .optional(),
});

export type BasePaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof BasePaginationQuerySchema>;

/**
 * Standard Paginated Query Schema with default pageSize of 20
 */
export const StandardPaginationQuerySchema = BasePaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'pageSize must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'pageSize must be >= 1' })
    .max(100, { message: 'pageSize cannot exceed 100' })
    .default(20),
});

export type StandardPaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof StandardPaginationQuerySchema>;

/**
 * Cursor Pagination Query Schema for large audit logs and streams
 */
export const CursorPaginationQuerySchema = z.object({
  cursor: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  limit: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'limit must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'limit must be >= 1' })
    .max(100, { message: 'limit cannot exceed 100' })
    .default(50),
  direction: z.enum(['forward', 'backward']).default('forward'),
});

export type CursorPaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof CursorPaginationQuerySchema>;

/**
 * Factory for standard paginated list response schema
 */
export function createPaginatedResponseSchema<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(itemSchema: T) {
  return z.object({
    items: z.array(itemSchema),
    page: z.number().int().min(1),
    pageSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(100),
    total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
    totalPages: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
  });
}

/**
 * Factory for standard cursor-paginated response schema
 */
export function createCursorPaginatedResponseSchema<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(itemSchema: T) {
  return z.object({
    items: z.array(itemSchema),
    nextCursor: z.string().nullable().optional(),
    prevCursor: z.string().nullable().optional(),
    hasMore: z.boolean(),
  });
}

/**
 * Standard Health Check Response Schema
 */
export const HealthStatusSchema = z.enum(['ok', 'degraded', 'down']);
export type HealthStatus = z.infer<typeof HealthStatusSchema>;

export const HealthResponseSchema = z.object({
  status: HealthStatusSchema,
  module: z.string().min(1),
  version: z.string().min(1),
  timestamp: z.string().datetime().optional(),
  dependencies: z
    .record(
      z.object({
        status: HealthStatusSchema,
        latencyMs: z.number().optional(),
        message: z.string().optional(),
      })
    )
    .optional(),
});

export type HealthResponse = z.infer<typeof HealthResponseSchema>;
=== packages/contracts/src/erp-1.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  StandardPaginationQuerySchema as PaginationQuerySchema,
  createPaginatedResponseSchema,
  ErrorEnvelopeSchema,
} from './common.js';

export const ERP1_API_PREFIX = '/api/v1/erp-products';

/**
 * Fixed list of units of measure per FR-ERP1-04.
 */
export const UnitOfMeasureSchema = z.enum([
  'pcs',
  'kg',
  'g',
  'litre',
  'metre',
  'box',
]);
export type UnitOfMeasure = z.infer<typeof UnitOfMeasureSchema>;

/**
 * ISO 4217 currency code. ERP-1 has no approved implicit currency default in
 * the SDS, so callers must supply the currency explicitly.
 */
export const CurrencySchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .regex(/^[A-Z]{3}$/, 'Currency must be an uppercase ISO 4217 code');

const PricePattern = /^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/;
const QuantityPattern = /^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/;
const TaxRatePattern = /^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/;

function normalizeDecimalInput(
  value: string | number,
  scale: number,
  message: string,
  ctx: z.RefinementCtx
): string {
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom, message });
      return z.NEVER;
    }
    return value.toFixed(scale);
  }

  const text = value.trim();
  const pattern = new RegExp(`^\\d+(\\.\\d{1,${scale}})?$`);
  if (!pattern.test(text)) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom, message });
    return z.NEVER;
  }
  return Number(text).toFixed(scale);
}

/**
 * Normalizes SKU strings for exact deterministic matching (uppercase, trimmed).
 */
export function normalizeSku(sku: string): string {
  return sku.trim().toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Category Schema (FR-ERP1-03: one level only, name & code).
 */
export const CategorySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(),
  name: z.string().min(1, 'Category name is required'),
  code: z.string().min(1, 'Category code is required'),
  createdAt: z.string().datetime(),
  updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type Category = z.infer<typeof CategorySchema>;

export const CreateCategoryInputSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Category name is required'),
  code: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Category code is required'),
});
export type CreateCategoryInput = z.infer<typeof CreateCategoryInputSchema>;

export const UpdateCategoryInputSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).optional(),
  code: z.string().trim().min(1).optional(),
});
export type UpdateCategoryInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateCategoryInputSchema>;

/**
 * Warehouse Schema (FR-ERP1-05: one warehouse in V1).
 */
export const WarehouseSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  code: z.string().min(1),
  isDefault: z.boolean().default(true),
  createdAt: z.string().datetime(),
  updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type Warehouse = z.infer<typeof WarehouseSchema>;

/**
 * Product Schema (FR-ERP1-01, FR-ERP1-02, BR-ERP1-01).
 *
 * Money and quantities use the SDS numeric(19,4) precision.
 */
export const ProductSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(),
  name: z.string().min(1, 'Product name is required'),
  sku: z.string().min(1, 'SKU is required'),
  normalizedSku: z.string().min(1),
  categoryId: z.string().uuid().nullable().optional(),
  unitOfMeasure: UnitOfMeasureSchema,
  price: z
    .string()
    .regex(PricePattern, 'Price must be a valid non-negative decimal string with up to 4 decimal places'),
  currency: CurrencySchema,
  taxRate: z
    .string()
    .regex(TaxRatePattern, 'Tax rate must be a valid non-negative decimal percentage')
    .default('0.00'),
  sourceSystem: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  sourceId: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  createdAt: z.string().datetime(),
  updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type Product = z.infer<typeof ProductSchema>;

export const CreateProductInputSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Product name is required'),
  sku: z.string().trim().min(1, 'SKU is required'),
  categoryId: z.string().uuid().nullable().optional(),
  unitOfMeasure: UnitOfMeasureSchema,
  price: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .transform((val, ctx) =>
      normalizeDecimalInput(
        val,
        4,
        'Price must be greater than or equal to 0',
        ctx
      )
    ),
  currency: CurrencySchema,
  taxRate: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .transform((val, ctx) =>
      normalizeDecimalInput(val, 2, 'Tax rate must be non-negative', ctx)
    )
    .default('0.00')
    .optional(),
  sourceSystem: z.string().trim().min(1).max(64).optional(),
  sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(128).optional(),
});
export type CreateProductInput = z.infer<typeof CreateProductInputSchema>;

export const UpdateProductInputSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).optional(),
  categoryId: z.string().uuid().nullable().optional(),
  unitOfMeasure: UnitOfMeasureSchema.optional(),
  price: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .transform((val, ctx) =>
      normalizeDecimalInput(
        val,
        4,
        'Price must be greater than or equal to 0',
        ctx
      )
    )
    .optional(),
  currency: CurrencySchema.optional(),
  taxRate: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .transform((val, ctx) =>
      normalizeDecimalInput(val, 2, 'Tax rate must be non-negative', ctx)
    )
    .optional(),
});
export type UpdateProductInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateProductInputSchema>;

/**
 * Stock Level Schema (FR-ERP1-06, BR-ERP1-02: quantity >= 0).
 */
export const StockLevelSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(),
  productId: z.string().uuid(),
  warehouseId: z.string().uuid(),
  quantity: z
    .string()
    .regex(QuantityPattern, 'Quantity must be a non-negative decimal with up to 4 decimal places'),
  updatedAt: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type StockLevel = z.infer<typeof StockLevelSchema>;

export const SetOpeningStockInputSchema = z.object({
  productId: z.string().uuid(),
  warehouseId: z.string().uuid().optional(),
  quantity: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .transform((val, ctx) =>
      normalizeDecimalInput(
        val,
        4,
        'Stock quantity cannot become negative (BR-ERP1-02)',
        ctx
      )
    ),
});
export type SetOpeningStockInput = z.infer<typeof SetOpeningStockInputSchema>;

/**
 * Query & List Envelopes.
 */
const Erp1PaginationQuerySchema = PaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int({ message: 'pageSize must be an integer' })
    .min(1, { message: 'pageSize must be >= 1' })
    .max(100, { message: 'pageSize cannot exceed 100' })
    .default(25),
});

export const ProductListQuerySchema = Erp1PaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  search: z.string().trim().optional(),
  categoryId: z.string().uuid().optional(),
});
export type ProductListQuery = z.infer<typeof ProductListQuerySchema>;

export const CategoryListQuerySchema = Erp1PaginationQuerySchema;
export type CategoryListQuery = z.infer<typeof CategoryListQuerySchema>;

export const PaginatedProductsResponseSchema = createPaginatedResponseSchema(ProductSchema);
export type PaginatedProductsResponse = z.infer<typeof PaginatedProductsResponseSchema>;

export const PaginatedCategoriesResponseSchema = createPaginatedResponseSchema(CategorySchema);
export type PaginatedCategoriesResponse = z.infer<typeof PaginatedCategoriesResponseSchema>;

export { ErrorEnvelopeSchema };

/**
 * ERP-2 Vendors & Purchasing — Public API Contract Types
 *
 * Single source of truth for:
 *   - Request/response validation (Fastify + Zod)
 *   - OpenAPI spec generation & documentation
 *   - TypeScript type inference across API, frontend, and tests
 *
 * Module API base: /api/v1/erp-vendors
 *
 * Traceability:
 *   - SDS ClearShift ERP v2.0 §4.3 (FR-ERP2-01..06, BR-ERP2-01), §4.7, §4.8
 *   - Project Plan §7.7
 *
 * @module contracts/erp-2
 */

import { z } from 'zod';
import { CurrencyCode, UuidSchema } from './erp-4.js';
import {
  ImportReceiptRecordSchema,
  ImportRowRequestSchema,
  ImportRowResponseSchema,
  TargetAdapterErrorSchema,
} from './import-target.js';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Shared Primitives
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Monetary amount: string-encoded decimal with up to 2 decimal places. */
export const MoneyAmount2Dp = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/, 'Amount must be a non-negative decimal with up to 2 decimal places');

/** Date format YYYY-MM-DD. */
export const IsoDateString = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, 'Must be a date in YYYY-MM-DD format');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Enum Schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Purchase order lifecycle status.
 * FR-ERP2-04: Draft / Sent / Received / Closed.
 * Allowed transitions are strictly unidirectional: Draft -> Sent -> Received -> Closed.
 */
export const PurchaseOrderStatusEnum = z.enum([
  'Draft',
  'Sent',
  'Received',
  'Closed',
]);
export type PurchaseOrderStatus = z.infer<typeof PurchaseOrderStatusEnum>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Vendor Schemas
// GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors
// POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors
// PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/{id}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Response shape for a single Vendor record. */
export const VendorSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
  tax_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  email: z.string().email(),
  phone: z.string().max(32).nullable(),
  address_line: z.string().max(255).nullable(),
  city: z.string().max(100).nullable(),
  country: z.string().max(64).nullable(),
  bank_masked: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^XXXX-XXXX-\d{4}$/, 'Masked bank format must match XXXX-XXXX-7890')
    .nullable(),
  currency: CurrencyCode.default('USD'),
  source_system: z.string().max(64).nullable(),
  source_id: z.string().max(128).nullable(),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
  updated_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type Vendor = z.infer<typeof VendorSchema>;

/** POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors — request body. */
export const CreateVendorRequestSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, 'Name is required').max(255),
  tax_number: z.string().min(1, 'Tax number is required').max(64),
  email: z.string().email('Valid email is required'),
  phone: z.string().max(32).optional().nullable(),
  address_line: z.string().max(255).optional().nullable(),
  city: z.string().max(100).optional().nullable(),
  country: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  bank_details: z.string().min(4).max(64).optional().nullable(),
  currency: CurrencyCode.default('USD'),
  source_system: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  source_id: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  idempotency_key: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  batch_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
  source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional().nullable(),
});
export type CreateVendorRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateVendorRequestSchema>;

/** PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors/{id} — request body. */
export const UpdateVendorRequestSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255).optional(),
  tax_number: z.string().min(1).max(64).optional(),
  email: z.string().email().optional(),
  phone: z.string().max(32).optional().nullable(),
  address_line: z.string().max(255).optional().nullable(),
  city: z.string().max(100).optional().nullable(),
  country: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  bank_details: z.string().min(4).max(64).optional().nullable(),
  currency: CurrencyCode.optional(),
});
export type UpdateVendorRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateVendorRequestSchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/vendors — query params with search and bounded pagination. */
export const VendorListQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, 'page must be >= 1').default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, 'pageSize must be >= 1').max(100, 'pageSize <= 100').default(20),
  search: z.string().max(100).optional(),
});
export type VendorListQuery = z.infer<typeof VendorListQuerySchema>;

/** Paginated vendor list response. */
export const PaginatedVendorsResponseSchema = z.object({
  items: z.array(VendorSchema),
  total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().positive(),
  pageSize: z.number().int().positive(),
  totalPages: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
});
export type PaginatedVendorsResponse = z.infer<typeof PaginatedVendorsResponseSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Purchase Order Line Schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Single purchase order line response. */
export const PurchaseOrderLineSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  po_id: UuidSchema,
  product_id: UuidSchema,
  quantity: MoneyAmount2Dp,
  price: MoneyAmount2Dp,
  line_total: MoneyAmount2Dp,
});
export type PurchaseOrderLine = z.infer<typeof PurchaseOrderLineSchema>;

/** One line in CreatePurchaseOrderRequest. */
export const CreatePurchaseOrderLineRequestSchema = z.object({
  product_id: UuidSchema,
  quantity: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .pipe(z.coerce.string())
    .refine((v) => /^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(v) && Number(v) > 0, {
      message: 'Quantity must be a positive decimal with up to 2 decimal places',
    }),
  price: z
    .union([z.string(), z.number()])
    .pipe(z.coerce.string())
    .refine((v) => /^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(v) && Number(v) >= 0, {
      message: 'Price must be a non-negative decimal with up to 2 decimal places',
    }),
});
export type CreatePurchaseOrderLineRequest = z.infer<typeof CreatePurchaseOrderLineRequestSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Purchase Order Schemas
// GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders
// GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/{id}
// POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders
// PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/{id}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Response shape for a Purchase Order with lines. */
export const PurchaseOrderSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  po_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  vendor_id: UuidSchema,
  order_date: IsoDateString,
  status: PurchaseOrderStatusEnum,
  currency: CurrencyCode,
  total: MoneyAmount2Dp,
  lines: z.array(PurchaseOrderLineSchema),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
  updated_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type PurchaseOrder = z.infer<typeof PurchaseOrderSchema>;

/** POST /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders — request body. */
export const CreatePurchaseOrderRequestSchema = z.object({
  po_number: z.string().min(1).max(64).optional(),
  vendor_id: UuidSchema,
  order_date: IsoDateString,
  currency: CurrencyCode.default('USD'),
  lines: z
    .array(CreatePurchaseOrderLineRequestSchema)
    .min(1, 'A purchase order must contain at least one line'),
  idempotency_key: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  batch_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
  source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional().nullable(),
});
export type CreatePurchaseOrderRequest = z.infer<typeof CreatePurchaseOrderRequestSchema>;

/** PATCH /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders/{id} — request body (allowed status transition only). */
export const UpdatePurchaseOrderStatusRequestSchema = z.object({
  status: PurchaseOrderStatusEnum,
});
export type UpdatePurchaseOrderStatusRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdatePurchaseOrderStatusRequestSchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-vendors/purchase-orders — query params. */
export const PurchaseOrderListQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
  vendor_id: UuidSchema.optional(),
  status: PurchaseOrderStatusEnum.optional(),
});
export type PurchaseOrderListQuery = z.infer<typeof PurchaseOrderListQuerySchema>;

/** Paginated purchase order list response. */
export const PaginatedPurchaseOrdersResponseSchema = z.object({
  items: z.array(PurchaseOrderSchema),
  total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().positive(),
  pageSize: z.number().int().positive(),
  totalPages: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
});
export type PaginatedPurchaseOrdersResponse = z.infer<typeof PaginatedPurchaseOrdersResponseSchema>;
=== packages/contracts/src/erp-4-events.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — Domain Event Contracts
 *
 * Strongly-typed domain event schemas conforming to ClearShift Transactional
 * Event Envelope Schema (PLT-04 / PLT-09).
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - Account lifecycle events (created, updated)
 *   - Journal entry lifecycle events (created, posted, reversed, voided)
 *   - Accounting period status transitions (open -> soft_closed -> locked)
 *   - Opening balance loads
 *   - Cross-module inbound event payload definitions (ERP-2, ERP-3, ERP-5)
 *
 * @module contracts/erp-4-events
 */

import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  CurrencyCode,
  MoneyAmount,
  PeriodLabel,
  AccountTypeEnum,
  JournalEntryStatusEnum,
  PeriodStatusEnum,
  UuidSchema,
} from './erp-4.js';
import { createEventEnvelopeSchema, createEventEnvelope, type EventEnvelope } from './events.js';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Event Type Constants
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const ERP4_EVENT_TYPES = {
  ACCOUNT_CREATED: 'erp.account.created',
  ACCOUNT_UPDATED: 'erp.account.updated',
  JOURNAL_ENTRY_CREATED: 'erp.journal_entry.created',
  JOURNAL_ENTRY_POSTED: 'erp.journal_entry.posted',
  JOURNAL_ENTRY_REVERSED: 'erp.journal_entry.reversed',
  JOURNAL_ENTRY_VOIDED: 'erp.journal_entry.voided',
  PERIOD_STATUS_CHANGED: 'erp.period.status_changed',
  OPENING_BALANCE_CREATED: 'erp.opening_balance.created',
  // Inbound cross-module event types consumed or projected by ERP-4
  INBOUND_VENDOR_BILL_POSTED: 'erp.vendor_bill.posted',
  INBOUND_INVOICE_POSTED: 'erp.invoice.posted',
  INBOUND_PAYROLL_TOTAL_POSTED: 'erp.payroll_total.posted',
} as const;

export type Erp4EventType = (typeof ERP4_EVENT_TYPES)[keyof typeof ERP4_EVENT_TYPES];

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Event Payload Schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const AccountCreatedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  code: z.string().min(1).max(32),
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
  type: AccountTypeEnum,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  is_posting_account: z.boolean(),
  is_active: z.boolean(),
  parent_id: UuidSchema.nullable(),
});
export type AccountCreatedPayload = z.infer<typeof AccountCreatedPayloadSchema>;

export const AccountUpdatedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  code: z.string().min(1).max(32),
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
  type: AccountTypeEnum,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  is_active: z.boolean(),
  is_posting_account: z.boolean(),
  parent_id: UuidSchema.nullable(),
});
export type AccountUpdatedPayload = z.infer<typeof AccountUpdatedPayloadSchema>;

export const JournalEntryCreatedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  period_id: UuidSchema,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
  status: z.literal('draft'),
  description: z.string().min(1),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  line_count: z.number().int().min(2),
  total_debit: MoneyAmount,
  total_credit: MoneyAmount,
});
export type JournalEntryCreatedPayload = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryCreatedPayloadSchema>;

export const JournalEntryPostedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  period_id: UuidSchema,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
  status: z.literal('posted'),
  description: z.string().min(1),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  posted_at: z.string().datetime(),
  posted_by_user_id: UuidSchema,
  total_amount: MoneyAmount,
  line_count: z.number().int().min(2),
});
export type JournalEntryPostedPayload = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryPostedPayloadSchema>;

export const JournalEntryReversedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  original_entry_id: UuidSchema,
  original_entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  reversal_entry_id: UuidSchema,
  reversal_entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  reversal_date: z.string().date(),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  total_amount: MoneyAmount,
  reason: z.string().optional(),
});
export type JournalEntryReversedPayload = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryReversedPayloadSchema>;

export const JournalEntryVoidedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  voided_at: z.string().datetime(),
  voided_by_user_id: UuidSchema.optional(),
});
export type JournalEntryVoidedPayload = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryVoidedPayloadSchema>;

export const PeriodStatusChangedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  period_label: PeriodLabel,
  previous_status: PeriodStatusEnum,
  new_status: PeriodStatusEnum,
  fiscal_year: z.string().min(1).max(9),
  changed_at: z.string().datetime(),
  changed_by_user_id: UuidSchema,
});
export type PeriodStatusChangedPayload = z.infer<typeof PeriodStatusChangedPayloadSchema>;

export const OpeningBalanceCreatedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  account_id: UuidSchema,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  balance_amount: z.string().regex(/^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/),
  as_of_date: z.string().date(),
  source_reference: z.string().nullable().optional(),
});
export type OpeningBalanceCreatedPayload = z.infer<typeof OpeningBalanceCreatedPayloadSchema>;

// Cross-Module Inbound Payloads
export const InboundVendorBillPostedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  bill_id: UuidSchema,
  vendor_id: UuidSchema,
  bill_number: z.string().min(1),
  amount: MoneyAmount,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
  expense_account_id: UuidSchema,
  liability_account_id: UuidSchema,
});
export type InboundVendorBillPostedPayload = z.infer<typeof InboundVendorBillPostedPayloadSchema>;

export const InboundInvoicePostedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  invoice_id: UuidSchema,
  customer_id: UuidSchema,
  invoice_number: z.string().min(1),
  amount: MoneyAmount,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
  receivable_account_id: UuidSchema,
  revenue_account_id: UuidSchema,
});
export type InboundInvoicePostedPayload = z.infer<typeof InboundInvoicePostedPayloadSchema>;

export const InboundPayrollTotalPostedPayloadSchema = z.object({
  payroll_total_id: UuidSchema,
  department_id: UuidSchema,
  month: PeriodLabel,
  total_amount: MoneyAmount,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
});
export type InboundPayrollTotalPostedPayload = z.infer<typeof InboundPayrollTotalPostedPayloadSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Event Envelope Schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const AccountCreatedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(AccountCreatedPayloadSchema);
export const AccountUpdatedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(AccountUpdatedPayloadSchema);
export const JournalEntryCreatedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(JournalEntryCreatedPayloadSchema);
export const JournalEntryPostedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(JournalEntryPostedPayloadSchema);
export const JournalEntryReversedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(JournalEntryReversedPayloadSchema);
export const JournalEntryVoidedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(JournalEntryVoidedPayloadSchema);
export const PeriodStatusChangedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(PeriodStatusChangedPayloadSchema);
export const OpeningBalanceCreatedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(OpeningBalanceCreatedPayloadSchema);

// Inbound envelopes
export const InboundVendorBillPostedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(InboundVendorBillPostedPayloadSchema);
export const InboundInvoicePostedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(InboundInvoicePostedPayloadSchema);
export const InboundPayrollTotalPostedEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(InboundPayrollTotalPostedPayloadSchema);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Event Helper Factory Functions
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createErp4Event<T>(params: {
  event_id?: string | undefined;
  event_type: Erp4EventType;
  schema_version?: string | undefined;
  occurred_at?: string | undefined;
  correlation_id: string;
  entity_type: 'account' | 'journal_entry' | 'accounting_period' | 'opening_balance' | 'bill' | 'invoice' | 'payroll_total';
  entity_id: string;
  batch_id?: string | undefined;
  payload: T;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
}): EventEnvelope {
  const envelopeParams: Parameters<typeof createEventEnvelope>[0] = {
    event_id: params.event_id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
    event_type: params.event_type,
    correlation_id: params.correlation_id,
    producer: 'erp-4',
    entity_type: params.entity_type,
    entity_id: params.entity_id,
    payload: params.payload,
  };
  if (params.schema_version !== undefined) {
    envelopeParams.schema_version = params.schema_version;
  }
  if (params.occurred_at !== undefined) {
    envelopeParams.occurred_at = params.occurred_at;
  }
  if (params.batch_id !== undefined && params.batch_id !== null) {
    envelopeParams.batch_id = params.batch_id;
  }
  if (params.metadata !== undefined) {
    envelopeParams.metadata = params.metadata;
  }
  return createEventEnvelope(envelopeParams);
}

 *   - PLT-04 / PLT-09: Bounded Pagination & Transactional Event Envelopes
