      closed_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      created_at: '2026-02-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      updated_at: '2026-03-10T09:00:00.000Z',
    },
  ];
  for (const per of seedPeriods) {
    periods.set(per.id, per);
  }

  // 3. Opening Balances (Synthetic Seed)
  const seedOpeningBalances: OpeningBalance[] = [
    {
      id: '33333333-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002', // BANK
      currency_code: 'INR',
      balance_amount: '250000.0000',
      as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
      source_reference: 'MIGRATION-RUN-001',
      loaded_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      created_at: '2026-04-01T00:00:00.000Z',
    },
  ];
  for (const ob of seedOpeningBalances) {
    openingBalances.set(ob.id, ob);
  }

  // 4. Balanced Journal Entries (Synthetic Seed: 1 Posted, 1 Draft)
  const seedJournals: JournalEntry[] = [
    {
      id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      entry_number: 'JV-2026-0001',
      period_id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posting_date: '2026-04-10',
      status: 'posted',
      description: 'Synthetic: Customer payment for consulting invoice',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      source_reference: 'ERP3-INV-2026-001',
      idempotency_key: 'seed-post-001',
      created_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_at: '2026-04-10T11:00:00.000Z',
      reversed_by_entry_id: null,
      lines: [
        {
          id: '55555555-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
          journal_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002', // BANK (Debit)
          debit_amount: '75000.0000',
          credit_amount: '0.0000',
          line_narration: 'Payment received in operating account',
          department_ref: 'DEP-SALES',
          created_at: '2026-04-10T10:30:00.000Z',
        },
        {
          id: '55555555-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
          journal_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000005', // SALES (Credit)
          debit_amount: '0.0000',
          credit_amount: '75000.0000',
          line_narration: 'Revenue recognised on services delivered',
          department_ref: 'DEP-SALES',
          created_at: '2026-04-10T10:30:00.000Z',
        },
      ],
      created_at: '2026-04-10T10:30:00.000Z',
      updated_at: '2026-04-10T11:00:00.000Z',
    },
    {
      id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
      entry_number: 'JV-2026-0002',
      period_id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posting_date: '2026-04-15',
      status: 'draft',
      description: 'Synthetic: Pending office stationery purchase',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      source_reference: 'ERP2-PO-2026-088',
      idempotency_key: 'seed-draft-002',
      created_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_by_user_id: null,
      posted_at: null,
      reversed_by_entry_id: null,
      lines: [
        {
          id: '55555555-0001-4000-8000-000000000003',
          journal_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000007', // SUPPLIES (Debit)
          debit_amount: '12500.0000',
          credit_amount: '0.0000',
          line_narration: 'Stationery and supplies voucher',
          department_ref: 'DEP-ADMIN',
          created_at: '2026-04-15T09:00:00.000Z',
        },
        {
          id: '55555555-0001-4000-8000-000000000004',
          journal_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000004', // AP (Credit)
          debit_amount: '0.0000',
          credit_amount: '12500.0000',
          line_narration: 'Vendor liability pending payment',
          department_ref: 'DEP-ADMIN',
          created_at: '2026-04-15T09:00:00.000Z',
        },
      ],
      created_at: '2026-04-15T09:00:00.000Z',
      updated_at: '2026-04-15T09:00:00.000Z',
    },
  ];
  for (const je of seedJournals) {
    journalEntries.set(je.id, je);
  }

  return {
    accounts,
    periods,
    journalEntries,
    openingBalances,
    emittedEvents: [],
  };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Fastify Deterministic Mock Plugin
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface Erp4MockPluginOptions {
  store?: Erp4MockStore;
  authRequired?: boolean;
}

export const erp4MockRoutes: FastifyPluginAsync<Erp4MockPluginOptions> = async (
  fastifyInstance,
  opts
) => {
  const store = opts.store ?? createInitialErp4MockData();
  const authRequired = opts.authRequired ?? false;

  // ── Error Envelope Handler (ADR-002) ───────────────────────────────────────
  fastifyInstance.setErrorHandler((error, request, reply) => {
    const correlationId =
      (request.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string | undefined) ??
      (request.headers['x-request-id'] as string | undefined) ??
      randomUUID();
    reply.header('x-correlation-id', correlationId);

    if (error instanceof ZodError) {
      return reply.status(400).send({
        statusCode: 400,
        error: 'Bad Request',
        code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
        message: error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'Contract validation failed',
        details: error.issues.map((i) => ({
          field: i.path.join('.'),
          message: i.message,
          code: i.code,
        })),
        correlation_id: correlationId,
      });
    }

    const err = (error && typeof error === 'object' ? error : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const statusCode = typeof err['statusCode'] === 'number' ? err['statusCode'] : 500;
    const code = typeof err['code'] === 'string' ? err['code'] : 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR';
    const message = typeof err['message'] === 'string' ? err['message'] : 'Internal mock error';

    return reply.status(statusCode).send({
      statusCode,
      error: statusCode === 400 ? 'Bad Request' : statusCode === 404 ? 'Not Found' : statusCode === 409 ? 'Conflict' : statusCode === 422 ? 'Unprocessable Entity' : 'Internal Server Error',
      code,
      message,
      details: Array.isArray(err['details']) ? err['details'] : [],
      correlation_id: correlationId,
    });
  });

  // ── Authentication & RBAC Guard Hook ───────────────────────────────────────
  fastifyInstance.addHook('onRequest', async (request, reply) => {
    const authHeader = request.headers.authorization;
    if (authRequired && !authHeader) {
      return reply.status(401).send({
        statusCode: 401,
        error: 'Unauthorized',
        code: 'UNAUTHORIZED',
        message: 'Missing Authorization header. Bearer token required.',
        details: [],
      });
    }

    if (authHeader) {
      if (!authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return reply.status(401).send({
          statusCode: 401,
          error: 'Unauthorized',
          code: 'UNAUTHORIZED',
          message: 'Invalid Authorization header format. Expected "Bearer <token>".',
          details: [],
        });
      }
      const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
      if (!token || token === 'invalid' || token === 'invalid-token' || token === 'expired') {
        return reply.status(401).send({
          statusCode: 401,
          error: 'Unauthorized',
          code: 'UNAUTHORIZED',
          message: 'Invalid or expired bearer token.',
          details: [],
        });
      }
    }
  });

  // ── 1. ACCOUNTS ENDPOINTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  // GET /accounts
  fastifyInstance.get('/accounts', async (req, reply) => {
    const query = AccountListQuerySchema.parse(req.query);
    let items = Array.from(store.accounts.values());

    if (query.type) {
      items = items.filter((a) => a.type === query.type);
    }
    if (query.is_active !== undefined) {
      items = items.filter((a) => a.is_active === query.is_active);
    }
    if (query.currency_code) {
      items = items.filter((a) => a.currency_code === query.currency_code);
    }
    if (query.search) {
      const s = query.search.toLowerCase();
      items = items.filter((a) => a.code.toLowerCase().includes(s) || a.name.toLowerCase().includes(s));
    }

    // Stable sort by code
    items.sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code));

    const total = items.length;
    const page = query.page;
    const pageSize = query.pageSize;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
    const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
    const paged = items.slice(start, start + pageSize);

    const response = {
      data: paged,
      total,
      page,
      pageSize,
      totalPages,
    };
    return reply.status(200).send(AccountListResponseSchema.parse(response));
  });

  // POST /accounts
  fastifyInstance.post('/accounts', async (req, reply) => {
    const body = CreateAccountRequestSchema.parse(req.body);

    // Natural Key duplicate check (UNIQUE code)
    const existingCode = Array.from(store.accounts.values()).find((a) => a.code === body.code);
    if (existingCode) {
      return reply.status(409).send({
        statusCode: 409,
        error: 'Conflict',
        code: 'ACCOUNT_CODE_EXISTS',
        message: `Account with code "${body.code}" already exists`,
        details: [{ field: 'code', code: 'DUPLICATE_CODE' }],
      });
    }

    // Parent account verification
    if (body.parent_id) {
      const parent = store.accounts.get(body.parent_id);
      if (!parent) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PARENT_NOT_FOUND,
          message: `Parent account "${body.parent_id}" not found`,
          details: [{ field: 'parent_id', code: 'PARENT_NOT_FOUND' }],
        });
      }
      if (parent.currency_code !== (body.currency_code ?? 'INR')) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.CURRENCY_MISMATCH,
          message: 'Parent and child account currencies must match',
          details: [{ field: 'currency_code', code: 'CURRENCY_MISMATCH' }],
        });
      }
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const newAccount: Account = {
      id: randomUUID(),
      code: body.code,
      name: body.name,
      type: body.type,
      currency_code: body.currency_code ?? 'INR',
      parent_id: body.parent_id ?? null,
      description: body.description ?? null,
      is_active: true,
      is_posting_account: body.is_posting_account ?? true,
      created_at: now,
      updated_at: now,
    };

    store.accounts.set(newAccount.id, newAccount);

    // Emit AccountCreated event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.ACCOUNT_CREATED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'account',
      entity_id: newAccount.id,
      batch_id: body.batch_id ?? undefined,
      payload: {
        id: newAccount.id,
        code: newAccount.code,
        name: newAccount.name,
        type: newAccount.type,
        currency_code: newAccount.currency_code,
        is_posting_account: newAccount.is_posting_account,
        is_active: newAccount.is_active,
        parent_id: newAccount.parent_id,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(201).send(AccountSchema.parse(newAccount));
  });

  // GET /accounts/:id
  fastifyInstance.get('/accounts/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const account = store.accounts.get(id);
    if (!account) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Account with ID "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }
    return reply.status(200).send(AccountSchema.parse(account));
  });

  // PATCH /accounts/:id
  fastifyInstance.patch('/accounts/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const body = UpdateAccountRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
    const account = store.accounts.get(id);
    if (!account) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Account with ID "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    if (body.parent_id) {
      if (body.parent_id === id) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.CYCLE_DETECTED,
          message: 'An account cannot be its own parent',
        });
      }
      const parent = store.accounts.get(body.parent_id);
      if (!parent) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PARENT_NOT_FOUND,
          message: `Parent account "${body.parent_id}" not found`,
        });
      }
    }

    const updatedAccount: Account = {
      ...account,
      ...(body.name !== undefined ? { name: body.name } : {}),
      ...(body.description !== undefined ? { description: body.description } : {}),
      ...(body.is_active !== undefined ? { is_active: body.is_active } : {}),
      ...(body.is_posting_account !== undefined ? { is_posting_account: body.is_posting_account } : {}),
      ...(body.parent_id !== undefined ? { parent_id: body.parent_id } : {}),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    store.accounts.set(id, updatedAccount);

    // Emit AccountUpdated event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.ACCOUNT_UPDATED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'account',
      entity_id: updatedAccount.id,
      payload: {
        id: updatedAccount.id,
        code: updatedAccount.code,
        name: updatedAccount.name,
        type: updatedAccount.type,
        currency_code: updatedAccount.currency_code,
        is_active: updatedAccount.is_active,
        is_posting_account: updatedAccount.is_posting_account,
        parent_id: updatedAccount.parent_id,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(200).send(AccountSchema.parse(updatedAccount));
  });

  // ── 2. JOURNAL ENTRIES ENDPOINTS ───────────────────────────────────────────

  // GET /journal-entries
  fastifyInstance.get('/journal-entries', async (req, reply) => {
    const query = JournalEntryListQuerySchema.parse(req.query);
    let items = Array.from(store.journalEntries.values());

    if (query.period) {
      const per = Array.from(store.periods.values()).find((p) => p.period_label === query.period);
      if (per) {
        items = items.filter((j) => j.period_id === per.id);
      } else {
        items = [];
      }
    }
    if (query.period_id) {
      items = items.filter((j) => j.period_id === query.period_id);
    }
    if (query.status) {
      items = items.filter((j) => j.status === query.status);
    }
    if (query.from_date) {
      items = items.filter((j) => j.posting_date >= query.from_date!);
    }
    if (query.to_date) {
      items = items.filter((j) => j.posting_date <= query.to_date!);
    }
    if (query.currency_code) {
      items = items.filter((j) => j.currency_code === query.currency_code);
    }
    if (query.search) {
      const s = query.search.toLowerCase();
      items = items.filter((j) => j.entry_number.toLowerCase().includes(s) || j.description.toLowerCase().includes(s));
    }

    // Stable sort by posting_date desc, created_at desc
    items.sort((a, b) => b.posting_date.localeCompare(a.posting_date) || b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));

    const total = items.length;
    const page = query.page;
    const pageSize = query.pageSize;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
    const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
    const paged = items.slice(start, start + pageSize);

    const response = {
      data: paged,
      total,
      page,
      pageSize,
      limit: pageSize,
      totalPages,
    };
    return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntryListResponseSchema.parse(response));
  });

  // POST /journal-entries (Create Draft)
  fastifyInstance.post('/journal-entries', async (req, reply) => {
    const body = CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema.parse(req.body);

    // Idempotent duplicate check
    if (body.idempotency_key) {
      const existing = Array.from(store.journalEntries.values()).find((j) => j.idempotency_key === body.idempotency_key);
      if (existing) {
        return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(existing));
      }
    }

    // Verify accounting period exists and is not locked
    const period = store.periods.get(body.period_id);
    if (!period) {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PERIOD_NOT_FOUND,
        message: `Accounting period "${body.period_id}" was not found`,
      });
    }
    if (period.status === 'locked') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PERIOD_LOCKED,
        message: `Fiscal period "${period.period_label}" is locked against postings`,
      });
    }

    // Validate account lines against existing chart of accounts
    for (const line of body.lines) {
      const acc = store.accounts.get(line.account_id);
      if (!acc) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND,
          message: `Account "${line.account_id}" does not exist`,
        });
      }
      if (!acc.is_active) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.ACCOUNT_INACTIVE,
          message: `Account "${acc.code}" is inactive and rejects postings`,
        });
      }
      if (!acc.is_posting_account) {
        return reply.status(422).send({
          statusCode: 422,
          error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
          code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.CANNOT_POST_TO_HEADER_ACCOUNT,
          message: `Account "${acc.code}" is a header account; only leaf posting accounts accept entries`,
        });
      }
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const journalId = randomUUID();
    const entrySeq = store.journalEntries.size + 1;
    const entryNumber = `JV-2026-${String(entrySeq).padStart(4, '0')}`;

    const lines = body.lines.map((l) => ({
      id: randomUUID(),
      journal_entry_id: journalId,
      account_id: l.account_id,
      debit_amount: l.debit_amount,
      credit_amount: l.credit_amount,
      line_narration: l.line_narration ?? null,
      department_ref: l.department_ref ?? null,
      created_at: now,
    }));

    const newEntry: JournalEntry = {
      id: journalId,
      entry_number: entryNumber,
      period_id: body.period_id,
      posting_date: body.posting_date,
      status: 'draft',
      description: body.description,
      currency_code: body.currency_code,
      source_reference: body.source_reference ?? null,
      idempotency_key: body.idempotency_key ?? null,
      created_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_by_user_id: null,
      posted_at: null,
      reversed_by_entry_id: null,
      lines,
      created_at: now,
      updated_at: now,
    };

    store.journalEntries.set(newEntry.id, newEntry);

    // Calculate total debits
    const totalDebit = lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + parseFloat(l.debit_amount), 0).toFixed(4);

    // Emit JournalEntryCreated event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_CREATED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: newEntry.id,
      batch_id: body.batch_id ?? undefined,
      payload: {
        id: newEntry.id,
        entry_number: newEntry.entry_number,
        period_id: newEntry.period_id,
        posting_date: newEntry.posting_date,
        status: 'draft',
        description: newEntry.description,
        currency_code: newEntry.currency_code,
        line_count: lines.length,
        total_debit: totalDebit,
        total_credit: totalDebit,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(201).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(newEntry));
  });

  // GET /journal-entries/:id
  fastifyInstance.get('/journal-entries/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const entry = store.journalEntries.get(id);
    if (!entry) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Journal entry "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }
    return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(entry));
  });

  // PATCH /journal-entries/:id (Update Draft)
  fastifyInstance.patch('/journal-entries/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const body = UpdateJournalEntryRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
    const entry = store.journalEntries.get(id);
    if (!entry) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Journal entry "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    if (entry.status !== 'draft') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.ENTRY_IMMUTABLE,
        message: `Journal entry is in "${entry.status}" status and cannot be modified. Only draft entries are mutable.`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    let updatedLines = entry.lines;
    if (body.lines) {
      updatedLines = body.lines.map((l) => ({
        id: randomUUID(),
        journal_entry_id: id,
        account_id: l.account_id,
        debit_amount: l.debit_amount,
        credit_amount: l.credit_amount,
        line_narration: l.line_narration ?? null,
        department_ref: l.department_ref ?? null,
        created_at: now,
      }));
    }

    const updatedEntry: JournalEntry = {
      ...entry,
      ...(body.posting_date !== undefined ? { posting_date: body.posting_date } : {}),
      ...(body.description !== undefined ? { description: body.description } : {}),
      ...(body.period_id !== undefined ? { period_id: body.period_id } : {}),
      lines: updatedLines,
      updated_at: now,
    };

    store.journalEntries.set(id, updatedEntry);
    return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(updatedEntry));
  });

  // POST /journal-entries/:id/post (Post Draft)
  fastifyInstance.post('/journal-entries/:id/post', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const entry = store.journalEntries.get(id);
    if (!entry) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Journal entry "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    if (entry.status === 'posted') {
      return reply.status(409).send({
        statusCode: 409,
        error: 'Conflict',
        code: 'ENTRY_ALREADY_POSTED',
        message: `Journal entry "${entry.entry_number}" is already posted`,
      });
    }

    if (entry.status !== 'draft') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION,
        message: `Cannot post entry in "${entry.status}" status`,
      });
    }

    // Verify period status
    const period = store.periods.get(entry.period_id);
    if (!period || period.status === 'locked') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PERIOD_LOCKED,
        message: `Period is locked against postings`,
      });
    }

    // Double-check balancing invariant (BR-ERP4-01)
    const totalDebit = entry.lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.debit_amount) * 10000), 0);
    const totalCredit = entry.lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.credit_amount) * 10000), 0);
    if (totalDebit !== totalCredit || totalDebit <= 0) {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: 'JOURNAL_UNBALANCED',
        message: `Journal entry debits (${totalDebit / 10000}) must equal credits (${totalCredit / 10000}) at currency precision`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const postedEntry: JournalEntry = {
      ...entry,
      status: 'posted',
      posted_at: now,
      posted_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      updated_at: now,
    };

    store.journalEntries.set(id, postedEntry);

    // Emit JournalEntryPosted event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_POSTED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: postedEntry.id,
      payload: {
        id: postedEntry.id,
        entry_number: postedEntry.entry_number,
        period_id: postedEntry.period_id,
        posting_date: postedEntry.posting_date,
        status: 'posted',
        description: postedEntry.description,
        currency_code: postedEntry.currency_code,
        posted_at: now,
        posted_by_user_id: postedEntry.posted_by_user_id!,
        total_amount: (totalDebit / 10000).toFixed(4),
        line_count: postedEntry.lines.length,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(postedEntry));
  });

  // POST /journal-entries/:id/reverse (Compensating Reversal)
  fastifyInstance.post('/journal-entries/:id/reverse', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const body = ReverseJournalEntryRequestSchema.parse(req.body ?? {});
    const entry = store.journalEntries.get(id);
    if (!entry) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Journal entry "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    if (entry.status !== 'posted') {
      return reply.status(409).send({
        statusCode: 409,
        error: 'Conflict',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.NOT_REVERSIBLE,
        message: `Only posted entries can be reversed. Current status: "${entry.status}"`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const reversalId = randomUUID();
    const reversalEntryNumber = `REV-${entry.entry_number}`;

    // Swap debits and credits
    const reversalLines = entry.lines.map((l) => ({
      id: randomUUID(),
      journal_entry_id: reversalId,
      account_id: l.account_id,
      debit_amount: l.credit_amount,
      credit_amount: l.debit_amount,
      line_narration: `Reversal of line ${l.id}`,
      department_ref: l.department_ref,
      created_at: now,
    }));

    const reversalEntry: JournalEntry = {
      id: reversalId,
      entry_number: reversalEntryNumber,
      period_id: entry.period_id,
      posting_date: body.reversal_date ?? entry.posting_date,
      status: 'posted',
      description: body.reversal_description ?? `Reversal of ${entry.entry_number}: ${entry.description}`,
      currency_code: entry.currency_code,
      source_reference: `REV:${entry.entry_number}`,
      idempotency_key: `reversal-${entry.id}`,
      created_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      posted_at: now,
      reversed_by_entry_id: null,
      lines: reversalLines,
      created_at: now,
      updated_at: now,
    };

    store.journalEntries.set(reversalId, reversalEntry);

    // Update original entry to 'reversed' and link to reversal
    const updatedOriginal: JournalEntry = {
      ...entry,
      status: 'reversed',
      reversed_by_entry_id: reversalId,
      updated_at: now,
    };
    store.journalEntries.set(id, updatedOriginal);

    const totalAmount = reversalLines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + parseFloat(l.debit_amount), 0).toFixed(4);

    // Emit JournalEntryReversed event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_REVERSED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: entry.id,
      payload: {
        original_entry_id: entry.id,
        original_entry_number: entry.entry_number,
        reversal_entry_id: reversalId,
        reversal_entry_number: reversalEntryNumber,
        reversal_date: reversalEntry.posting_date,
        currency_code: entry.currency_code,
        total_amount: totalAmount,
        reason: body.reversal_description,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(201).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(reversalEntry));
  });

  // POST /journal-entries/:id/void (Void Draft)
  fastifyInstance.post('/journal-entries/:id/void', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const entry = store.journalEntries.get(id);
    if (!entry) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Journal entry "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    if (entry.status !== 'draft') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.ENTRY_IMMUTABLE,
        message: `Only draft entries can be voided. Current status: "${entry.status}"`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const voidedEntry: JournalEntry = {
      ...entry,
      status: 'voided',
      updated_at: now,
    };
    store.journalEntries.set(id, voidedEntry);

    // Emit JournalEntryVoided event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_VOIDED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: id,
      payload: {
        id,
        entry_number: entry.entry_number,
        voided_at: now,
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(200).send(JournalEntrySchema.parse(voidedEntry));
  });

  // ── 3. TRIAL BALANCE ENDPOINT ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  // GET /trial-balance?period=YYYY-MM
  fastifyInstance.get('/trial-balance', async (req, reply) => {
    const query = TrialBalanceQuerySchema.parse(req.query);
    const targetPeriod = Array.from(store.periods.values()).find((p) => p.period_label === query.period);
    if (!targetPeriod) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'PERIOD_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Fiscal period "${query.period}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    // Filter posted journal entries within the period
    const postedEntries = Array.from(store.journalEntries.values()).filter(
      (j) => j.period_id === targetPeriod.id && (j.status === 'posted' || j.status === 'reversed')
    );

    const accountTotals = new Map<string, { totalDebit: number; totalCredit: number }>();
    for (const entry of postedEntries) {
      for (const line of entry.lines) {
        const cur = accountTotals.get(line.account_id) ?? { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
        cur.totalDebit += Math.round(parseFloat(line.debit_amount) * 10000);
        cur.totalCredit += Math.round(parseFloat(line.credit_amount) * 10000);
        accountTotals.set(line.account_id, cur);
      }
    }

    let overallDebit = 0;
    let overallCredit = 0;
    const rows = Array.from(store.accounts.values())
      .filter((a) => a.is_posting_account && (!query.currency_code || a.currency_code === query.currency_code))
      .map((acc) => {
        const debCred = accountTotals.get(acc.id) ?? { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
        const debitStr = (debCred.totalDebit / 10000).toFixed(4);
        const creditStr = (debCred.totalCredit / 10000).toFixed(4);
        const net = (debCred.totalDebit - debCred.totalCredit) / 10000;

        // Opening balance for this account
        const ob = Array.from(store.openingBalances.values()).find((o) => o.account_id === acc.id);
        const obVal = ob ? parseFloat(ob.balance_amount) : 0;
        const closingVal = obVal + net;

        overallDebit += debCred.totalDebit;
        overallCredit += debCred.totalCredit;

        return {
          account_id: acc.id,
          account_code: acc.code,
          account_name: acc.name,
          account_type: acc.type,
          currency_code: acc.currency_code,
          total_debit: debitStr,
          total_credit: creditStr,
          net_balance: net.toFixed(4),
          opening_balance: obVal.toFixed(4),
          closing_balance: closingVal.toFixed(4),
        };
      });

    const diff = ((overallDebit - overallCredit) / 10000).toFixed(4);
    const trialBalanceResponse: TrialBalanceResponse = {
      period: query.period,
      generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      rows,
      totals: {
        total_debit: (overallDebit / 10000).toFixed(4),
        total_credit: (overallCredit / 10000).toFixed(4),
        difference: diff,
        is_balanced: overallDebit === overallCredit,
      },
    };

    return reply.status(200).send(TrialBalanceResponseSchema.parse(trialBalanceResponse));
  });

  // ── 4. OPENING BALANCES ENDPOINTS ──────────────────────────────────────────

  // POST /opening-balances
  fastifyInstance.post('/opening-balances', async (req, reply) => {
    const body = CreateOpeningBalancesRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
    const created: OpeningBalance[] = [];
    const skipped: Array<{ account_id: string; currency_code: string; reason: string }> = [];

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    for (const item of body.balances) {
      // Check account exists
      const acc = store.accounts.get(item.account_id);
      if (!acc) {
        skipped.push({
          account_id: item.account_id,
          currency_code: item.currency_code,
          reason: 'ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND',
        });
        continue;
      }

      // Check unique (account_id, currency_code)
      const existing = Array.from(store.openingBalances.values()).find(
        (ob) => ob.account_id === item.account_id && ob.currency_code === item.currency_code
      );
      if (existing) {
        skipped.push({
          account_id: item.account_id,
          currency_code: item.currency_code,
          reason: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.DUPLICATE_OPENING_BALANCE,
        });
        continue;
      }

      const newOb: OpeningBalance = {
        id: randomUUID(),
        account_id: item.account_id,
        currency_code: item.currency_code,
        balance_amount: item.balance_amount,
        as_of_date: item.as_of_date,
        source_reference: item.source_reference ?? null,
        loaded_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        created_at: now,
      };

      store.openingBalances.set(newOb.id, newOb);
      created.push(newOb);

      // Emit OpeningBalanceCreated event
      const event = createErp4Event({
        event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.OPENING_BALANCE_CREATED,
        correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
        entity_type: 'opening_balance',
        entity_id: newOb.id,
        batch_id: item.batch_id ?? undefined,
        payload: {
          id: newOb.id,
          account_id: newOb.account_id,
          currency_code: newOb.currency_code,
          balance_amount: newOb.balance_amount,
          as_of_date: newOb.as_of_date,
          source_reference: newOb.source_reference,
        },
      });
      store.emittedEvents.push(event);
    }

    return reply.status(201).send(
      CreateOpeningBalancesResponseSchema.parse({
        created,
        skipped,
      })
    );
  });

  // GET /opening-balances
  fastifyInstance.get('/opening-balances', async (req, reply) => {
    const query = OpeningBalanceListQuerySchema.parse(req.query);
    let items = Array.from(store.openingBalances.values());

    if (query.account_id) {
      items = items.filter((o) => o.account_id === query.account_id);
    }
    if (query.currency_code) {
      items = items.filter((o) => o.currency_code === query.currency_code);
    }

    const total = items.length;
    const page = query.page;
    const pageSize = query.pageSize;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
    const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
    const paged = items.slice(start, start + pageSize);

    return reply.status(200).send(
      OpeningBalanceListResponseSchema.parse({
        data: paged,
        total,
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages,
      })
    );
  });

  // GET /opening-balances/:id
  fastifyInstance.get('/opening-balances/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const ob = store.openingBalances.get(id);
    if (!ob) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'OPENING_BALANCE_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Opening balance "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }
    return reply.status(200).send(OpeningBalanceSchema.parse(ob));
  });

  // ── 5. PERIODS ENDPOINTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  // GET /periods
  fastifyInstance.get('/periods', async (req, reply) => {
    const query = PeriodListQuerySchema.parse(req.query);
    let items = Array.from(store.periods.values());

    if (query.status) {
      items = items.filter((p) => p.status === query.status);
    }
    if (query.fiscal_year) {
      items = items.filter((p) => p.fiscal_year === query.fiscal_year);
    }

    // Sort by start_date desc
    items.sort((a, b) => b.start_date.localeCompare(a.start_date));

    const total = items.length;
    const page = query.page;
    const pageSize = query.pageSize;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
    const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
    const paged = items.slice(start, start + pageSize);

    return reply.status(200).send(
      PeriodListResponseSchema.parse({
        data: paged,
        total,
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages,
      })
    );
  });

  // GET /periods/:id
  fastifyInstance.get('/periods/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const period = store.periods.get(id);
    if (!period) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'PERIOD_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Period "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }
    return reply.status(200).send(AccountingPeriodSchema.parse(period));
  });

  // PATCH /periods/:id (Status Transition)
  fastifyInstance.patch('/periods/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = IdParamSchema.parse(req.params);
    const body = UpdatePeriodRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
    const period = store.periods.get(id);
    if (!period) {
      return reply.status(404).send({
        statusCode: 404,
        error: 'Not Found',
        code: 'PERIOD_NOT_FOUND',
        message: `Period "${id}" was not found`,
      });
    }

    // FSM rule: locked is terminal
    if (period.status === 'locked' && body.status && body.status !== 'locked') {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        statusCode: 422,
        error: 'Unprocessable Entity',
        code: RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.INVALID_PERIOD_TRANSITION,
        message: `Locked accounting periods are permanent and cannot be reopened`,
      });
    }

    const previousStatus = period.status;
    const newStatus = body.status ?? period.status;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const updatedPeriod: AccountingPeriod = {
      ...period,
      status: newStatus,
      closed_at: newStatus === 'open' ? null : now,
      closed_by_user_id: newStatus === 'open' ? null : '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      updated_at: now,
    };
    store.periods.set(id, updatedPeriod);

    // Emit PeriodStatusChanged event
    const event = createErp4Event({
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.PERIOD_STATUS_CHANGED,
      correlation_id: (req.headers['x-correlation-id'] as string) ?? randomUUID(),
      entity_type: 'accounting_period',
      entity_id: id,
      payload: {
        id,
        period_label: updatedPeriod.period_label,
        previous_status: previousStatus,
        new_status: newStatus,
        fiscal_year: updatedPeriod.fiscal_year,
        changed_at: now,
        changed_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      },
    });
    store.emittedEvents.push(event);

    return reply.status(200).send(AccountingPeriodSchema.parse(updatedPeriod));
  });
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Standalone Server Factory for In-Process Testing and Standalone CLI
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface Erp4MockServerInstance {
  app: FastifyInstance;
  store: Erp4MockStore;
  reset: () => void;
  start: (port?: number, host?: string) => Promise<string>;
  stop: () => Promise<void>;
}

export function createErp4MockServer(options: Erp4MockPluginOptions = {}): Erp4MockServerInstance {
  const store = options.store ?? createInitialErp4MockData();
  const app = fastify({ logger: false });

  app.register(erp4MockRoutes, {
    prefix: '/api/v1/erp-finance',
    store,
    authRequired: options.authRequired ?? false,
  });

  return {
    app,
    store,
    reset: () => {
      const fresh = createInitialErp4MockData();
      store.accounts = fresh.accounts;
      store.periods = fresh.periods;
      store.journalEntries = fresh.journalEntries;
      store.openingBalances = fresh.openingBalances;
      store.emittedEvents = [];
    },
    start: async (port: number = ERP4_MOCK_DEFAULT_PORT, host: string = '0.0.0.0') => {
      await app.listen({ port, host });
      return `http://${host}:${port}/api/v1/erp-finance`;
    },
    stop: async () => {
      await app.close();
    },
  };
}
=== packages/contracts/src/erp-4.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — Public API Contract Types
 *
 * These Zod schemas are the single source of truth for:
 *   - Request/response validation (Fastify + Zod)
 *   - OpenAPI spec generation (fastify-zod or zod-openapi)
 *   - TypeScript type inference (no separate type files needed)
 *
 * Module API base: /api/v1/erp-finance
 *
 * @module contracts/erp-4
 */

import { z } from 'zod';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Shared primitives
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** ISO 4217 currency code — exactly 3 uppercase letters. */
export const CurrencyCode = z
  .string()
  .length(3)
  .regex(/^[A-Z]{3}$/, 'Must be a 3-letter ISO 4217 currency code (e.g. INR, USD)');

/** UUID v4 format. */
export const UuidSchema = z.string().uuid('Must be a valid UUID v4');

/** Monetary amount: string-encoded decimal with up to 4 decimal places. */
export const MoneyAmount = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/, 'Amount must be a non-negative decimal with up to 4 decimal places');

/** Fiscal period label: YYYY-MM format. */
export const PeriodLabel = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/, 'Period must be in YYYY-MM format');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Enum schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const AccountTypeEnum = z.enum(['asset', 'liability', 'income', 'expense']);
export type AccountType = z.infer<typeof AccountTypeEnum>;

export const JournalEntryStatusEnum = z.enum(['draft', 'posted', 'reversed', 'voided']);
export type JournalEntryStatus = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryStatusEnum>;

export const PeriodStatusEnum = z.enum(['open', 'soft_closed', 'locked']);
export type PeriodStatus = z.infer<typeof PeriodStatusEnum>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Account schemas
// GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts
// POST /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts (seed/admin/editor only)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Response shape for a single Account. */
export const AccountSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  code: z.string().min(1).max(32),
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
  type: AccountTypeEnum,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  parent_id: UuidSchema.nullable(),
  description: z.string().max(2000).nullable(),
  is_active: z.boolean(),
  is_posting_account: z.boolean(),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
  updated_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type Account = z.infer<typeof AccountSchema>;

/** POST /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts — request body. */
export const CreateAccountRequestSchema = z.object({
  code: z
    .string()
    .min(1)
    .max(32)
    .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/, 'Code must be alphanumeric with hyphens/underscores'),
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
  type: AccountTypeEnum,
  currency_code: CurrencyCode.default('INR'),
  parent_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
  description: z.string().max(2000).optional().nullable(),
  is_posting_account: z.boolean().default(true),
  idempotency_key: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  batch_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
  source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional().nullable(),
});
export type CreateAccountRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateAccountRequestSchema>;

/** PATCH /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/:id — request body. */
export const UpdateAccountRequestSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1).max(255).optional(),
  description: z.string().max(2000).optional().nullable(),
  is_active: z.boolean().optional(),
  is_posting_account: z.boolean().optional(),
  parent_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
});
export type UpdateAccountRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateAccountRequestSchema>;

/** Common URL ID parameter schema. */
export const IdParamSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
});
export type IdParam = z.infer<typeof IdParamSchema>;

/** Generic pagination query parameters with hard cap of 100. */
export const PaginationQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, 'page must be greater than or equal to 1').default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1, 'pageSize must be greater than or equal to 1')
    .max(100, 'pageSize cannot exceed 100')
    .default(20),
});
export type PaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof PaginationQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts — query params. */
export const AccountListQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, 'page must be greater than or equal to 1').default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1, 'pageSize must be greater than or equal to 1')
    .max(100, 'pageSize cannot exceed 100')
    .default(20),
  search: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
  type: AccountTypeEnum.optional(),
  is_active: z
    .union([
      z.boolean(),
      z.enum(['true', 'false']).transform((v) => v === 'true'),
    ])
    .optional(),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode.optional(),
});
export type AccountListQuery = z.infer<typeof AccountListQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts — response wrapper with bounded paging metadata. */
export const AccountListResponseSchema = z.object({
  data: z.array(AccountSchema),
  total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().positive().default(1),
  pageSize: z.number().int().positive().default(20),
  totalPages: z.number().int().nonnegative().default(1),
});
export type AccountListResponse = z.infer<typeof AccountListResponseSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Journal Entry schemas
// GET /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries
// GET /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id
// POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries (creates draft)
// POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id/post
// POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id/reverse
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Response shape for a Journal Line within an entry. */
export const JournalLineSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  journal_entry_id: UuidSchema,
  account_id: UuidSchema,
  debit_amount: z.string(),  // NUMERIC returned as string from pg
  credit_amount: z.string(),
  line_narration: z.string().max(255).nullable(),
  department_ref: z.string().max(64).nullable(),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type JournalLine = z.infer<typeof JournalLineSchema>;

/** Response shape for a Journal Entry (with embedded lines). */
export const JournalEntrySchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  entry_number: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  period_id: UuidSchema,
  posting_date: z.string().date(),
  status: JournalEntryStatusEnum,
  description: z.string().min(1),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode,
  source_reference: z.string().max(128).nullable(),
  idempotency_key: z.string().max(128).nullable(),
  created_by_user_id: UuidSchema,
  posted_by_user_id: UuidSchema.nullable(),
  posted_at: z.string().datetime().nullable(),
  reversed_by_entry_id: UuidSchema.nullable(),
  lines: z.array(JournalLineSchema),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
  updated_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type JournalEntry = z.infer<typeof JournalEntrySchema>;

/** Request shape for a single line when creating a journal entry. */
export const CreateJournalLineRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    account_id: UuidSchema,
    debit_amount: MoneyAmount.default('0.0000'),
    credit_amount: MoneyAmount.default('0.0000'),
    line_narration: z.string().max(255).optional().nullable(),
    department_ref: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  })
  .refine(
    (line) => {
      const debit = parseFloat(line.debit_amount);
      const credit = parseFloat(line.credit_amount);
      // At least one must be non-zero
      if (debit <= 0 && credit <= 0) return false;
      // Cannot be both
      if (debit > 0 && credit > 0) return false;
      return true;
    },
    {
      message:
        'Each line must have exactly one of debit_amount > 0 OR credit_amount > 0 (not both, not neither)',
    }
  );
export type CreateJournalLineRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateJournalLineRequestSchema>;

/**
 * POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries — request body.
 *
 * Application-level invariants validated here (DB enforces per-line constraints):
 *   - At least 2 lines (minimum meaningful double-entry: 1 debit + 1 credit)
 *   - All lines in same currency as header
 *   - SUM(debit) == SUM(credit) at 4 decimal place precision
 */
export const CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    period_id: UuidSchema,
    posting_date: z.string().date(),
    description: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
    currency_code: CurrencyCode,
    source_reference: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
    idempotency_key: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
    batch_id: UuidSchema.optional().nullable(),
    source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional().nullable(),
    lines: z
      .array(CreateJournalLineRequestSchema)
      .min(2, 'A journal entry must have at least 2 lines'),
  })
  .refine(
    (entry) => {
      // BR-ERP4-01: SUM(debits) must equal SUM(credits) at 4dp
      const totalDebit = entry.lines.reduce(
        (sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.debit_amount) * 10000),
        0
      );
      const totalCredit = entry.lines.reduce(
        (sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.credit_amount) * 10000),
        0
      );
      return totalDebit === totalCredit;
    },
    {
      message:
        'Journal entry is not balanced: SUM(debit_amount) must equal SUM(credit_amount) at 4 decimal precision',
      path: ['lines'],
    }
  );
export type CreateJournalEntryRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema>;

/**
 * BR-ERP4-01: Validates that total debits equal total credits.
 * Supports both frontend models ({ debit, credit }) and API/DB models ({ debit_amount, credit_amount }).
 */
export function isJournalEntryBalanced(
  lines: Array<{
    debit?: number | string;
    credit?: number | string;
    debit_amount?: number | string;
    credit_amount?: number | string;
  }>
): boolean {
  if (!lines || lines.length < 2) return false;
  const totalDebit = lines.reduce((sum, l) => {
    const val = l.debit !== undefined ? Number(l.debit) : Number(l.debit_amount ?? 0);
    return sum + Math.round(val * 10000);
  }, 0);
  const totalCredit = lines.reduce((sum, l) => {
    const val = l.credit !== undefined ? Number(l.credit) : Number(l.credit_amount ?? 0);
    return sum + Math.round(val * 10000);
  }, 0);
  return totalDebit === totalCredit;
}

/** PATCH /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id — request body (draft edits). */
export const UpdateJournalEntryRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    posting_date: z.string().date().optional(),
    description: z.string().min(1).max(2000).optional(),
    period_id: UuidSchema.optional(),
    lines: z
      .array(CreateJournalLineRequestSchema)
      .min(2, 'A journal entry must have at least 2 lines')
      .optional(),
  })
  .refine(
    (entry) => {
      if (!entry.lines) return true;
      const totalDebit = entry.lines.reduce(
        (sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.debit_amount) * 10000),
        0
      );
      const totalCredit = entry.lines.reduce(
        (sum, l) => sum + Math.round(parseFloat(l.credit_amount) * 10000),
        0
      );
      return totalDebit === totalCredit;
    },
    {
      message:
        'Journal entry is not balanced: SUM(debit_amount) must equal SUM(credit_amount) at 4 decimal precision',
      path: ['lines'],
    }
  );
export type UpdateJournalEntryRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateJournalEntryRequestSchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries — list query params with bounded pagination. */
export const JournalEntryListQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, 'page must be greater than or equal to 1').default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1, 'pageSize must be greater than or equal to 1')
    .max(100, 'pageSize cannot exceed 100')
    .default(20),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).optional(),
  search: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
  period: PeriodLabel.optional(),
  period_id: UuidSchema.optional(),
  status: JournalEntryStatusEnum.optional(),
  from_date: z.string().date().optional(),
  to_date: z.string().date().optional(),
  currency_code: CurrencyCode.optional(),
});
export type JournalEntryListQuery = z.infer<typeof JournalEntryListQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries — response wrapper. */
export const JournalEntryListResponseSchema = z.object({
  data: z.array(JournalEntrySchema),
  total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().positive(),
  pageSize: z.number().int().positive().default(20),

});

=== packages/contracts/src/erp-5.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-5 HR & Employees — Public API Contract Types
 *
 * These Zod schemas are the single source of truth for:
 *   - Request/response validation (Fastify + Zod)
 *   - OpenAPI spec generation
 *   - TypeScript type inference (no separate type files needed)
 *
 * Module API base: /api/v1/erp-hr
 *
 * Schema owner: ERP-5 (Group 10). The shared error envelope
 * (`{ code, message, details, correlation_id }`) is owned by the platform
 * package `@cs/kernel` (ErrorEnvelopeSchema) and is NOT redefined here.
 *
 * @module contracts/erp-5
 */

import { z } from 'zod';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Shared primitives (module-scoped names — other module contracts export
// `CurrencyCode`, `UuidSchema`, `MoneyAmount`, `PeriodLabel`)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** UUID primary key. */
export const HrUuid = z.string().uuid('Must be a valid UUID');

/** ISO 4217 currency code — exactly 3 uppercase letters. */
export const HrCurrencyCode = z
  .string()
  .length(3)
  .regex(/^[A-Z]{3}$/, 'Must be a 3-letter ISO 4217 currency code (e.g. INR, USD)');

/** Calendar date — YYYY-MM-DD. */
export const HrIsoDate = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, 'Date must be in YYYY-MM-DD format');

/** Payroll month — YYYY-MM. */
export const HrMonth = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/, 'Month must be in YYYY-MM format');

/** Monetary amount: string-encoded decimal, non-negative, up to 4 decimal places. */
export const HrMoneyAmount = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$/, 'Amount must be a non-negative decimal with up to 4 decimal places');

/** Declared employee identifier, e.g. "EMP-0001". */
export const HrEmployeeNo = z
  .string()
  .min(1)
  .max(32)
  .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/, 'Employee number may contain letters, digits, hyphen and underscore only');

/** Department code, e.g. "ENG". */
export const HrDepartmentCode = z
  .string()
  .min(1)
  .max(32)
  .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/, 'Department code may contain letters, digits, hyphen and underscore only');

/** Person name component. */
export const HrPersonName = z.string().min(1).max(120);

/** Job title. */
export const HrJobTitle = z.string().min(1).max(120);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Enum schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** FR-ERP5-04: leave type is sick / holiday / other. */
export const LeaveTypeEnum = z.enum(['sick', 'holiday', 'other']);
export type LeaveType = z.infer<typeof LeaveTypeEnum>;

/** Leave record status. V1 records the status; V2 adds the approval workflow. */
export const LeaveStatusEnum = z.enum(['pending', 'approved', 'rejected']);
export type LeaveStatus = z.infer<typeof LeaveStatusEnum>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Domain rule identifiers
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Stable error codes emitted by the ERP-5 domain layer. The database enforces
 * the same invariants; these codes are the API-facing representation.
 */
export const HR_DOMAIN_RULES = {
  /** BR-ERP5-01: join date must not be before birth date. */
  JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE: 'HR_JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE',
  /** Leave end date must not precede the start date. */
  LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID: 'HR_LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID',
  /** Salary must not be negative. */
  SALARY_NON_NEGATIVE: 'HR_SALARY_NON_NEGATIVE',
  /** Declared employee number must be unique. */
  EMPLOYEE_NO_DUPLICATE: 'HR_EMPLOYEE_NO_DUPLICATE',
  /** Department code must be unique. */
  DEPARTMENT_CODE_DUPLICATE: 'HR_DEPARTMENT_CODE_DUPLICATE',
  /** Referenced department must exist. */
  DEPARTMENT_REQUIRED: 'HR_DEPARTMENT_REQUIRED',
  /** Referenced employee must exist. */
  EMPLOYEE_REQUIRED: 'HR_EMPLOYEE_REQUIRED',
  /** Only one payroll aggregate per department and month. */
  PAYROLL_TOTAL_DUPLICATE: 'HR_PAYROLL_TOTAL_DUPLICATE',
  /** BR-ERP5-02: salary is never returned in full. */
  SALARY_MASKED: 'HR_SALARY_MASKED',
  /** Source identity `(source_system, source_id)` must be unique. */
  SOURCE_IDENTITY_DUPLICATE: 'HR_SOURCE_IDENTITY_DUPLICATE',
  /** A department can never be its own parent. */
  DEPARTMENT_SELF_PARENT: 'HR_DEPARTMENT_SELF_PARENT',
  /** The department hierarchy must stay acyclic. */
  DEPARTMENT_CYCLE: 'HR_DEPARTMENT_CYCLE',
  /** A protected / system-maintained field was sent by a client. */
  PROTECTED_FIELD: 'HR_PROTECTED_FIELD',
  /** Import provenance is incomplete (batch_id / source_row / idempotency key). */
  IMPORT_PROVENANCE_REQUIRED: 'HR_IMPORT_PROVENANCE_REQUIRED',
  /** The idempotency key was already used for a different payload. */
  IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT: 'HR_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT',
  /** HR stores no credentials and grants no application roles. */
  CREDENTIALS_FORBIDDEN: 'HR_CREDENTIALS_FORBIDDEN',
  /** A payroll aggregate carries no employee-level rows. */
  PAYROLL_AGGREGATE_ONLY: 'HR_PAYROLL_AGGREGATE_ONLY',
  /** The leave status transition is not allowed (ERP5-06 state matrix). */
  INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITION: 'HR_INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITION',
  /** The leave type is outside the approved enumeration. */
  INVALID_LEAVE_TYPE: 'HR_INVALID_LEAVE_TYPE',
  /** The leave status is outside the approved enumeration. */
  INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS: 'HR_INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS',
  /** Exactly one of `source_system` / `source_id` was supplied. */
  SOURCE_IDENTITY_INCOMPLETE: 'HR_SOURCE_IDENTITY_INCOMPLETE',
  /** A referenced record does not exist in the owning entity. */
  INVALID_REFERENCE: 'HR_INVALID_REFERENCE',
  /** A required relationship is missing. */
  MISSING_REQUIRED_REFERENCE: 'HR_MISSING_REQUIRED_REFERENCE',
  /** The persisted row does not satisfy the read model of its entity. */
  READ_MODEL_INCONSISTENT: 'HR_READ_MODEL_INCONSISTENT',
} as const;

export type HrDomainRule = (typeof HR_DOMAIN_RULES)[keyof typeof HR_DOMAIN_RULES];

/**
 * Domain rules whose violation is an **invalid business value**.
 *
 * A request that breaks one of these rules is answered with
 * `422 Unprocessable Entity` and the stable code below. Structural problems
 * (malformed UUID, missing field, unknown key, bad page size) stay
 * `400 Bad Request`.
 */
export const HR_UNPROCESSABLE_CODES: readonly string[] = [
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.SALARY_NON_NEGATIVE,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.DEPARTMENT_SELF_PARENT,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.DEPARTMENT_CYCLE,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITION,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.INVALID_LEAVE_TYPE,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.INVALID_LEAVE_STATUS,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INCOMPLETE,
  HR_DOMAIN_RULES.READ_MODEL_INCONSISTENT,
];

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Relationship rules (ERP5-06)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * An **absent optional relationship** is reported as an approved warning.
 *
 * The module never invents a record to satisfy an optional link: the warning
 * says so explicitly. The code is module-scoped so it can never collide with
 * another module's warning code.
 */
export const HR_OPTIONAL_REFERENCE_ABSENT = 'HR_OPTIONAL_REFERENCE_ABSENT';

export const HrRelationshipWarningSchema = z.object({
  /** The relationship that is absent, e.g. `DEPARTMENT_PARENT`. */
  relationship: z.string(),
  /** The field the relationship hangs on, e.g. `parent_id`. */
  field: z.string(),
  code: z.literal(HR_OPTIONAL_REFERENCE_ABSENT),
  message: z.string(),
});
export type HrRelationshipWarning = z.infer<typeof HrRelationshipWarningSchema>;

/**
 * The V1 leave state matrix (ERP5-06).
 *
 * A leave record is created in `pending` status by default and may be
 * decided once. `approved` and `rejected` are terminal — a decision is not
 * reversed, and a leave is never un-decided. The full request → approve /
 * reject **workflow with a log** is FR-ERP5-10 and stays in V2 (see DR-10).
 */
export const HR_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS: Readonly<
  Record<LeaveStatus, readonly LeaveStatus[]>
> = {
  pending: ['approved', 'rejected'],
  approved: [],
  rejected: [],
};

/** Leave statuses from which no further transition is allowed. */
export const HR_LEAVE_TERMINAL_STATUSES: readonly LeaveStatus[] = ['approved', 'rejected'];

/** The leave statuses the module accepts. */
export const HR_LEAVE_STATUSES: readonly LeaveStatus[] = ['pending', 'approved', 'rejected'];

/** The leave types the module accepts (FR-ERP5-04). */
export const HR_LEAVE_TYPES: readonly LeaveType[] = ['sick', 'holiday', 'other'];

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Salary masking (BR-ERP5-02)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** The only salary representation a non-privileged viewer may receive. */
export const HR_SALARY_MASK = 'XX,XXX';

/** The representation used when salary is not disclosed at all. */
export const HR_SALARY_HIDDEN = 'salary hidden';

/**
 * Masks a salary value for serialization.
 *
 * BR-ERP5-02: salary is never shown in full to viewers and never appears in a
 * log. The masked form carries no part of the underlying value.
 */
export function maskHrSalary(): string {
  return HR_SALARY_MASK;
}

/** Hides salary entirely (field withheld from the payload). */
export function hideHrSalary(): string {
  return HR_SALARY_HIDDEN;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Import provenance (KIT-08 / Master Architecture Spec §6.1 slot 01)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Migration batch identifier.
 *
 * `erp5.import_receipts.batch_id` is a UUID column, so the batch identity is
 * a UUID. The Migration Kit owns the batch; the module only stores it on the
 * receipt it writes next to the target mutation.
 */
export const HrBatchId = z.string().uuid('Batch id must be a valid UUID');

/** Source row number inside the batch. */
export const HrSourceRow = z.number().int().min(0).max(1_000_000);

/**
 * Stable idempotency key — `batch id + source row + target entity/schema
 * version`. A retry reuses the key and returns the original receipt without
 * performing a second mutation.
 */
export const HrIdempotencyKey = z.string().min(1).max(128);

/** Default ISO 4217 currency applied when a client does not supply one. */
export const HR_DEFAULT_CURRENCY = 'INR';

/**
 * Fields that are never writable through any ERP-5 endpoint.
 *
 * They are either system-maintained (`id`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) or
 * identity/credential fields that the HR module does not own at all
 * (Master Architecture Spec C3 — HR stores no credentials and grants no
 * application roles).
 */
export const HR_NEVER_WRITABLE_FIELDS = [
  'id',
  'created_at',
  'updated_at',
  'password',
  'password_hash',
  'token',
  'access_token',
  'refresh_token',
  'secret',
  'roles',
  'role',
  'permissions',
  'employee_ref',
  'salary_enc',
] as const;

/**
 * Source-identity fields.
 *
 * They are system-maintained for a browser write. Only the Migration Kit load
 * path — a request carrying the full import provenance (`batch_id`,
 * `source_row` **and** an idempotency key) — may set them.
 */
export const HR_IMPORT_ONLY_FIELDS = ['source_system', 'source_id'] as const;

/**
 * Every field name that is protected or system-maintained for a browser write.
 *
 * Used to classify a rejected payload with the stable `HR_PROTECTED_FIELD`
 * code instead of a generic validation failure.
 */
export const HR_PROTECTED_FIELD_NAMES = [
  ...HR_NEVER_WRITABLE_FIELDS,
  ...HR_IMPORT_ONLY_FIELDS,
  'currency_code',
  // Employee-level fields — never part of a payroll aggregate (FR-ERP5-06).
  'employee_id',
  'employee_ids',
  'employee_no',
  'employees',
  'first_name',
  'last_name',
  'birth_date',
  'salary',
] as const;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type HrLeave = z.infer<typeof HrLeaveSchema>;

/** FR-ERP5-06 — department/month payroll aggregate. No employee rows. */
export const HrPayrollTotalSchema = z.object({
  id: HrUuid,
  department_id: HrUuid,
  month: HrMonth,

/**

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Request schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Import provenance accepted by every ERP-5 write endpoint.
 *
 * The three fields travel together: an idempotency key without a batch and a
 * source row cannot produce a durable receipt, so a partial triple is
 * rejected instead of being silently ignored.
 */
const hrImportProvenanceShape = {
  batch_id: HrBatchId.optional(),
  source_row: HrSourceRow.optional(),
  idempotency_key: HrIdempotencyKey.optional(),
};

/** POST /api/v1/erp-hr/departments */
export const CreateHrDepartmentRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z.string().min(1).max(255),
    code: HrDepartmentCode,
    parent_id: HrUuid.nullish(),
    ...hrImportProvenanceShape,
  })
  .strict();
export type CreateHrDepartmentRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateHrDepartmentRequestSchema>;

/**
 * PATCH /api/v1/erp-hr/departments/{id}
 *
 * `parent_id` may be cleared with an explicit `null`.
 */
export const UpdateHrDepartmentRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z.string().min(1).max(255).optional(),
    code: HrDepartmentCode.optional(),
    parent_id: HrUuid.nullish(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length > 0, {
    message: 'At least one field must be provided',
  });
export type UpdateHrDepartmentRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateHrDepartmentRequestSchema>;

/**
 * POST /api/v1/erp-hr/employees
 *
 * BR-ERP5-01 is asserted here and again by the service layer and by the
 * database constraint. The refinement carries the stable domain code so the
 * route boundary can answer `422` instead of a generic `400`.
 */
export const CreateHrEmployeeRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    employee_no: HrEmployeeNo,
    first_name: HrPersonName,
    last_name: HrPersonName,
    birth_date: HrIsoDate,
    join_date: HrIsoDate,
    department_id: HrUuid,
    job_title: HrJobTitle,
    salary: HrMoneyAmount,
    currency_code: HrCurrencyCode.optional(),
    source_system: z.string().max(64).optional(),
    source_id: z.string().max(128).optional(),
    ...hrImportProvenanceShape,
  })
  .strict()
  .refine((value) => value.join_date >= value.birth_date, {
    message: 'join_date must not be before birth_date',
    path: ['join_date'],
    params: { code: HR_DOMAIN_RULES.JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE },
  });
export type CreateHrEmployeeRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateHrEmployeeRequestSchema>;

/**
 * PATCH /api/v1/erp-hr/employees/{id}
 *
 * Only the allow-listed mutable fields are accepted. `currency_code`,
 * `source_system`, `source_id`, `id`, `created_at` and `updated_at` are
 * system-maintained and are rejected by the strict object.
 */
export const UpdateHrEmployeeRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    employee_no: HrEmployeeNo.optional(),
    first_name: HrPersonName.optional(),
    last_name: HrPersonName.optional(),
    birth_date: HrIsoDate.optional(),
    join_date: HrIsoDate.optional(),
    department_id: HrUuid.optional(),
    job_title: HrJobTitle.optional(),
    salary: HrMoneyAmount.optional(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      value.join_date === undefined ||
      value.birth_date === undefined ||
      value.join_date >= value.birth_date,
    {
      message: 'join_date must not be before birth_date',
      path: ['join_date'],
      params: { code: HR_DOMAIN_RULES.JOIN_DATE_AFTER_BIRTH_DATE },
    }
  )
  .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length > 0, {
    message: 'At least one field must be provided',
  });
export type UpdateHrEmployeeRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateHrEmployeeRequestSchema>;

/**
 * POST /api/v1/erp-hr/leave
 * The leave end date can never precede the start date.
 */
export const CreateHrLeaveRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    employee_id: HrUuid,
    date_from: HrIsoDate,
    date_to: HrIsoDate,
    type: LeaveTypeEnum,
    status: LeaveStatusEnum.optional(),
    ...hrImportProvenanceShape,
  })
  .strict()
  .refine((value) => value.date_to >= value.date_from, {
    message: 'date_to must not be before date_from',
    path: ['date_to'],
    params: { code: HR_DOMAIN_RULES.LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID },
  });
export type CreateHrLeaveRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateHrLeaveRequestSchema>;

/**
 * POST /api/v1/erp-hr/payroll-totals
 *
 * FR-ERP5-06 — one aggregate per department and month. The strict object
 * carries no employee-level field, so an employee row can never be stored.
 */
export const CreateHrPayrollTotalRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    department_id: HrUuid,
    month: HrMonth,
    total_amount: HrMoneyAmount,
    currency_code: HrCurrencyCode.optional(),
    headcount: z.number().int().min(0).optional(),
    ...hrImportProvenanceShape,
  })
  .strict();
export type CreateHrPayrollTotalRequest = z.infer<typeof CreateHrPayrollTotalRequestSchema>;

/**
 * PATCH /api/v1/erp-hr/leave/{id}
 *
 * `status` is the state transition and is checked against the V1 leave state
 * matrix (`HR_LEAVE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS`). The recorded subject — the employee
 * the leave belongs to — is **immutable**: a leave is a fact about one
 * employee and is never re-parented.
 *
 * `date_from`, `date_to` and `type` stay editable and are re-validated
 * (the date range and the enumeration) by the domain layer.
 */
export const UpdateHrLeaveRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    status: LeaveStatusEnum.optional(),
    date_from: HrIsoDate.optional(),
    date_to: HrIsoDate.optional(),
    type: LeaveTypeEnum.optional(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      value.date_from === undefined ||
      value.date_to === undefined ||
      value.date_to >= value.date_from,
    {
      message: 'date_to must not be before date_from',
      path: ['date_to'],
      params: { code: HR_DOMAIN_RULES.LEAVE_DATE_RANGE_VALID },
    }
  )
  .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length > 0, {
    message: 'At least one field must be provided',
  });
export type UpdateHrLeaveRequest = z.infer<typeof UpdateHrLeaveRequestSchema>;

/** Path parameter of every ERP-5 resource endpoint. */
export const HrIdParamSchema = z.object({ id: HrUuid });
export type HrIdParam = z.infer<typeof HrIdParamSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Import receipt (written in the same transaction as the target mutation)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Durable import receipt — mirrors `erp5.import_receipts`. */
export const HrImportReceiptSchema = z.object({
  id: HrUuid,
  batch_id: HrUuid,
  source_row: z.number().int().min(0),
  entity: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  schema_version: z.number().int().positive().default(1),
  idempotency_key: z.string().min(1).max(128),
  target_id: HrUuid,
  kind: z.enum(['created', 'linked']).default('created'),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type HrImportReceipt = z.infer<typeof HrImportReceiptSchema>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Mutation response schemas
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Response of a create/edit endpoint.
 *
 * `import_receipt` is present only when the write carried the full import
 * provenance (`batch_id` + `source_row` + idempotency key). A repeated import
 * with the same idempotency key returns the original receipt.
 *
 * `warnings` reports **absent optional relationships**. The module never
 * invents a record to satisfy an optional link.
 */
export const HrEmployeeMutationResponseSchema = HrEmployeeViewSchema.extend({
  import_receipt: HrImportReceiptSchema.optional(),
  warnings: z.array(HrRelationshipWarningSchema).optional(),
});
export type HrEmployeeMutationResponse = z.infer<typeof HrEmployeeMutationResponseSchema>;

export const HrDepartmentMutationResponseSchema = HrDepartmentSchema.extend({
  import_receipt: HrImportReceiptSchema.optional(),
  warnings: z.array(HrRelationshipWarningSchema).optional(),
});
export type HrDepartmentMutationResponse = z.infer<typeof HrDepartmentMutationResponseSchema>;

export const HrLeaveMutationResponseSchema = HrLeaveSchema.extend({
  import_receipt: HrImportReceiptSchema.optional(),
  warnings: z.array(HrRelationshipWarningSchema).optional(),
});
export type HrLeaveMutationResponse = z.infer<typeof HrLeaveMutationResponseSchema>;

export const HrPayrollTotalMutationResponseSchema = HrPayrollTotalSchema.extend({
  import_receipt: HrImportReceiptSchema.optional(),
  warnings: z.array(HrRelationshipWarningSchema).optional(),
});
export type HrPayrollTotalMutationResponse = z.infer<
  typeof HrPayrollTotalMutationResponseSchema
>;


// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Bounded pagination + list responses
