/** Default page size for every ERP-5 collection endpoint. */
export const HR_PAGE_SIZE_DEFAULT = 25;

/** Hard page-size ceiling — a larger request is rejected with 400. */
export const HR_PAGE_SIZE_MAX = 100;

/** Bounded pagination — `page` starts at 1, `pageSize` is 1..100. */
export const HrPaginationQuerySchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(HR_PAGE_SIZE_MAX).default(HR_PAGE_SIZE_DEFAULT),
});
export type HrPaginationQuery = z.infer<typeof HrPaginationQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-hr/employees */
export const HrEmployeeListQuerySchema = HrPaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  search: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
  department_id: HrUuid.optional(),
  sort: z.enum(['name', 'employee_no', 'join_date']).default('name'),
});
export type HrEmployeeListQuery = z.infer<typeof HrEmployeeListQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-hr/departments */
export const HrDepartmentListQuerySchema = HrPaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  search: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
  parent_id: HrUuid.optional(),
  sort: z.enum(['name', 'code']).default('name'),
});
export type HrDepartmentListQuery = z.infer<typeof HrDepartmentListQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-hr/leave */
export const HrLeaveListQuerySchema = HrPaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  employee_id: HrUuid.optional(),
  type: LeaveTypeEnum.optional(),
  status: LeaveStatusEnum.optional(),
});
export type HrLeaveListQuery = z.infer<typeof HrLeaveListQuerySchema>;

/** GET /api/v1/erp-hr/payroll-totals?month=YYYY-MM */
export const HrPayrollTotalListQuerySchema = HrPaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  month: HrMonth.optional(),
  department_id: HrUuid.optional(),
});
export type HrPayrollTotalListQuery = z.infer<typeof HrPayrollTotalListQuerySchema>;

/**
 * GET /api/v1/erp-hr/identity-users
 *
 * The read-only Hub Identity projection. The query may only narrow the
 * result set — it never selects a field, so no credential or token column
 * can be reached through it.
 */
export const HrIdentityUserListQuerySchema = HrPaginationQuerySchema.extend({
  search: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
});
export type HrIdentityUserListQuery = z.infer<typeof HrIdentityUserListQuerySchema>;

/** Collection metadata returned with every list response. */
export const HrListMetaSchema = z.object({
  page: z.number().int().min(1),
  pageSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(HR_PAGE_SIZE_MAX),
  total: z.number().int().min(0),
  totalPages: z.number().int().min(0),
});
export type HrListMeta = z.infer<typeof HrListMetaSchema>;

/** Generic bounded list response. */
export function hrListResponseSchema<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(item: T) {
  return z.object({
    data: z.array(item),
    meta: HrListMetaSchema,
  });
}
=== packages/contracts/src/events.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod';

/**
 * Standard ClearShift Transactional Event Envelope Schema (PLT-04 / PLT-09)
 * Used across Outbox, Redis Streams relay, and idempotent consumers.
 */
export const EventEnvelopeSchema = z.object({
  event_id: z.string().uuid({ message: 'event_id must be a valid UUID' }),
  event_type: z.string().min(1, { message: 'event_type is required' }),
  schema_version: z.string().min(1, { message: 'schema_version is required' }).default('1.0.0'),
  occurred_at: z.string().datetime({ message: 'occurred_at must be an ISO 8601 datetime string' }),
  correlation_id: z.string().min(1, { message: 'correlation_id is required' }),
  producer: z.string().min(1, { message: 'producer module identifier is required' }),
  entity_type: z.string().min(1, { message: 'entity_type is required' }),
  entity_id: z.string().min(1, { message: 'entity_id is required' }),
  batch_id: z.string().optional(),
  payload: z.unknown(),
  metadata: z.record(z.unknown()).optional(),
});

export type EventEnvelope = z.infer<typeof EventEnvelopeSchema>;

/**
 * Factory for strongly-typed domain event envelopes
 */
export function createEventEnvelopeSchema<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(payloadSchema: T) {
  return EventEnvelopeSchema.extend({
    payload: payloadSchema,
  });
}

/**
 * Factory helper to construct an EventEnvelope instance
 */
export function createEventEnvelope<T>(params: {
  event_id: string;
  event_type: string;
  schema_version?: string;
  occurred_at?: string;
  correlation_id: string;
  producer: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  batch_id?: string;
  payload: T;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}): EventEnvelope {
  return {
    event_id: params.event_id,
    event_type: params.event_type,
    schema_version: params.schema_version ?? '1.0.0',
    occurred_at: params.occurred_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    correlation_id: params.correlation_id,
    producer: params.producer,
    entity_type: params.entity_type,
    entity_id: params.entity_id,
    ...(params.batch_id ? { batch_id: params.batch_id } : {}),
    payload: params.payload,
    ...(params.metadata ? { metadata: params.metadata } : {}),
  };
}
export * from './erp-4-events.js';
=== packages/contracts/src/golden-id.ts ===
import { z } from "zod";

export const EntityTypeEnum = z.enum([
  "customer",
  "vendor",
  "contact",
  "employee",
  "account",
  "deal"
]);

export type EntityType = z.infer<typeof EntityTypeEnum> | (string & {});

export const SecondaryIdentifiersSchema = z.record(z.string()).and(
  z.object({
    tax_id: z.string().optional(),
    email: z.string().optional(),
    phone: z.string().optional()
  })
);

export type SecondaryIdentifiers = z.infer<typeof SecondaryIdentifiersSchema>;

export const GoldenIdStatusSchema = z.enum([
  "active",
  "needs_review",
  "merged"
]);

export type GoldenIdStatus = z.infer<typeof GoldenIdStatusSchema>;

export const GoldenIdRecordSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid("Primary Golden ID must be a valid UUID"),
  display_gid: z.string().regex(/^GID-[A-Z]+-[A-Z0-9]+$/, "Display GID must follow GID-<TYPE>-<ID> format"),
  entity_type: z.string().min(1),
  source_system: z.string().min(1),
  source_id: z.string().min(1),
  identifiers: SecondaryIdentifiersSchema.default({}),
  status: GoldenIdStatusSchema.default("active"),
  review_reason: z.string().optional(),
  conflicting_ids: z.array(z.string().uuid()).default([]),
  created_at: z.string(),
  updated_at: z.string()
});

export type GoldenIdRecord = z.infer<typeof GoldenIdRecordSchema>;

export const LookupGoldenIdRequestSchema = z.object({
  entity_type: z.string().min(1, "entity_type is required"),
  source_system: z.string().min(1, "source_system is required"),
  source_id: z.string().min(1, "source_id is required")
});

export type LookupGoldenIdRequest = z.infer<typeof LookupGoldenIdRequestSchema>;

export const UpsertGoldenIdRequestSchema = z.object({
  entity_type: z.string().min(1, "entity_type is required"),
  source_system: z.string().min(1, "source_system is required"),
  source_id: z.string().min(1, "source_id is required"),
  identifiers: SecondaryIdentifiersSchema.optional()
});

export type UpsertGoldenIdRequest = z.infer<typeof UpsertGoldenIdRequestSchema>;

export const GoldenIdResponseSchema = z.object({
  data: GoldenIdRecordSchema,
  is_new: z.boolean(),
  needs_review: z.boolean()
});

export type GoldenIdResponse = z.infer<typeof GoldenIdResponseSchema>;
=== packages/contracts/src/import-target.ts ===
/**
 * ClearShift Migration Kit — Target Adapter & Import Contract Types
 *
 * Defines Zod schemas and TypeScript types for the target adapter contract
 * used by KIT-08 and public module import endpoints.
 *
 * Decision C4 & KIT-08:
 * - Module local DB transaction writes domain mutation and receipt together.
 * - Same key + same canonical request returns prior result.
 * - Same key + different request returns HTTP 409 Conflict.
 * - Business validation reports rejected with stable reason code.
 * - Unexpected infrastructure failure (503) remains retryable and not misclassified as bad data.
 *
 * @module contracts/import-target
 */

import { z } from 'zod';
import { UuidSchema } from './erp-4.js';

/**
 * Migration row outcome classification (Section 4.3 & KIT-08).
 */
export const ImportOutcomeEnum = z.enum([
  'loaded',
  'rejected',
  'merged',
  'skipped',
]);
export type ImportOutcome = z.infer<typeof ImportOutcomeEnum>;

/**
 * Import receipt kind.
 */
export const ImportKindEnum = z.enum(['created', 'linked']);
export type ImportKind = z.infer<typeof ImportKindEnum>;

/**
 * Durable import receipt schema (Decision C4 & KIT-08).
 * Stored in <module>_import_receipts table.
 */
export const ImportReceiptRecordSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  batch_id: UuidSchema,
  source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  source_system: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  source_id: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  entity: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  schema_version: z.number().int().positive().default(1),
  idempotency_key: z.string().min(1).max(128),
  request_hash: z.string().max(64),
  target_id: UuidSchema,
  kind: ImportKindEnum.default('created'),
  outcome: ImportOutcomeEnum.default('loaded'),
  response_payload: z.unknown().optional().nullable(),
  created_at: z.string().datetime(),
});
export type ImportReceiptRecord = z.infer<typeof ImportReceiptRecordSchema>;

/**
 * Public row import request schema accepted by target adapter / import routes.
 */
export const ImportRowRequestSchema = z.object({
  batch_id: UuidSchema,
  source_row: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  source_system: z.string().max(64).optional().nullable(),
  source_id: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  idempotency_key: z.string().min(1).max(128),
  correlation_id: z.string().max(128).optional().nullable(),
  entity: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  record: z.record(z.unknown()),
});
export type ImportRowRequest = z.infer<typeof ImportRowRequestSchema>;

/**
 * Successful target adapter response payload.
 */
export const ImportRowResponseSchema = z.object({
  target_id: UuidSchema,
  target_version: z.union([z.number(), z.string()]).default(1),
  outcome: ImportOutcomeEnum.default('loaded'),
  receipt: ImportReceiptRecordSchema,
  prior_result: z.boolean().default(false),
});
export type ImportRowResponse = z.infer<typeof ImportRowResponseSchema>;

/**
 * Target adapter error / rejection schema.
 */
export const TargetAdapterErrorSchema = z.object({
  reason_code: z.string(),
  message: z.string(),
  field: z.string().optional(),
  retryable: z.boolean().default(false),
});
export type TargetAdapterError = z.infer<typeof TargetAdapterErrorSchema>;

=== packages/contracts/tests/erp-1.test.ts ===
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import {
  CategoryListQuerySchema,
  CreateProductInputSchema,
  ProductListQuerySchema,
  ProductSchema,
  SetOpeningStockInputSchema,
  UpdateCategoryInputSchema,
} from '../src/erp-1.js';

const PRODUCT_ID = '00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';

describe('ERP-1 Products & Inventory contracts', () => {
  describe('Product money and currency contracts', () => {
    it('accepts SDS numeric(19,4) price precision', () => {
      const parsed = ProductSchema.parse({
        id: PRODUCT_ID,
        name: 'Synthetic Widget',
        sku: 'WDG-001',
        normalizedSku: 'WDG-001',
        unitOfMeasure: 'pcs',
        price: '19.9900',
        currency: 'USD',
        taxRate: '5.00',
        createdAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        updatedAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
      });

      expect(parsed.price).toBe('19.9900');
      expect(parsed.currency).toBe('USD');
    });

    it('rejects more than four decimal places in a price', () => {
      expect(
        ProductSchema.safeParse({
          id: PRODUCT_ID,
          name: 'Synthetic Widget',
          sku: 'WDG-001',
          normalizedSku: 'WDG-001',
          unitOfMeasure: 'pcs',
          price: '19.99000',
          currency: 'USD',
          createdAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
          updatedAt: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        }).success
      ).toBe(false);
    });

    it('requires an explicit uppercase ISO 4217 currency on create', () => {
      const base = {
        name: 'Synthetic Widget',
        sku: 'WDG-001',
        unitOfMeasure: 'pcs',
        price: 19.5,
      };

      expect(CreateProductInputSchema.safeParse(base).success).toBe(false);
      expect(
        CreateProductInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, currency: 'usd' }).success
      ).toBe(false);

      const parsed = CreateProductInputSchema.parse({ ...base, currency: 'USD' });
      expect(parsed.price).toBe('19.5000');
      expect(parsed.currency).toBe('USD');
    });

    it('rejects malformed decimal strings instead of partially parsing them', () => {
      const base = {
        name: 'Synthetic Widget',
        sku: 'WDG-001',
        unitOfMeasure: 'pcs',
        currency: 'USD',
      };

      expect(
        CreateProductInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, price: '12abc' }).success
      ).toBe(false);
      expect(
        CreateProductInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, price: '0x10' }).success
      ).toBe(false);
      expect(
        CreateProductInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, price: '19.99000' }).success
      ).toBe(false);
    });

    it('normalizes stock quantities to four decimal places', () => {
      const parsed = SetOpeningStockInputSchema.parse({
        productId: PRODUCT_ID,
        quantity: '150.5',
      });
      expect(parsed.quantity).toBe('150.5000');

      expect(() =>
        SetOpeningStockInputSchema.parse({
          productId: PRODUCT_ID,
          quantity: '-0.0001',
        })
      ).toThrow(/Stock quantity cannot become negative/);
    });
  });

  describe('Pagination contracts', () => {
    it('uses the architecture default page size of 25 and caps it at 100', () => {
      expect(ProductListQuerySchema.parse({}).pageSize).toBe(25);
      expect(CategoryListQuerySchema.parse({}).pageSize).toBe(25);
      expect(ProductListQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: 100 }).success).toBe(true);
      expect(ProductListQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: 101 }).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('accepts product search and category filters', () => {
      const parsed = ProductListQuerySchema.parse({
        search: 'widget',
        categoryId: PRODUCT_ID,
      });
      expect(parsed.search).toBe('widget');
      expect(parsed.categoryId).toBe(PRODUCT_ID);
    });
  });

  describe('Category edit contract', () => {
    it('accepts a partial category update', () => {
      expect(UpdateCategoryInputSchema.parse({ name: 'Renamed' })).toEqual({
        name: 'Renamed',
      });
    });
  });

  describe('ERP-1 OpenAPI document', () => {
    const content = readFileSync(
      resolve(__dirname, '../openapi/erp-1.yaml'),
      'utf8'
    );

    it('documents every required ERP-1 endpoint', () => {
      const requiredPaths = [
        '/api/v1/erp-products/products:',
        '/api/v1/erp-products/products/{id}:',
        '/api/v1/erp-products/categories:',
        '/api/v1/erp-products/categories/{id}:',
        '/api/v1/erp-products/stock:',
        '/api/v1/erp-products/stock/opening:',
      ];

      for (const path of requiredPaths) {
        expect(content).toContain(path);
      }
      expect(content).toContain('operationId: updateErp1Category');
      expect(content).toContain('operationId: getErp1Product');
    });

    it('uses the shared flat pagination response instead of an undefined PaginationMeta', () => {
      expect(content).not.toContain('PaginationMeta');
      expect(content).toContain('ProductListResponse:');
      expect(content).toContain('CategoryListResponse:');
      expect(content).toContain('required: [items, page, pageSize, total]');
    });

    it('documents numeric(19,4) strings and explicit currency', () => {
      expect(content).toContain('pattern: \'^\\d+(\\.\\d{1,4})?$\'');
      expect(content).toContain('Currency:');
      expect(content).toContain("pattern: '^[A-Z]{3}$'");
      expect(content).toContain('required: [name, sku, unitOfMeasure, price, currency]');
    });

    it('resolves every local component schema reference', () => {
      const componentNames = new Set(
        [...content.matchAll(/^    ([A-Za-z0-9]+):$/gm)].map((match) => match[1])
      );
      const localRefs = [
        ...content.matchAll(/\$ref: '#\/components\/schemas\/([A-Za-z0-9]+)'/g),
      ].map((match) => match[1]);

      expect(localRefs.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const ref of localRefs) {
        expect(componentNames.has(ref as string), `missing component ${ref}`).toBe(true);
      }
    });
  });
});
=== packages/contracts/tests/erp-4.contract.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — Zod Contract & Invariant Test Suite
 *
 * Proves that:
 *   - Account schemas enforce ISO 4217 currency code and alphanumeric code patterns.
 *   - Journal line schemas enforce exactly one of debit > 0 or credit > 0.
 *   - Journal entry creation/update schemas enforce BR-ERP4-01: SUM(debits) === SUM(credits).
 *   - MoneyAmount enforces decimal string format (up to 4 decimal places) and rejects floats/negatives.
 *   - Pagination schemas enforce positive integers and the non-negotiable pageSize <= 100 limit.
 *   - Accounting period FSM transitions and locks are validated.
 *
 * @module contracts/tests/erp-4.contract.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  CurrencyCode,
  MoneyAmount,
  PeriodLabel,
  AccountTypeEnum,
  JournalEntryStatusEnum,
  PeriodStatusEnum,
  CreateAccountRequestSchema,
  UpdateAccountRequestSchema,
  CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema,
  UpdateJournalEntryRequestSchema,
  CreateJournalLineRequestSchema,
  isJournalEntryBalanced,
  AccountListQuerySchema,
  JournalEntryListQuerySchema,
  TrialBalanceQuerySchema,
  CreateOpeningBalancesRequestSchema,
  UpdatePeriodRequestSchema,
  RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES,
} from '../src/erp-4.js';

describe('ERP-4 Shared Primitives & Enums', () => {
  it('validates ISO 4217 CurrencyCode format', () => {
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('INR').success).toBe(true);
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('USD').success).toBe(true);
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('EUR').success).toBe(true);
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('inr').success).toBe(false); // lower case rejected
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('USDD').success).toBe(false); // length != 3
    expect(CurrencyCode.safeParse('123').success).toBe(false); // non-alpha
  });

  it('validates MoneyAmount format (string-encoded non-negative decimal, up to 4dp)', () => {
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('100').success).toBe(true);
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('100.5').success).toBe(true);
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('100.50').success).toBe(true);
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('100.5000').success).toBe(true);
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('0.0000').success).toBe(true);

    // Rejects more than 4 decimal places
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('100.50001').success).toBe(false);
    // Rejects negative amounts (lines must use credit/debit distinction, not negative signs)
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('-100.00').success).toBe(false);
    // Rejects non-string
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse(100.5 as any).success).toBe(false);
    // Rejects text
    expect(MoneyAmount.safeParse('abc').success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('validates PeriodLabel format (YYYY-MM)', () => {
    expect(PeriodLabel.safeParse('2026-04').success).toBe(true);
    expect(PeriodLabel.safeParse('2026-12').success).toBe(true);
    expect(PeriodLabel.safeParse('2026-00').success).toBe(false);
    expect(PeriodLabel.safeParse('2026-13').success).toBe(false);
    expect(PeriodLabel.safeParse('2026-4').success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('enforces exact domain enum types', () => {
    expect(AccountTypeEnum.options).toEqual(['asset', 'liability', 'income', 'expense']);
    expect(JournalEntryStatusEnum.options).toEqual(['draft', 'posted', 'reversed', 'voided']);
    expect(PeriodStatusEnum.options).toEqual(['open', 'soft_closed', 'locked']);
  });
});

describe('ERP-4 Chart of Accounts Contract', () => {
  it('accepts valid CreateAccountRequest', () => {
    const valid = {
      code: '1010-CASH',
      name: 'Cash on Hand',
      type: 'asset',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      is_posting_account: true,
    };
    const result = CreateAccountRequestSchema.safeParse(valid);
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    if (result.success) {
      expect(result.data.code).toBe('1010-CASH');
      expect(result.data.currency_code).toBe('INR');
      expect(result.data.is_posting_account).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('rejects account code with invalid characters (spaces, special symbols)', () => {
    expect(
      CreateAccountRequestSchema.safeParse({
        code: '1010 CASH', // Space not allowed
        name: 'Cash',
        type: 'asset',
      }).success
    ).toBe(false);

    expect(
      CreateAccountRequestSchema.safeParse({
        code: '1010@CASH!', // Special symbols
        name: 'Cash',
        type: 'asset',
      }).success
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it('accepts UpdateAccountRequest with mutable fields', () => {
    expect(
      UpdateAccountRequestSchema.safeParse({
        name: 'Updated Name',
        is_active: false,
      }).success
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it('enforces bounded pagination on AccountListQuerySchema (max 100)', () => {
    expect(AccountListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 20 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(AccountListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 100 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(AccountListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 101 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(AccountListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 0 }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('ERP-4 Double-Entry Invariant (BR-ERP4-01)', () => {
  const accountA = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';
  const accountB = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002';
  const periodId = '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';

  it('validates single line rule: exactly one of debit > 0 or credit > 0', () => {
    // Valid debit
    expect(
      CreateJournalLineRequestSchema.safeParse({
        account_id: accountA,
        debit_amount: '500.0000',
        credit_amount: '0.0000',
      }).success
    ).toBe(true);

    // Valid credit
    expect(
      CreateJournalLineRequestSchema.safeParse({
        account_id: accountA,
        debit_amount: '0.0000',
        credit_amount: '500.0000',
      }).success
    ).toBe(true);

    // Invalid: both zero
    expect(
      CreateJournalLineRequestSchema.safeParse({
        account_id: accountA,
        debit_amount: '0.0000',
        credit_amount: '0.0000',
      }).success
    ).toBe(false);

    // Invalid: both non-zero
    expect(
      CreateJournalLineRequestSchema.safeParse({
        account_id: accountA,
        debit_amount: '100.0000',
        credit_amount: '50.0000',
      }).success
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it('accepts perfectly balanced journal entry', () => {
    const balancedEntry = {
      period_id: periodId,
      posting_date: '2026-04-15',
      description: 'Test balanced journal',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      lines: [
        {
          account_id: accountA,
          debit_amount: '1250.5000',
          credit_amount: '0.0000',
        },
        {
          account_id: accountB,
          debit_amount: '0.0000',
          credit_amount: '1250.5000',
        },
      ],
    };
    const result = CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema.safeParse(balancedEntry);
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects unbalanced journal entry (total debit != total credit)', () => {
    const unbalancedEntry = {
      period_id: periodId,
      posting_date: '2026-04-15',
      description: 'Test unbalanced journal',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      lines: [
        {
          account_id: accountA,
          debit_amount: '1250.5000',
          credit_amount: '0.0000',
        },
        {
          account_id: accountB,
          debit_amount: '0.0000',
          credit_amount: '1250.4900', // 1 cent difference
        },
      ],
    };
    const result = CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema.safeParse(unbalancedEntry);
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    if (!result.success) {
      expect(result.error.issues[0]?.message).toContain('not balanced');
    }
  });

  it('rejects journal entry with fewer than 2 lines', () => {
    const singleLineEntry = {
      period_id: periodId,
      posting_date: '2026-04-15',
      description: 'Test single line entry',
      currency_code: 'INR',
      lines: [
        {
          account_id: accountA,
          debit_amount: '100.0000',
          credit_amount: '0.0000',
        },
      ],
    };
    expect(CreateJournalEntryRequestSchema.safeParse(singleLineEntry).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('isJournalEntryBalanced helper correctly evaluates multi-line split entries', () => {
    // 1 debit of 100, 2 credits of 60 and 40
    const splitLines = [
      { account_id: 'a', debit_amount: '100.0000', credit_amount: '0.0000' },
      { account_id: 'b', debit_amount: '0.0000', credit_amount: '60.0000' },
      { account_id: 'c', debit_amount: '0.0000', credit_amount: '40.0000' },
    ];
    expect(isJournalEntryBalanced(splitLines)).toBe(true);

    // Also supports frontend view model { debit, credit }
    const feLines = [
      { debit: 250, credit: 0 },
      { debit: 0, credit: 250 },
    ];
    expect(isJournalEntryBalanced(feLines)).toBe(true);

    // Unbalanced
    const badLines = [
      { debit: 250, credit: 0 },
      { debit: 0, credit: 249.99 },
    ];
    expect(isJournalEntryBalanced(badLines)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('enforces bounded pagination on JournalEntryListQuerySchema (max 100)', () => {
    expect(JournalEntryListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 20 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(JournalEntryListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 100 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(JournalEntryListQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 101 }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('ERP-4 Accounting Periods & Opening Balances Contracts', () => {
  it('validates TrialBalanceQuerySchema requires valid PeriodLabel', () => {
    expect(TrialBalanceQuerySchema.safeParse({ period: '2026-04' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(TrialBalanceQuerySchema.safeParse({ period: 'invalid' }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('validates UpdatePeriodRequestSchema requires valid status', () => {
    expect(UpdatePeriodRequestSchema.safeParse({ status: 'soft_closed' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UpdatePeriodRequestSchema.safeParse({ status: 'locked' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UpdatePeriodRequestSchema.safeParse({ status: 'unknown_status' as any }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('validates CreateOpeningBalancesRequestSchema accepts signed amounts (negative for contra)', () => {
    const valid = {
      balances: [
        {
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
          currency_code: 'INR',
          balance_amount: '50000.0000',
          as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
        },
        {
          account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
          currency_code: 'INR',
          balance_amount: '-15000.0000', // Signed decimal
          as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
        },
      ],
    };
    expect(CreateOpeningBalancesRequestSchema.safeParse(valid).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('exports stable relationship error codes', () => {
    expect(RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.PERIOD_LOCKED).toBe('PERIOD_LOCKED');
    expect(RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.CANNOT_POST_TO_HEADER_ACCOUNT).toBe('CANNOT_POST_TO_HEADER_ACCOUNT');
    expect(RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.CYCLE_DETECTED).toBe('CYCLE_DETECTED');
    expect(RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.UNBALANCED_ENTRY).toBe('UNBALANCED_ENTRY');
    expect(RELATIONSHIP_ERROR_CODES.NOT_REVERSIBLE).toBe('NOT_REVERSIBLE');
  });
});
=== packages/contracts/tests/erp-4.events.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — Domain Events Test Suite
 *
 * Proves that:
 *   - All ERP-4 domain event schemas validate correctly formatted event envelopes.
 *   - Event envelopes strictly enforce UUID event_id, ISO occurred_at, and producer: 'erp-4'.
 *   - Payloads strictly match domain event schemas (AccountCreated, JournalEntryPosted, etc.).
 *   - Inbound event schemas (vendor bill, invoice, payroll total) parse valid cross-module inputs.
 *   - Helper factory createErp4Event properly defaults event_id, occurred_at, and producer.
 *
 * @module contracts/tests/erp-4.events.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  ERP4_EVENT_TYPES,
  AccountCreatedEventSchema,
  AccountUpdatedEventSchema,
  JournalEntryCreatedEventSchema,
  JournalEntryPostedEventSchema,
  JournalEntryReversedEventSchema,
  JournalEntryVoidedEventSchema,
  PeriodStatusChangedEventSchema,
  OpeningBalanceCreatedEventSchema,
  InboundVendorBillPostedEventSchema,
  InboundInvoicePostedEventSchema,
  InboundPayrollTotalPostedEventSchema,
  createErp4Event,
} from '../src/erp-4-events.js';

describe('ERP-4 Domain Event Envelopes (PLT-04 / PLT-09)', () => {
  const correlationId = 'req-trace-12345';
  const entityId = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';

  it('validates AccountCreatedEvent with complete payload', () => {
    const rawEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.ACCOUNT_CREATED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'account',
      entity_id: entityId,
      payload: {
        id: entityId,
        code: '1010-CASH',
        name: 'Cash on Hand',
        type: 'asset',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        is_posting_account: true,
        is_active: true,
        parent_id: null,
      },
    };

    const parsed = AccountCreatedEventSchema.safeParse(rawEvent);
    expect(parsed.success).toBe(true);
    if (parsed.success) {
      expect(parsed.data.payload.code).toBe('1010-CASH');
      expect(parsed.data.producer).toBe('erp-4');
    }
  });

  it('validates AccountUpdatedEvent with updated attributes', () => {
    const rawEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.ACCOUNT_UPDATED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'account',
      entity_id: entityId,
      payload: {
        id: entityId,
        code: '1010-CASH',
        name: 'Cash on Hand — Operational',
        type: 'asset',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        is_active: true,
        is_posting_account: true,
        parent_id: null,
      },
    };
    expect(AccountUpdatedEventSchema.safeParse(rawEvent).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('validates JournalEntryCreatedEvent (draft state)', () => {
    const rawEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000003',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_CREATED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-10T10:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        entry_number: 'JV-2026-0001',
        period_id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        posting_date: '2026-04-10',
        status: 'draft',
        description: 'Office supply draft voucher',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        line_count: 2,
        total_debit: '5000.0000',
        total_credit: '5000.0000',
      },
    };
    expect(JournalEntryCreatedEventSchema.safeParse(rawEvent).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('validates JournalEntryPostedEvent (posted state with user stamp)', () => {
    const rawEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000004',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_POSTED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-10T11:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        entry_number: 'JV-2026-0001',
        period_id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        posting_date: '2026-04-10',
        status: 'posted',
        description: 'Office supply draft voucher',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        posted_at: '2026-04-10T11:00:00.000Z',
        posted_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        total_amount: '5000.0000',
        line_count: 2,
      },
    };
    expect(JournalEntryPostedEventSchema.safeParse(rawEvent).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('validates JournalEntryReversedEvent and JournalEntryVoidedEvent', () => {
    const revEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000005',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_REVERSED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-11T09:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        original_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        original_entry_number: 'JV-2026-0001',
        reversal_entry_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002',
        reversal_entry_number: 'REV-JV-2026-0001',
        reversal_date: '2026-04-11',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        total_amount: '5000.0000',
        reason: 'Duplicate payment correction',
      },
    };
    expect(JournalEntryReversedEventSchema.safeParse(revEvent).success).toBe(true);

    const voidEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000006',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.JOURNAL_ENTRY_VOIDED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-11T09:30:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'journal_entry',
      entity_id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000003',
      payload: {
        id: '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000003',
        entry_number: 'JV-2026-0003',
        voided_at: '2026-04-11T09:30:00.000Z',
      },
    };
    expect(JournalEntryVoidedEventSchema.safeParse(voidEvent).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('validates PeriodStatusChangedEvent and OpeningBalanceCreatedEvent', () => {
    const periodEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000007',

      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-30T23:59:59.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'accounting_period',
      entity_id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        id: '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        period_label: '2026-04',
        previous_status: 'open',
        new_status: 'soft_closed',
        fiscal_year: 'FY2026-27',
        changed_at: '2026-04-30T23:59:59.000Z',
        changed_by_user_id: '99999999-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      },
    };
    expect(PeriodStatusChangedEventSchema.safeParse(periodEvent).success).toBe(true);

    const obEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000008',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.OPENING_BALANCE_CREATED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-4',
      entity_type: 'opening_balance',
      entity_id: '33333333-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        id: '33333333-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        account_id: entityId,
        currency_code: 'INR',
        balance_amount: '100000.0000',
        as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
      },
    };
    expect(OpeningBalanceCreatedEventSchema.safeParse(obEvent).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it('validates cross-module inbound event contracts (ERP-2, ERP-3, ERP-5)', () => {
    // Inbound Vendor Bill (from ERP-2)
    const billEvent = {

      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-12T10:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-2',
      entity_type: 'bill',
      entity_id: '88888888-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        bill_id: '88888888-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        vendor_id: '77777777-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        bill_number: 'BILL-2026-004',
        amount: '45000.0000',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        posting_date: '2026-04-12',
        expense_account_id: entityId,
        liability_account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000004',
      },
    };
    expect(InboundVendorBillPostedEventSchema.safeParse(billEvent).success).toBe(true);

    // Inbound Sales Invoice (from ERP-3)
    const invoiceEvent = {
      event_id: 'aaaaaaaa-0001-4000-8000-000000000010',
      event_type: ERP4_EVENT_TYPES.INBOUND_INVOICE_POSTED,
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      occurred_at: '2026-04-14T14:00:00.000Z',
      correlation_id: correlationId,
      producer: 'erp-3',
      entity_type: 'invoice',
      entity_id: '66666666-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      payload: {
        invoice_id: '66666666-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        customer_id: '55555555-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        invoice_number: 'INV-2026-012',

        currency_code: 'INR',
        posting_date: '2026-04-14',
        receivable_account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000003',
        revenue_account_id: '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000005',

=== packages/contracts/tests/erp-4.mock.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — Deterministic Mock Server Test Suite
 *
 * Proves that:
 *   - The mock server boots and provides representative, schema-valid payloads for all 18 V1 routes.
 *   - Accounts endpoints support listing, paging (bounded <= 100), creating, reading, and updating.
 *   - Journal entries endpoints enforce BR-ERP4-01 double-entry balancing and period lock guards.
 *   - Posting transitions draft -> posted, and reversal creates compensating entries.
 *   - Trial balance endpoint computes debit/credit totals grouped by account and reports balanced status.
 *   - Opening balances endpoint allows bulk loading and prevents duplicate account/currency pairs.
 *   - Accounting periods endpoint enforces FSM transitions (locked is permanent).
 *   - Emitted events are captured in the mock store for verification by dependent modules.
 *   - Reset helper returns store to deterministic seed state.
 *
 * @module contracts/tests/erp-4.mock.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterAll } from 'vitest';
import { createErp4MockServer, type Erp4MockServerInstance } from '../src/erp-4-mock.js';
import {
  AccountSchema,
  AccountListResponseSchema,
  JournalEntrySchema,
  JournalEntryListResponseSchema,
  TrialBalanceResponseSchema,
  CreateOpeningBalancesResponseSchema,
  OpeningBalanceListResponseSchema,
  PeriodListResponseSchema,
  AccountingPeriodSchema,
} from '../src/erp-4.js';

describe('ERP-4 Deterministic Mock Server & Routes', () => {
  let mockServer: Erp4MockServerInstance;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockServer = createErp4MockServer();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await mockServer.stop();
  });

  // ── 1. ACCOUNTS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Accounts Endpoints', () => {
    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts returns deterministic seed accounts with bounded pagination', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts?page=1&pageSize=5',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(AccountListResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.page).toBe(1);
      expect(json.pageSize).toBe(5);
      expect(json.data.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5);
      expect(json.total).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8);
      expect(json.data[0].code).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts rejects pageSize > 100 (non-negotiable invariant)', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts?pageSize=101',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
      expect(json.message).toContain('pageSize cannot exceed 100');
    });

    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts filters by type and active status', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts?type=asset&is_active=true',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      for (const acc of json.data) {
        expect(acc.type).toBe('asset');
        expect(acc.is_active).toBe(true);
      }
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts creates account and emits domain event', async () => {
      const newAccPayload = {
        code: '1099-PETTY',
        name: 'Petty Cash',
        type: 'asset',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        is_posting_account: true,
      };

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts',
        payload: newAccPayload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(AccountSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.code).toBe('1099-PETTY');

      // Verify domain event emitted
      const events = mockServer.store.emittedEvents.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'erp.account.created');
      expect(events.length).toBe(1);
      expect((events[0]?.payload as any).code).toBe('1099-PETTY');
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts returns 409 on duplicate code', async () => {
      const duplicatePayload = {
        code: '1010-CASH', // already exists in seed
        name: 'Duplicate Cash',
        type: 'asset',
      };

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts',
        payload: duplicatePayload,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
      expect(res.json().code).toBe('ACCOUNT_CODE_EXISTS');
    });

    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/:id returns single account or 404', async () => {
      // Existing
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
      });
      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(res.json().code).toBe('1010-CASH');

      // Nonexistent
      const res404 = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
      });
      expect(res404.statusCode).toBe(404);
      expect(res404.json().code).toBe('ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND');
    });

    it('PATCH /api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/:id updates mutable fields', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts/11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001',
        payload: {
          name: 'Main Petty Cash Vault',
          description: 'Updated description',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(res.json().name).toBe('Main Petty Cash Vault');
    });
  });

  // ── 2. JOURNAL ENTRIES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Journal Entries Endpoints & BR-ERP4-01 Double-Entry Invariant', () => {
    const periodId = '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001'; // Open period
    const accountBank = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002';
    const accountSales = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000005';
    const accountHdr = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000008'; // Non-posting header account

    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries returns paginated entries', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries?page=1&pageSize=10',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(JournalEntryListResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.data.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries creates draft when balanced', async () => {
      const balancedDraft = {
        period_id: periodId,
        posting_date: '2026-04-18',
        description: 'Synthetic: Customer payment received',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        lines: [
          {
            account_id: accountBank,
            debit_amount: '25000.0000',
            credit_amount: '0.0000',
            line_narration: 'Deposit to operating account',
          },
          {
            account_id: accountSales,
            debit_amount: '0.0000',
            credit_amount: '25000.0000',
            line_narration: 'Consulting sales income',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries',
        payload: balancedDraft,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(JournalEntrySchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.status).toBe('draft');
      expect(json.entry_number).toMatch(/^JV-2026-\d{4}$/);
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries rejects unbalanced entry (400 validation)', async () => {
      const unbalanced = {
        period_id: periodId,
        posting_date: '2026-04-18',
        description: 'Unbalanced entry',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        lines: [
          {
            account_id: accountBank,
            debit_amount: '25000.0000',
            credit_amount: '0.0000',
          },
          {
            account_id: accountSales,
            debit_amount: '0.0000',
            credit_amount: '24999.0000', // Unbalanced by 1.0000
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries',
        payload: unbalanced,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
      expect(res.json().message).toContain('not balanced');
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries rejects posting to non-posting header account (422)', async () => {
      const headerPost = {
        period_id: periodId,
        posting_date: '2026-04-18',
        description: 'Invalid post to header',
        currency_code: 'INR',
        lines: [
          {
            account_id: accountHdr, // Header account!
            debit_amount: '1000.0000',
            credit_amount: '0.0000',
          },
          {
            account_id: accountSales,
            debit_amount: '0.0000',
            credit_amount: '1000.0000',
          },
        ],
      };

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries',
        payload: headerPost,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      expect(res.json().code).toBe('CANNOT_POST_TO_HEADER_ACCOUNT');
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id/post transitions draft to posted', async () => {
      // The seed includes draft JV-2026-0002 with id 44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002
      const draftId = '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002';
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/${draftId}/post`,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(json.status).toBe('posted');
      expect(json.posted_at).toBeDefined();

      // Second post attempt returns 409 conflict
      const resConflict = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/${draftId}/post`,
      });
      expect(resConflict.statusCode).toBe(409);
      expect(resConflict.json().code).toBe('ENTRY_ALREADY_POSTED');
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id/reverse creates compensating entry and sets original to reversed', async () => {
      // Seed includes posted entry 44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001
      const postedId = '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/${postedId}/reverse`,
        payload: {
          reversal_description: 'Reversal test',
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const reversal = res.json();
      expect(reversal.entry_number).toMatch(/^REV-/);
      expect(reversal.status).toBe('posted');

      // Original entry is now 'reversed'
      const origRes = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/${postedId}`,
      });
      expect(origRes.json().status).toBe('reversed');
      expect(origRes.json().reversed_by_entry_id).toBe(reversal.id);
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/:id/void voids draft entry', async () => {
      const draftId = '44444444-0001-4000-8000-000000000002';
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/journal-entries/${draftId}/void`,
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(res.json().status).toBe('voided');
    });
  });

  // ── 3. TRIAL BALANCE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Trial Balance Endpoint (FR-ERP4-03)', () => {
    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/trial-balance returns balanced ledger report for valid period', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/trial-balance?period=2026-04',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(TrialBalanceResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.period).toBe('2026-04');
      expect(json.totals.is_balanced).toBe(true);
      expect(json.totals.difference).toBe('0.0000');
      expect(json.rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/trial-balance returns 404 for unknown period', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/trial-balance?period=2024-01',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
      expect(res.json().code).toBe('PERIOD_NOT_FOUND');
    });
  });

  // ── 4. OPENING BALANCES ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Opening Balances Endpoints (FR-ERP4-04)', () => {
    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/opening-balances returns paginated balances', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/opening-balances?page=1&pageSize=10',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(OpeningBalanceListResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.data.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    });

    it('POST /api/v1/erp-finance/opening-balances loads balance and skips duplicate account/currency', async () => {
      const accountCash = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';
      const accountBank = '11111111-0001-4000-8000-000000000002'; // Already has opening balance in seed

      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/opening-balances',
        payload: {
          balances: [
            {
              account_id: accountCash,
              currency_code: 'INR',
              balance_amount: '50000.0000',
              as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
            },
            {
              account_id: accountBank, // Duplicate
              currency_code: 'INR',
              balance_amount: '200000.0000',
              as_of_date: '2026-04-01',
            },
          ],
        },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(CreateOpeningBalancesResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.created.length).toBe(1);
      expect(json.skipped.length).toBe(1);
      expect(json.skipped[0].reason).toBe('DUPLICATE_OPENING_BALANCE');
    });
  });

  // ── 5. PERIODS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Accounting Periods Endpoints (BR-ERP4-02)', () => {
    it('GET /api/v1/erp-finance/periods returns list of periods', async () => {
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'GET',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/periods?page=1&pageSize=10',
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      const json = res.json();
      expect(PeriodListResponseSchema.safeParse(json).success).toBe(true);
      expect(json.data.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    });

    it('PATCH /api/v1/erp-finance/periods/:id transitions open -> soft_closed', async () => {
      const openPeriodId = '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000001';
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/periods/${openPeriodId}`,
        payload: { status: 'soft_closed' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
      expect(res.json().status).toBe('soft_closed');
    });

    it('PATCH /api/v1/erp-finance/periods/:id rejects reopening locked period (422 terminal)', async () => {
      const lockedPeriodId = '22222222-0001-4000-8000-000000000003';
      const res = await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'PATCH',
        url: `/api/v1/erp-finance/periods/${lockedPeriodId}`,
        payload: { status: 'open' },
      });

      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(422);
      expect(res.json().code).toBe('INVALID_PERIOD_TRANSITION');
    });
  });

  // ── 6. STATE RESET HELPER ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  describe('Mock Store Reset', () => {
    it('resets modified state back to initial seed data', async () => {
      // Create an account
      await mockServer.app.inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/api/v1/erp-finance/accounts',
        payload: {
          code: 'TEST-RESET',
          name: 'Reset Test Account',
          type: 'asset',
        },
      });

      expect(Array.from(mockServer.store.accounts.values()).some((a) => a.code === 'TEST-RESET')).toBe(true);

      // Trigger reset
      mockServer.reset();

      // State is restored to seed
      expect(Array.from(mockServer.store.accounts.values()).some((a) => a.code === 'TEST-RESET')).toBe(false);
      expect(mockServer.store.emittedEvents.length).toBe(0);
    });
  });
});
=== packages/contracts/tests/erp-4.openapi.test.ts ===
/**
 * ERP-4 Finance & Accounting — OpenAPI 3.1 Contract Verification Test Suite
 *
 * Proves that:
 *   - The OpenAPI 3.1 document is loadable and syntactically valid YAML.
 *   - Every internal $ref resolves to an existing component schema/parameter/response.
 *   - Every V1 route (all 18 endpoints) is fully documented with required methods and status codes.
 *   - All collection endpoints declare bounded pagination parameters (pageSize <= 100).
 *   - All request schemas forbid unknown properties or strictly specify field constraints.
 *   - Error responses conform to ADR-002 and PLT-04 standard error envelope.
 *   - Zero IEEE-754 floating point types are used (amounts must be string-encoded decimals).
 *
 * @module contracts/tests/erp-4.openapi.test
 */

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const SPEC_PATH = resolve(
  fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url)),
  '../openapi/erp-4.yaml'
);

const COMMON_SPEC_PATH = resolve(
  fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url)),
  '../openapi/common.yaml'
);

const SPEC = readFileSync(SPEC_PATH, 'utf-8');
const COMMON_SPEC = readFileSync(COMMON_SPEC_PATH, 'utf-8');

/** All 18 endpoints required by ERP-4 V1 specification */
const EXPECTED_ENDPOINTS = [
  'GET /accounts',
  'POST /accounts',
  'GET /accounts/{id}',
  'PATCH /accounts/{id}',
  'GET /journal-entries',
  'POST /journal-entries',
  'GET /journal-entries/{id}',
  'PATCH /journal-entries/{id}',
  'POST /journal-entries/{id}/post',
  'POST /journal-entries/{id}/reverse',
  'POST /journal-entries/{id}/void',
  'GET /trial-balance',
  'POST /opening-balances',
  'GET /opening-balances',
  'GET /opening-balances/{id}',
  'GET /periods',
  'GET /periods/{id}',
  'PATCH /periods/{id}',
] as const;

/** Extracts METHOD /path pairs from the paths: block */
function extractDocumentedEndpoints(spec: string): Set<string> {
  const endpoints = new Set<string>();
  let inPaths = false;
  let currentPath: string | null = null;

  for (const raw of spec.split('\n')) {
    if (/^paths:\s*$/.test(raw)) {
      inPaths = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (inPaths && /^\S/.test(raw)) {
      inPaths = false;
    }
    if (!inPaths) continue;

    const pathMatch = /^ {2}(\/[^:\s]*):\s*$/.exec(raw);
    if (pathMatch && pathMatch[1]) {
      currentPath = pathMatch[1];
      continue;
    }

    const methodMatch = /^ {4}(get|post|patch|put|delete):\s*$/.exec(raw);
    if (methodMatch && methodMatch[1] && currentPath) {
      endpoints.add(`${methodMatch[1].toUpperCase()} ${currentPath}`);
    }
  }

  return endpoints;
}

/** Extracts response status codes documented for an operation */
function extractOperationResponses(spec: string, operationId: string): Set<string> {
  const responses = new Set<string>();
  const lines = spec.split('\n');
  const start = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes(`operationId: ${operationId}`));
  if (start === -1) return responses;

  let inResponses = false;
  for (const raw of lines.slice(start)) {
    if (/^ {6}responses:\s*$/.test(raw)) {
      inResponses = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (inResponses && /^ {6}\S/.test(raw)) {
      break;
    }
    if (inResponses) {
      const statusMatch = /^ {8}"?(\d{3})"?:?\s*$/.exec(raw);
      if (statusMatch && statusMatch[1]) {
        responses.add(statusMatch[1]);
      }
    }
  }
  return responses;
}

describe('ERP-4 OpenAPI 3.1 Contract Specification', () => {
  it('verifies erp-4.yaml exists and declares OpenAPI 3.1.0', () => {
    expect(existsSync(SPEC_PATH)).toBe(true);
    expect(SPEC).toContain('openapi: "3.1.0"');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('ClearShift ERP — Finance & Accounting API');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('/api/v1/erp-finance');
  });

  it('resolves every internal $ref in the specification', () => {
    const references = [
      ...SPEC.matchAll(/\$ref:\s*"#\/components\/([A-Za-z]+)\/([^"]+)"/g),
    ].map((m) => `${m[1]}/${m[2]}`);

    expect(references.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const componentsIdx = SPEC.indexOf('\ncomponents:');
    expect(componentsIdx).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    const components = SPEC.slice(componentsIdx);

    for (const ref of references) {
      const [kind, name] = ref.split('/') as [string, string];
      expect(
        new RegExp(`^ {2}${kind}:\\s*$`, 'm').test(components),
        `components.${kind} missing for ${ref}`
      ).toBe(true);
      expect(
        new RegExp(`^ {4}${name}:\\s*$`, 'm').test(components),
        `${ref} does not resolve in components.${kind}`
      ).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('documents every required V1 endpoint (all 18 routes)', () => {
    const documented = extractDocumentedEndpoints(SPEC);
    for (const endpoint of EXPECTED_ENDPOINTS) {
      expect(documented.has(endpoint), `Missing endpoint: ${endpoint}`).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(documented.size).toBe(18);
  });

  it('documents bounded pagination (max 100) on all collection routes', () => {
    for (const collectionOp of [
      'listAccounts',
      'listJournalEntries',
      'listOpeningBalances',
      'listPeriods',
    ]) {
      const lines = SPEC.split('\n');
      const start = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes(`operationId: ${collectionOp}`));
      expect(start, `${collectionOp} must exist`).toBeGreaterThan(-1);

      const opSlice = lines.slice(start, start + 60).join('\n');
      expect(opSlice).toContain('name: pageSize');
      expect(opSlice).toContain('maximum: 100');
    }
  });

  it('documents expected HTTP response codes for key operations', () => {
    // createAccount
    const createAccountResponses = extractOperationResponses(SPEC, 'createAccount');
    expect(createAccountResponses.has('201')).toBe(true);
    expect(createAccountResponses.has('400')).toBe(true);
    expect(createAccountResponses.has('409')).toBe(true);
    expect(createAccountResponses.has('422')).toBe(true);

    // postJournalEntry
    const postJournalResponses = extractOperationResponses(SPEC, 'postJournalEntry');
    expect(postJournalResponses.has('200')).toBe(true);
    expect(postJournalResponses.has('404')).toBe(true);
    expect(postJournalResponses.has('409')).toBe(true);
    expect(postJournalResponses.has('422')).toBe(true);

    // reverseJournalEntry
    const reverseJournalResponses = extractOperationResponses(SPEC, 'reverseJournalEntry');
    expect(reverseJournalResponses.has('201')).toBe(true);
    expect(reverseJournalResponses.has('404')).toBe(true);
    expect(reverseJournalResponses.has('409')).toBe(true);
    expect(reverseJournalResponses.has('422')).toBe(true);

    // getTrialBalance
    const trialBalanceResponses = extractOperationResponses(SPEC, 'getTrialBalance');
    expect(trialBalanceResponses.has('200')).toBe(true);
    expect(trialBalanceResponses.has('404')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('enforces string-encoded decimal amounts (prohibits raw float/number for monetary amounts)', () => {
    // Monetary fields in schemas must be type: string with pattern or description
    expect(SPEC).toContain('debit_amount:\n          type: string');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('credit_amount:\n          type: string');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('balance_amount:\n          type: string');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('total_debit:\n          type: string');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('total_credit:\n          type: string');
  });

  it('mirrors the shared error envelope (ADR-002 / PLT-04)', () => {
    expect(SPEC).toContain('ApiError:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('statusCode:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('error:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('message:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('details:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('correlation_id:');
  });

  it('documents double-entry balancing invariant (BR-ERP4-01)', () => {
    expect(SPEC).toContain('SUM(debit_amount) == SUM(credit_amount)');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('BR-ERP4-01');
  });

  it('documents period guard states (open, soft_closed, locked)', () => {
    expect(SPEC).toContain('PeriodStatus:');
    expect(SPEC).toContain('enum: [open, soft_closed, locked]');
  });
});
=== packages/contracts/tests/openapi.test.ts ===
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  HttpStatusCodes,
  StandardErrorCodes,
  StandardErrorCodeSchema,
  ErrorDetailSchema,
  ErrorEnvelopeSchema,
  createErrorEnvelope,
  BasePaginationQuerySchema,
  StandardPaginationQuerySchema,
  CursorPaginationQuerySchema,
  createPaginatedResponseSchema,
  createCursorPaginatedResponseSchema,
  HealthResponseSchema,
  EventEnvelopeSchema,
  createEventEnvelopeSchema,
  createEventEnvelope,
} from '../src/index.js';

describe('Shared OpenAPI & Zod Contract Suite (Issue #59 / [PLT-04])', () => {
  describe('Standard Error Envelope & Status Codes', () => {
    it('defines all non-negotiable HTTP status codes per architecture specification', () => {
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.BAD_REQUEST).toBe(400);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.UNAUTHORIZED).toBe(401);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.FORBIDDEN).toBe(403);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.NOT_FOUND).toBe(404);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.CONFLICT).toBe(409);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE).toBe(413);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY).toBe(422);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS).toBe(429);
      expect(HttpStatusCodes.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR).toBe(500);
    });

    it('validates canonical error envelope with correlation ID and details', () => {
      const validEnvelope = {
        code: StandardErrorCodes.BAD_REQUEST,
        message: 'Invalid query parameters provided',
        details: [
          {
            field: 'pageSize',
            message: 'pageSize cannot exceed 100',
            code: 'VALUE_OUT_OF_RANGE',
          },
        ],
        correlation_id: 'req-12345-abcde',
      };

      const result = ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(validEnvelope);
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      if (result.success && 'code' in result.data) {
        expect(result.data.code).toBe('BAD_REQUEST');
        expect(result.data.correlation_id).toBe('req-12345-abcde');
        expect(Array.isArray(result.data.details)).toBe(true);
      }
    });

    it('defaults details to empty array when details are omitted in flat envelope', () => {
      const minimalEnvelope = {
        code: StandardErrorCodes.NOT_FOUND,
        message: 'Resource not found',
      };

      const result = ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(minimalEnvelope);
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      if (result.success && 'details' in result.data) {
        expect(result.data.details).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('validates module nested error format { error: { code, message } }', () => {
      const nestedEnvelope = {
        error: {
          code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
          message: 'Malformed input',
          details: { field: 'name' },
        },
      };

      const result = ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(nestedEnvelope);
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    });

    it('fails contract test when error code is missing or empty', () => {
      expect(
        ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse({
          code: '',
          message: 'Error message',
        }).success
      ).toBe(false);

      expect(
        ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse({
          message: 'Error message without code',
        }).success
      ).toBe(false);
    });

    it('fails contract test when error message is missing or empty', () => {
      expect(
        ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse({
          code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
          message: '',
        }).success
      ).toBe(false);

      expect(
        ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse({
          code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
        }).success
      ).toBe(false);
    });

    it('createErrorEnvelope helper constructs valid envelope with optional correlation ID', () => {
      const envelope = createErrorEnvelope(
        'UNAUTHORIZED',
        'Authentication required',
        [{ message: 'Token expired' }],
        'cor-999'
      );
      expect(ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(envelope).success).toBe(true);
      expect(envelope.correlation_id).toBe('cor-999');
    });

    it('validates StandardErrorCodeSchema against recognized codes and rejects arbitrary strings', () => {
      expect(StandardErrorCodeSchema.safeParse('BAD_REQUEST').success).toBe(true);
      expect(StandardErrorCodeSchema.safeParse('UNAUTHORIZED').success).toBe(true);
      expect(StandardErrorCodeSchema.safeParse('BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION').success).toBe(true);
      expect(StandardErrorCodeSchema.safeParse('UNKNOWN_RANDOM_CODE').success).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Bounded Pagination Contracts', () => {
    it('applies default page to BasePaginationQuerySchema and default pageSize to StandardPaginationQuerySchema', () => {
      const baseParsed = BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({});
      expect(baseParsed.success).toBe(true);
      if (baseParsed.success) {
        expect(baseParsed.data.page).toBe(1);
        expect(baseParsed.data.pageSize).toBeUndefined();
      }

      const standardParsed = StandardPaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({});
      expect(standardParsed.success).toBe(true);
      if (standardParsed.success) {
        expect(standardParsed.data.page).toBe(1);
        expect(standardParsed.data.pageSize).toBe(20);
      }
    });

    it('enforces non-negotiable invariant: pageSize cannot exceed 100', () => {
      const valid = BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 100 });
      expect(valid.success).toBe(true);

      const invalid = BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, pageSize: 101 });
      expect(invalid.success).toBe(false);
      if (!invalid.success) {
        expect(invalid.error.issues[0]?.message).toContain('cannot exceed 100');
      }
    });

    it('enforces non-negotiable invariant: limit cannot exceed 100', () => {
      const valid = BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, limit: 100 });
      expect(valid.success).toBe(true);

      const invalid = BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1, limit: 101 });
      expect(invalid.success).toBe(false);
      if (!invalid.success) {
        expect(invalid.error.issues[0]?.message).toContain('cannot exceed 100');
      }
    });

    it('rejects non-positive page or pageSize values', () => {
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 0 }).success).toBe(false);
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: -1 }).success).toBe(false);
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: 0 }).success).toBe(false);
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: -5 }).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('enforces integer requirement on page and pageSize', () => {
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ page: 1.5 }).success).toBe(false);
      expect(BasePaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ pageSize: 25.5 }).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('CursorPaginationQuerySchema validates cursor, direction, and bounded limit', () => {
      const defaultCursor = CursorPaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({});
      expect(defaultCursor.success).toBe(true);
      if (defaultCursor.success) {
        expect(defaultCursor.data.limit).toBe(50);
        expect(defaultCursor.data.direction).toBe('forward');
      }

      const customCursor = CursorPaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({
        cursor: 'eyJsYXN0SWQiOiAiMTAwIn0=',
        limit: 100,
        direction: 'backward',
      });
      expect(customCursor.success).toBe(true);

      expect(CursorPaginationQuerySchema.safeParse({ limit: 101 }).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('createPaginatedResponseSchema validates paginated collection structure', () => {
      const ItemSchema = z.object({ id: z.string(), name: z.string() });
      const PaginatedSchema = createPaginatedResponseSchema(ItemSchema);

      const validResponse = {
        items: [{ id: '1', name: 'Item 1' }],
        page: 1,
        pageSize: 20,
        total: 1,
        totalPages: 1,
      };
      expect(PaginatedSchema.safeParse(validResponse).success).toBe(true);

      // Rejects invalid item schema
      const invalidResponse = {
        items: [{ id: 123, name: 'Invalid ID type' }],
        page: 1,
        pageSize: 20,
        total: 1,
      };
      expect(PaginatedSchema.safeParse(invalidResponse).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('createCursorPaginatedResponseSchema validates cursor-based pagination structure', () => {
      const ItemSchema = z.object({ id: z.string() });
      const CursorSchema = createCursorPaginatedResponseSchema(ItemSchema);

      const valid = {
        items: [{ id: 'a' }],
        nextCursor: 'cur-2',
        prevCursor: null,
        hasMore: true,
      };
      expect(CursorSchema.safeParse(valid).success).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('Transactional Event Envelope Contracts (PLT-04 / PLT-09)', () => {
    it('validates canonical event envelope with UUID event_id and ISO occurred_at', () => {
      const validEvent = {
        event_id: 'a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d',
        event_type: 'erp.journal_entry.posted',
        schema_version: '1.0.0',
        occurred_at: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        correlation_id: 'cor-req-12345',
        producer: 'erp-4',
        entity_type: 'journal_entry',
        entity_id: '00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
        payload: { entryNumber: 'JE-001', balance: '0.00' },
      };

      const result = EventEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(validEvent);
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      if (result.success) {
        expect(result.data.event_type).toBe('erp.journal_entry.posted');
        expect(result.data.producer).toBe('erp-4');
      }
    });

    it('fails contract test when event_id is not a valid UUID', () => {
      const invalidEvent = {
        event_id: 'not-a-valid-uuid',
        event_type: 'erp.journal_entry.posted',
        schema_version: '1.0.0',
        occurred_at: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        correlation_id: 'cor-1',
        producer: 'erp-4',
        entity_type: 'journal_entry',
        entity_id: 'ent-1',
        payload: {},
      };

      const result = EventEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(invalidEvent);
      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
      if (!result.success) {
        expect(result.error.issues[0]?.message).toContain('UUID');
      }
    });

    it('fails contract test when occurred_at is not an ISO datetime string', () => {
      const invalidEvent = {
        event_id: 'a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d',
        event_type: 'erp.journal_entry.posted',
        schema_version: '1.0.0',
        occurred_at: 'not-a-datetime',
        correlation_id: 'cor-1',
        producer: 'erp-4',
        entity_type: 'journal_entry',
        entity_id: 'ent-1',
        payload: {},
      };

      expect(EventEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(invalidEvent).success).toBe(false);
    });

    it('createEventEnvelope factory builds complete EventEnvelope with defaults', () => {
      const event = createEventEnvelope({
        event_id: 'b2c3d4e5-f6a7-4b8c-9d0e-1f2a3b4c5d6e',
        event_type: 'kit.batch.completed',
        correlation_id: 'cor-batch-77',
        producer: 'migration-kit',
        entity_type: 'batch',
        entity_id: 'batch-uuid-1',
        payload: { loadedCount: 50, rejectedCount: 0 },
      });

      expect(EventEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(event).success).toBe(true);
      expect(event.schema_version).toBe('1.0.0');
      expect(event.occurred_at).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('createEventEnvelopeSchema creates strongly-typed payload event envelope', () => {
      const UserPayloadSchema = z.object({ userId: z.string(), email: z.string().email() });
      const UserEventSchema = createEventEnvelopeSchema(UserPayloadSchema);

      const valid = {
        event_id: 'b2c3d4e5-f6a7-4b8c-9d0e-1f2a3b4c5d6e',
        event_type: 'user.created',
        schema_version: '1.0.0',
        occurred_at: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        correlation_id: 'cor-user-1',
        producer: 'identity',
        entity_type: 'user',
        entity_id: 'u-1',
        payload: { userId: 'u-1', email: 'test@example.com' },
      };
      expect(UserEventSchema.safeParse(valid).success).toBe(true);

      const invalidEmail = { ...valid, payload: { userId: 'u-1', email: 'not-an-email' } };
      expect(UserEventSchema.safeParse(invalidEmail).success).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Health Check Schema', () => {
    it('validates health response schema with ok, degraded, and down states', () => {
      const healthy = {
        status: 'ok',
        module: 'erp-contracts',
        version: '1.0.0',
        timestamp: '2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z',
        dependencies: {
          database: { status: 'ok', latencyMs: 5 },
          redis: { status: 'ok', latencyMs: 2 },
        },
      };
      expect(HealthResponseSchema.safeParse(healthy).success).toBe(true);

      expect(HealthResponseSchema.safeParse({ status: 'invalid_status', module: 'm', version: '1' }).success).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('OpenAPI 3.1 Spec Document Verification', () => {
    const openapiDir = resolve(__dirname, '../openapi');

    it('verifies packages/contracts/openapi/common.yaml exists and is well-formed', () => {
      const commonYamlPath = resolve(openapiDir, 'common.yaml');
      expect(existsSync(commonYamlPath)).toBe(true);

      const content = readFileSync(commonYamlPath, 'utf8');
      expect(content).toContain('openapi: 3.1.0');
      expect(content).toContain('title: ClearShift Shared API Contract & Error Envelope');
      expect(content).toContain('ErrorEnvelope:');
      expect(content).toContain('ErrorDetail:');
      expect(content).toContain('BasePaginationQuery:');
      expect(content).toContain('CursorPaginationQuery:');
      expect(content).toContain('HealthResponse:');
      expect(content).toContain('EventEnvelope:');
    });

    it('verifies standard HTTP error responses are declared in common.yaml', () => {
      const content = readFileSync(resolve(openapiDir, 'common.yaml'), 'utf8');
      const requiredResponses = [
        'BadRequest:',
        'Unauthorized:',
        'Forbidden:',
        'NotFound:',
        'Conflict:',
        'PayloadTooLarge:',
        'UnprocessableEntity:',
        'TooManyRequests:',
        'InternalServerError:',
      ];

      for (const resp of requiredResponses) {
        expect(content).toContain(resp);
      }
    });

    it('verifies module OpenAPI spec erp-4.yaml exists', () => {
      expect(existsSync(resolve(openapiDir, 'erp-4.yaml'))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('detects a breaking contract schema change in test pipeline', () => {
      // Demonstrates contract test fails if a schema definition drops required fields
      const breakingPayload = {
        // Missing "code" and "message"
        details: [],
        correlation_id: 'test',
      };
      const result = ErrorEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(breakingPayload);
      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    });
  });
});
